- **1914.08** `iw18-infancy.ceiling-shadows::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-coat-is-just-a-coat` tags: infancy, night, wonder, gaslight, fear, family, vienna, urban
  Josef Bauer把Klara抱起来，走到衣钩下面，让孩子伸手去抓那件旧大衣。粗呢的袖口擦过掌心，Klara先愣住，随后皱着鼻子哭了一声。Josef Bauer笑着把大衣掀开，说“看，只有衣服”，灯影也跟着晃了一下。
  _effects: mood 0.03; flag +learned_the_coat_rack_

- **1915.10** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  楼梯间的灯罩空着，第三级踏板比别处高。Klara一脚踩空，滚到平台，右小臂磕在铁扶手的弯头上。Josef Bauer听见哭声跑出来，把Klara抱回屋，用冷水毛巾敷那块青紫，再用旧布条缠了两圈。夜里Klara哭醒两次。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Alois Winter在绳子那头喊。Klara踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Alois Winter数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Klara把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1918.05** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-win` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  巷子窄，两个孩子并排推着铁环，铁环撞在一起叮当作响。Klara的钩子先绕过最后一块界石。Mitzi Lechner不服，说再比一次，手却已经伸进口袋，摸出三颗彩色弹珠。Klara把弹珠举到眼前看，Mitzi Lechner在旁边踢墙，踢得鞋尖发白。
  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood 0.05; skill_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-carried-afternoon` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  Rosa Bauer哭得打嗝，手指一直朝井台方向指。Klara蹲下把Rosa Bauer驮上背，沿着墙根走，数砖缝给Rosa Bauer听：一、二、三……数到第四圈，背上的呼吸才匀了。天黑时Klara的胳膊麻得抬不过肩，Josef Bauer回来接过孩子，Klara扶着门框甩了好一会儿手。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (背弟妹走了一下午，胳膊酸痛); skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000285:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 1 -> 2; cash -1 (wasted material); mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1927.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/forewoman-catches` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  客户刚把床单接过去，Gustav Berg就从楼梯口上来，盯着Klara看了两秒，说“你不是这家的”。Gustav Berg当场把工钱收回，说明天要去找Rosa Bauer的雇主。Klara站在门口，手里还攥着空筐绳，回家一路上没吃东西，Rosa Bauer问起来，只摇了摇头。
  _effects: mood -0.07; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000285:child; health -0.020000000000000018 (白跑一趟，路上没吃东西)_

- **1927.04** `kernel-edu.friendship::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/edu-friend-library` tags: adolescence, school, friendship, books, family, reform, medical, schooling
  Klara在图书馆门口认识Anton，两人合用一张借书证，轮流把书带回家。Anton读得快，常在书页边留铅笔字；Klara读到那些字，像在跟人说话。冬天过去，书证上多了两个名字。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; skill; flag +study_friend; mood 0.03999999999999998_

- **1927.05** `yf18-body.danube-cabin::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/stay-behind-the-curtain` tags: body, desire, river, swimming, shame, first_love, reform, medical
  Klara把浴巾裹到下巴，说自己怕水。Hedwig在门外笑，说去年还见Klara从木塔上跳下去；Klara没有解释，只隔着板缝看Emil把水撩到肩上。回家时，Klara的泳衣还是干的，衣角被手攥得起了皱。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:114; flag +yf18_stayed_dry; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1927.05** `r3-teen.domestic-servant-14::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/escapes` tags: work, domestic_service, servant, wages, escape, reform, medical, schooling
  Klara在天亮前从后门溜走，只带了一个包袱和一双鞋。她沿着电车轨道走到工厂区，门房问她会不会认字，她说会写自己的名字。Alois Berg后来托人带来一句话：太太把你的工钱扣了。
  _effects: flag +left_domestic_service_for_factory; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; move place-wien-printshop-winter; employment Arbeiterin @ org-winter-print; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

- **1927.11** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-humiliation` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002766_

  Klara连续上了十二天夜班，最后一天在机器旁睡着，被工头踢醒。月底多拿了两克朗，咳嗽却一直没好。
  _effects: cash 2 (overtime pay); health -0.050000000000000044 (night shifts); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1928.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Klara在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Emil。这一次对方先点了头，Klara准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Klara才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 40 -> 45; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 0 -> 8; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: state_transition apprenticeship-contract -> failed-apprentice; flag +failed_apprenticeship; mood -0.08000000000000002; role_

- **1928.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.learn-the-task/small-ad-set` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:work
  Maria Weiss先让Klara排价目表，自己站在背后看。Klara把字距一行行校齐，锁版时手心全是汗。Maria Weiss没夸，只把一张小广告挪到案头，说“你来”。Klara排完最后一行，作坊里两个伙计围过来看，Maria Weiss把版举到窗光下，点了点头。
  _effects: skill; reputation 0.04000000000000001; mood 0.04999999999999999; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

- **1928.07** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  外套腋下裂了一道口子，里布翻出来。Klara把袖口套在桌角上撑开，穿针时试了三次才引上线。针脚一针长一针短，像蜈蚣。Josef Bauer端着汤进来，站在桌边看了一会儿，把碗往Klara那边推了推，自己只留了半碗。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

  报名桌后面的Rosa Lechner把考试费收据和成绩单一起递出来，纸上有复写的紫色字迹。预科班在同一个院子里上课，走廊尽头能看见制图室的窗子。Gustav Berg说，先去把书看了再想。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; knowledge “技术学院差一分可先读一年预科再考”; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg_

  Klara在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Emil。这一次对方先点了头，Klara准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Klara才发现，自己把书拿倒了。

  Gustav Berg看了看Klara抄的字母表，说大小不一，让他回去用直尺画格子重抄一遍，下个月再来。少年把纸折好，在咖啡馆门口站了一会儿，走回印刷厂时绕到排字房窗外看了很久。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; skill; flag +mentor_pending_retry; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg_

- **1929.06** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-rejected` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  “太小，手也薄。”Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说完，把Klara推过的木板翻过来，指着上面一道啃痕。Josef Bauer把布包往肩上提了提，说打扰了。Klara在门口多站了一会儿，直到里面的刨子声把话头盖过去，才跟着Josef Bauer下楼。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; reputation -0.05; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

  考试周里，Klara把课本摊在桌上，眼睛却一直看着窗外那条电车线。笔记的页边多了几个没有写完的名字，Klara用橡皮擦掉，纸面起了毛。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 45 -> 42; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 2_

  Elisabeth Bauer走后，靠墙那张床空了出来，Klara把自己的铺盖搬过去，也接过那本记着欠账的旧账本：面包店三克朗、煤铺一克朗八十赫勒。晚上Klara坐在床沿一笔笔抄清，第一次知道家里的钱是怎么缺的。
  _effects: relation kin with person-elisabeth-bauer; skill; mood -0.04999999999999999; cash -0.5 (替兄姐补齐欠煤铺的零头)_

  两个星期不来往，门口碰见时Klara和Ferdinand Berg 2都先看别处。这次Klara先开口，把账本里的一页撕下来摊在柜台上，说数字是自己写错的；Ferdinand Berg 2看了半天，说下回别在酒馆里算账。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一小束紫罗兰和两只 Semmel); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; mood 0.08000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

- **1930.07** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/nod-and-pots` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  汤喝到一半，Josef Bauer放下勺子点了头，但说得很清楚：先攒出一套锅碗、一只挂钟，再把话往下说。Rosa Huber在桌下用膝盖碰了碰Klara，Josef Bauer把最后一块煮牛肉推过来，饭桌上的空气总算松了；这一顿让对方家里管了饭，自家灶上省下两克朗的吃食，也吃出一个带条件的准信。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; food_

- **1930.08** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.sign` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  老工人凑近看那张表，说这纸比工资袋还薄。Klara把拇指蘸进印泥，按下去时听见工头说了句“下一个”。学徒在旁边数少掉的铜板，数到四十赫勒时不出声了。
  _effects: cash -1 (星期六下午减少的计件工钱); mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +accepted_hours_reform_

  Klara没有倒。第九回合还剩四十秒，Klara把Mitzi逼到绳圈角，连续三拳打在他护齿上。Grete Stein在台下砸了毛巾，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne站起来又坐下。终场铃响时，裁判举起Klara的手，观众却有一半在骂。
  _effects: cash 4 (第九回合的软拳); health -0.06000000000000005 (第九回合的软拳); reputation 0.08; flag +refused_to_take_dive; role; relation rival with person-grete-stein; relation rival with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912_

- **1931.01** `p9-work-career.night-course/p9-work-career.night-course.dropout` tags: work, career, craft, clerk, education, task, night_school, bookkeeping
  Klara头两次课都到了，第三次车间赶一批订单，工头留他加班到九点。他赶到夜校时黑板已经擦干净。此后两个月，加班总落在星期三。六克朗学费不退，课本只翻到第十四页，书脊还是硬的。他把书塞进床底下的木箱。
  _effects: cash -6 (学费不退，等于白付); mood -0.03999999999999998; skill_

- **1931.02** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-handcart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, injury
  第一趟过街口时车上的煤箱差点翻下来，Emil用膝盖顶住，Klara把绳子又收了一道。第三趟回来，两人靠在门框上喘气，鞋底都是灰，省下的钱还揣在围裙里。
  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销); health -0.050000000000000044 (三趟推车扛家具累得肩臂酸痛); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912_

  Klara把厨房角落隔出一块，租给一个在附近上工的学徒。每月多四克朗，但煤、水和厕所都要排队；夜里有人咳嗽，Klara睡不踏实，第二天眼下发青，孩子也在床上翻来覆去，四克朗换来一屋子醒着的人。
  _effects: cash 4 (lodger rent); health -0.040000000000000036 (overcrowded housing); condition_

- **1931.12** `vienna-printshop-daily.union-pamphlet/pamphlet-print-secretly` tags: work, factory, labour, social

  _effects: flag +printed_union_pamphlet; relation colleague with person-alois-lechner; mood -0.02999999999999997_

  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne在事故后第三天通知Klara不用再来了，说岗位不能一直空着。Klara拿着医院证明去工会，诊断书被工头压在抽屉里；治伤花去六克朗，Klara的手还没好，工作先没了，工牌交回去时手指还缠着纱布。
  _effects: unemployment (dismissed after accident); health -0.12 (injury and lost job); cash -6 (medical treatment)_

  仪式做完，Gustav Berg把铜钥匙放进Klara手里，钥匙却烫得像刚从炉子里夹出来。Klara松手时掌心已经起泡；第二天，聘书上的名字褪成一片空白，只有Klara的手还疼。Gustav Berg说这算成了，也算没成。

  市场掌柜把账本翻到空白页，铅笔写上“月底清”，Klara 把拇指按进印泥，红印盖得有点歪。Emil Weiss 扛着半袋面粉往回走，袋底漏出的粉在石板路上留下白印。

  Klara把传单一张张塞进炉子，火苗窜到烟囱口。Rosa Berg 2在门外问有没有闻到纸味，Klara说是在烧旧账本。第二天，街口的墙上贴出新的告示，纸色和昨晚烧掉的一模一样。
  _effects: knowledge “新告示的纸和昨夜传单是同一种纸。”; flag +burned_revolution_leaflets; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77_

  遗嘱里还附了一笔丧葬费，条件是要有“家属”到场。死者没有亲人，Klara只好穿黑衣站在墓旁，听牧师念一个从未听过的名字。雨把花圈上的纸花打湿，泥水漫过鞋面；葬礼结束后，Gustav Berg把九克朗交给他，说账已结清。Klara回家脱鞋，发现袜子上沾着别人的土。
  _effects: cash 7 (以家属身份到场后的遗产余款); reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg; condition; flag +buried_a_stranger_for_the_estate; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1933.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Klara在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Klara把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.050000000000000044 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004907_

  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Klara站在一只倒扣的木箱上讲了十分钟。开始时只有十几个人，讲到房租和工时，巷口已经站满了。Gustav Kral把Klara的名字记进名单，说下次还来。
  _effects: reputation 0.08000000000000002; historical development:seed-vienna-social-democracy-1912 as organizer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

  Anna Hofer摸了半天肚子，忽然让Klara去街口叫医生，说胎位不对。Klara披着外套跑过两条街，医生来了以后只说了句“送医院”，又收了一次出诊费。天亮前，马车才把Rosa Bauer拉走。
  _effects: cash -5 (夜里敲接生婆的门); health -0.030000000000000027 (夜里敲接生婆的门); flag +complicated_birth_sent_to_hospital; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; knowledge “the midwife suspected a malpresentation before dawn”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27_

- **1933.05** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.door-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Klara把药包和半块肥皂交给开门的孩子，嘱咐用水冲开、一天两次，就下了楼。楼梯间里孩子一直站在门口看着，手里攥着纸包，没关门。Klara走到二层又折回去，帮他把门带上，说自己家里也等着火。
  _effects: health 0.010000000000000009 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

  Gustav Berg在“身份”一栏写了“房客”，说这个类别每三个月要重新登记。Klara问为什么不能写“住户”，Gustav Berg说住户要有租约和门牌。Helene Winter在旁边小声提醒，三个月后来盖章要带一克朗。
  _effects: cash -1 (门牌号与登记簿); condition; flag +registered_as_lodger; relation acquaintance with person-helene-winter; knowledge “lodger status must be renewed every three months”_

  会议开到一半，原来的主持人被人叫走。Klara被推到那把椅子上，没有人反对。散会时，Klara手里多了一串钥匙和一份需要签字的名单。
  _effects: reputation 0.09999999999999998; position 委员会主席 @org-sdap_

- **1934.01** `major-work-first-employment/hired-regular` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  清晨六点，印刷厂门口已经站了十几个人。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头让Klara把手摊开，看了看指节，又问能不能读一页纸。Klara读完，工头在名册上写了名字：明天六点，带工票和一双干净的手。第一周洗墨辊，月底工资三十克朗，柜上先预支四克朗。
  _effects: employment Druckerhelfer @ org-winter-print; role; cash 4 (first wage advance); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; flag +first_regular_job; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82_

  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation 0.08000000000000002; position 名誉主席 @org-sdap; flag +puppet_office_

- **1934.02** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara把Ferdinand Winter和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Klara把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-ferdinand-winter; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +tenant_collective_

- **1934.06** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.sick_child_doorstep/borrows_coal_risk` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  Klara把自家煤箱里仅剩的四块煤分了两块给Emil，自己家那一夜没生火，裹着大衣睡的。孩子烧退了，Emil第二天还煤时多塞了一把粗盐。Klara当天下午就开始流清鼻涕，第三天咳得直不起腰。
  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (受凉咳嗽); food_

- **1935.01** `r14-edu.night-class::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/learned-to-read` tags: education, adult_learning, literacy, night_school, vienna, urban, crisis, war

  _effects: cash -1 (夜校的第二排长凳); flag +adult_literacy; skill_

  _effects: unemployment (laid off in the depression); cash -5 (lost wages); condition_

- **1935.01** `r15-wish.found-faction::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/founded` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, office, status, vienna, urban

- **1935.09** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.sign` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life

  _effects: cash -1 (星期六下午减少的计件工钱); mood 0.050000000000000044_

  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person-gustav-berg; reputation 0.040000000000000036_

  晚饭后的第三天，Klara去邮局取钱，发现存折上少了三十克朗。Alois Bauer在母亲的旧照片前承认拿了钱，说这些年照顾母亲的情分总该折成数目。Rosa Huber去叫了警察；Klara站在两个最熟悉的人中间，听见楼下有人把留声机开得很响。

  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销); health -0.050000000000000044 (三趟推车扛家具累得肩臂酸痛); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68_

- **1936.09** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Klara说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Emil Weiss没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Klara听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; flag +funeral_society_left_

  _effects: state_add stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:362; cash 1 (替角的排练补贴); mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +understudy_king; flag +stage_sovereign; reputation 0.040000000000000036_

- **1939.03** `r3-war.war-loan::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/bought-war-bonds` tags: war, war_loan, bonds, bank, inflation, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi Winter 2把第六期爱国债券的表格推到Klara面前，说“买一份也是爱国”。Klara把刚领的工钱数了一遍，留下房租，其余全买了债券；Anna Hofer在旁边小声说，上一期的利息还没兑。Grete Stein在门口挂了一条横幅，红布被风吹得啪啪响。
  _effects: cash -10 (爱国债券的第六期); flag +bought_war_bonds; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; knowledge “the previous bond interest is still unpaid”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73_

- **1939.04** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.with-priest` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Klara觉得一个人去不合适，绕到教堂请了修女一同上楼。修女看过病人，留下药包和一小瓶圣水，说会从教区账上出钱请医生来看一趟。Mitzi Hofer在楼下等着，说这样妥当，病人家里没男人，多个见证人心里安稳。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +parish_sick_visitor_

- **1939.12** `absurd15-wish.crown-for-a-day::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-delegation-never-comes` tags: wish, power, crown, office, irony, festival, vienna, urban
  Klara等了一整天，代表团没有来。傍晚，Klara在窗户里看见游行队伍从街口经过，新的“国王”骑着借来的白马，朝人群挥手。Klara把订做的戏服退掉，押金没有拿回来。
  _effects: cash -2 (订做戏服的押金); mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +wish_crown_failed_

- **1940.01** `curse15-bargain.borrowed-name::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/a-key-and-a-title` tags: wish, power, curse, ritual, bargain, office, irony, vienna
  办事处后门的Gustav Berg把一份旧名册翻到空页，让Klara在“会长”后面签下名字。三天后，Klara真的收到一枚铜钥匙和一张聘书：夜校同学会会长，职责是保管储物间。同学会只有三个成员，储物间的锁已经坏了。Gustav Berg没有再出现。
  _effects: organization_found 夜校同学会 (other); position 夜校同学会会长 @org:curse15-borrowed:evt:world-vienna-1912:008892; state_add curse-bargain state:world-vienna-1912:461; reputation 0.030000000000000027; cash -1 (聘书与印花的费用)_

- **1940.01** `agency:organize_meeting` tags: agency, agency:organize_meeting
  Klara原定在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang开会，但来的人不够，或者有人临时退缩；最后只留下几张没发完的纸条。
  _effects: reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1940.01** `initiative:found_process:org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:006313`
  权力俱乐部在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这不是预先写好的历史，而是成员自己累积出来的运动。
  _effects: historical process 权力俱乐部的持续运动; historical development:initiative-org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:006313-1940 as organizer; historical development:initiative-org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:006313-1940 as organizer; knowledge “权力俱乐部的持续运动不是一次会议，而是我们这些年积累出来的持续组织。”; reputation 0.040000000000000036_

- **1940.01** `historical_phase`
  历史进程《权力俱乐部在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这不是预先》从 emerging 进入 mobilizing。

- **1940.03** `r3-mig.displaced-persons-camp::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/dp-card-issued` tags: migration, refugees, camp, relief, papers, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi Winter 2在硬纸卡上写下Klara的姓名、出生地和“无国籍”，又盖了一个蓝印。卡上可以领六块饼干和一罐炼乳。Rosa Winter 2教Klara把卡缝进内衣，说“丢了卡，你就不是人了”。
  _effects: food; flag +dp_card_issued; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; knowledge “the DP card is the only claim to rations”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85_

- **1940.06** `kernel-health.childbirth-complication::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/birth-postpartum-haemorrhage` tags: health, childbirth, motherhood, hospital, death, vienna, urban, crisis
  产婆换到第三条毛巾时，血还没有停。Klara被抬进医院，医生用冷水拍她的脸，又把手伸进去取胎盘；孩子生下来没有哭声，护士用布包好放在床脚，谁也没敢揭开。护士把床脚的布包抱走，没人告诉她是男是女。
  _effects: health -0.21999999999999997 (postpartum haemorrhage and manual removal of the placenta); condition; role; cash -20 (midwife, hospital transport and burial cloth); move place-wien-allgemeines-krankenhaus; knowledge “产婆先按肚子、再用手取胎盘；血止不住时只能送医院，孩子没有哭声。”_

- **1940.09** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-full` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  Klara把床上的旧褥子重新铺平，Helene Kral 2放下自己的铺盖卷，从怀里掏出一把钥匙看了看。厨房里多了一口人的碗，灶边的煤省着烧也够。Otto Winter在门口看了一眼，没多话。
  _effects: cash 4 (出租空床收进的月租); household; relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter_

- **1941.01** `r15-wish.power-intrigue::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/made-a-deal` tags: politics, power, wish, intrigue, office, vienna, urban, crisis
  Klara和Gustav Berg在咖啡馆后间谈了一个钟头。没有人知道他们交换了什么，但第二天，两个人都投了对方的票。
  _effects: reputation 0.06000000000000005; relation colleague with person-gustav-berg; knowledge “我知道{rival}在关键委员会里真正想要什么，也知道这笔交易的价格。”_

- **1941.01** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara没有立刻答话，先去敲Emil的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Emil提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Klara把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1941.01** `state_expire:curse-bargain` tags: curse-bargain, curse, ritual, wish, bargain, debt, secret, supernatural
  Klara的「诅咒的定金」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove curse-bargain state:world-vienna-1912:461_

- **1941.01** `r13-fam.plan-wedding::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::wedding-war/wedding-refused-by-unit` tags: task, family, marriage, wedding, household, debt, vienna, urban
  Rosa Huber的部队不准假，婚礼只能取消。Klara把做好的礼服叠好，送到裁缝那里改成一件能日常穿的外套。Rosa Huber托人带回来一张纸条，说等打完这一仗；Klara把纸条夹进祈祷书，没有回信。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; condition; flag +wartime_wedding_cancelled; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; knowledge “the unit refused leave for the wedding”_

- **1941.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money, injury
  药房伙计从褐色瓶里倒出药水，叮嘱别咽。Emil在柜台边说，这玩意儿治标不治本。Klara把棉球塞进嘴里，腮帮子鼓出一块，走回巷子时拿手帕捂着脸，怕风吹。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水); health -0.020000000000000018 (牙病拖延未治); condition_

- **1941.09** `office16-hire.protege::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hired-competent` tags: office, hire, patronage, bureaucracy, work, power, vienna, urban
  Klara把Emil的名字写在编制表的空行上，又在旁边注了“试用三个月”。Gustav Kral提醒说预算只够半个位置，Klara还是把名字留下了。第二天，Emil提前半小时到，把走廊里的旧档案按年份重新排了一遍。
  _effects: position 助理文书 @zoo:person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:004043; reputation 0.030000000000000027; flag +hired_protege_

- **1942.01** `r15-history.street-assembly::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/spoke` tags: politics, assembly, organization, history, risk, vienna, urban, crisis
  Klara站在一只倒扣的木箱上讲了十分钟。开始时只有十几个人，讲到房租和工时，巷口已经站满了。Grete Moser 2把Klara的名字记进名单，说下次还来。
  _effects: reputation 0.07999999999999996; historical development:initiative-zoo:person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:004043-1931 as organizer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

- **1942.01** `task-vienna-family-care.care-sick-parent/night-watch` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  Klara整夜没合眼，按时把最后两包药粉喂给Josef Bauer，水盆换到第五次。天快亮时咳嗽声稀了，老人的呼吸慢慢平下来。Klara在椅子上打了个盹，醒来时炉子灭了，赶到工坊迟了一刻钟，被扣了半天工钱。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (整夜未睡，受凉); relation kin with person-josef-bauer; cash -1.2 (迟到被扣的半天工钱)_

- **1942.02** `hs18-queue.refugee::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/give-a-false-address` tags: refugee, queue, station, papers, official, history, vienna, urban
  Klara把隔壁空屋的门牌号写在表格上。Gustav Kral看了一眼，居然没有追问。Rosa Stein 2千恩万谢，提着箱子往那条街走；Klara没有提醒那屋已经塌了半边，只把围巾拉高，转身去赶电车。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65; knowledge “空屋的门牌号也能通过登记。”; flag +gave_false_refugee_address; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65_

  市立太平间缺一个夜班杂役，Gustav Kral只问Klara怕不怕冷。地下室的抽屉一排十二个，有的拉开了就不再推回去；Klara的工作是登记遗物、给无名尸挂上编号，再在天亮前把地拖干净。
  _effects: organization_found 市立太平间 (state); position 太平间杂役 @morgue:person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:009912; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:520; cash 2 (municipal night wages); condition; flag +mortuary_worker; knowledge “太平间地下室的抽屉一排十二个，无名尸也要有编号。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-120; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

  Klara坐在那张有权力的桌子后面，签下自己的名字。愿望没有因此变近，但桌上的纸张、印章和等待回话的人，都变得具体了。
  _effects: reputation 0.05999999999999994_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009912_

  婚礼在借来的房间里举行，证人是洗衣房的Rosa Stein 2。戒指是从当铺买的，誓词写在洗衣单背面；Rosa Stein 2念到“无论贫穷还是疾病”时，楼下有人敲门，三个人同时屏住呼吸。门开了，是来收煤气费的。

  亲戚的婚礼上，Klara被安排和伴侣分开坐。司仪问有没有人反对，Klara低头喝汤；伴侣在另一桌替别人倒酒，手很稳，酒没有洒出来。散席时，两个人在衣帽间碰见，只说了一句“外面下雨”。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 29 -> 39; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.grief 0 -> 4_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010641_

- **1943.01** `agency:organize_meeting` tags: agency, agency:organize_meeting
  Klara原定在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang开会，但来的人不够，或者有人临时退缩；最后只留下几张没发完的纸条。
  _effects: reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1943.03** `r2-leisure.zoo-escape::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::zoo-escape-war/recaptured-the-escaped-monkey` tags: leisure, zoo, animal, panic, absurdity, vienna, urban, crisis
  空袭之后，猴山的墙塌了一角，猴子跑到街上，蹲在面包店门口。Klara和Mitzi Weiss拿着网兜追了三条街，最后用一只苹果把它引回来。Grete Moser 2说猴子算逃兵，Mitzi Weiss说猴子没有国籍。Klara把苹果核扔进笼子，猴子接住了。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (猴山空了); reputation 0.020000000000000018; condition; flag +recaptured_escaped_monkey; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; knowledge “the escaped monkey trusts apples more than nets”_

- **1943.10** `r15-wish.faction-price::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/split` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, intrigue, office, vienna, urban
  俱乐部在一次表决中分裂。Hedwig带走了将近一半的人，留下的那间办公室突然显得很大。Klara把两把没人坐的椅子搬到墙边，没有扔掉。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; reputation -0.050000000000000044_

- **1943.11** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Otto Hofer这周主动擦了黑板。Klara把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 23; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010495_

- **1944.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Maria Bauer这周主动擦了黑板。Klara把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。

- **1944.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Klara在Allgemeines Krankenhaus靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Ferdinand Huber的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-122_

- **1944.01** `office16-appointment.patronage-ledger::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/appointed-ally` tags: office, appointment, patronage, organization, politics, power, vienna, urban
  Klara把Mitzi Weiss的名字写在“联络员”后面，月薪一克朗，职责是每周来两次、把外面的消息带进来。Grete Moser 2说这个岗位没有编制，Klara说先按临时用工算；第一个月，Mitzi Weiss带来的消息比文件还多。
  _effects: position 联络员 @morgue:person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:009912; organization_funds; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; flag +appointed_ally; knowledge “联络员不在正式编制里，工资走临时用工。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.01** `ml18-inheritance.uncles-press::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/sell-the-press-for-scrap` tags: inheritance, family, business, debt, legal, money, vienna, urban
  Klara把压机拆开卖给收废铁的人，得了八克朗；还掉六克朗欠账，剩下两克朗。订阅名单被Klara带回家，夹在电话簿里，偶尔拿出来看看那些名字。印刷所的门从里面锁上，窗户上贴了一张招租纸，风把纸角吹得一直响。
  _effects: cash 2 (scrap value of the press after paying its debts); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89; flag +sold_the_press; knowledge “那台压机卖作废铁只值八克朗，订阅名单却可能值更多。”; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1944.01** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.remake-coat/remake-coat.left-on-chair` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, mending, clothing, household
  Klara只把外套的里衬拆了一半，就搁在椅背上。冬天天短，她每天回来都看见那件敞着口子的外套，想着明天接着弄，明天又总是有别的事。到开春外套还搭在那儿，呢面上落了一层灰。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +unfinished_coat_

- **1944.01** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/the-papers-hear-about-it` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  报纸的社会版把Klara家的门牌号和那笔钱的数目印在一起，标题是“一笔不该有的好运”。第二天门口多了两个陌生人，一个说是远亲，一个说代表一家孤儿院。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.public true -> true; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 2 -> 4; reputation -0.020000000000000018; flag +windfall_went_public_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010987_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Klara被叫到学校，老师说Helene Bauer这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Klara没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 14 -> 9; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 75 -> 80; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.01** `state_reaction:ld18-impossible-attachment/a-question-at-the-office` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  办公室有人问Klara，为什么总有一个女人在楼下等她。Klara说那是表妹；对方笑了一下，说现在的表妹真体贴。下午，Klara把桌上的相框扣倒，又在没人时翻回来，用袖子擦了擦玻璃上的灰。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 39 -> 47; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 48 -> 44; reputation -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011122_

  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Klara把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Klara来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 4 -> 5; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 21 -> 16; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 50 -> 55; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils)_

- **1946.01** `p9-work-career.clerk-post/p9-work-career.clerk-post.hired` tags: work, career, craft, clerk, education, task, office, application
  Klara在保险公司写字间的小桌上抄完一页德文书信，又算出利息表上最后一行，速记只漏了两个词。Gustav Kral把卷子翻过来看背面，说手是稳的。月底他领到七十克朗，扣掉十五克朗保证金，签字画押，坐进靠窗那把椅子，桌上有一块吸墨纸和一支借来的笔。
  _effects: cash 55 (首月工钱扣除保证金后实发); skill; employment 保险写字间试用办事员 @ org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:006313; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116_

- **1946.01** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-office` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction
  Klara坐在那张有权力的桌子后面，签下自己的名字。愿望没有因此变近，但桌上的纸张、印章和等待回话的人，都变得具体了。
  _effects: reputation 0.05999999999999994_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012392_

- **1946.10** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-declined` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara正要把空床许给Ferdinand Berg，Luise Winter却在楼梯上拦住了，说这条街上去年有人招了生人合住，失过钥匙，惹出些麻烦。Klara回家把铺盖重新叠好，回绝了Ferdinand Berg，只让他白天来喝口热水。空床仍空着，房租还是从自己的工钱里补。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000750:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1947.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction, partnership, household
  Franz Stein 2在几天后找到Klara，说可以试试。两人开始合付房租，也把各自欠的账摊在桌上。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-108; household; role; role; interaction_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012109_

- **1947.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Friedrich Winter的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction_

- **1947.01** `office16-favor.private-sinecure::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sinecure-created` tags: office, favor, corruption, patronage, family, power, vienna, urban
  Klara在编制表最下面添了一行“顾问”，月薪两克朗，职责是“必要时提供咨询”。Maria Lechner第二天就搬来一张靠窗的桌子，带来一盆天竺葵；一个月后，Mitzi Winter 2发现顾问唯一经手的文件是自己的午餐菜单。
  _effects: position 顾问 @morgue:person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:009912; organization_funds; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003489:child; knowledge “顾问岗位没有固定职责，考勤由我签字。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1947.04** `bc18-ghost.death-certificate::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/correct-it-twice` tags: bureaucracy, legal, death, identity, mistake, paperwork, absurd, vienna
  Klara在窗口要求更正，Mitzi Winter 2把表格翻了三遍，最后开出一张新的出生证明，名字却少了一个字母。于是城里同时住着一个死人、一个活人，和一个只在档案里出生的陌生人。Klara拿着两张纸站在区公所门口，忽然不知道该用哪一张去领下个月的煤票。
  _effects: cash -1 (更正材料的手续费和车钱); state_add bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:676; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; condition; flag +two_living_files_one_death; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +bureaucratic_ghost; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1948.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/stay-with-relatives` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family, injury
  分房名单又往后推了一年，Klara一家搬进亲戚家的厨房隔间。Berta Stein送来一块旧帘子当隔断；夜里有人起身，全屋都听得见。
  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs); health -0.030000000000000027 (crowded sleeping space); condition; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-127; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-127_

- **1948.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/daughter-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Helene Winter。接生的人收六克朗，Friedrich Winter在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Klara起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; health -0.07999999999999996 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee)_

- **1948.01** `bc18-ghost.double-tax::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/ghost-pays` tags: bureaucracy, tax, identity, paperwork, legal, absurd, money, vienna
  死人的档案里挂着一笔从未结清的税，Klara索性让那笔账继续欠着，自己用活人的名字去领煤票。两个窗口各自记账，互不打扰；Klara每月省下一克朗，却要绕两条街，避免同时经过两家税务所。
  _effects: cash -1 (绕路多花的车钱); state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 40 -> 48; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 15 -> 27; flag +clean_living_tax_record; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1948.01** `state_reaction:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/another-file-opened` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  Klara刚在一个窗口证明自己活着，另一个窗口又从抽屉里抽出一张死亡通知。两个办事员隔着走廊互相看了一眼，谁也没有先开口；Klara站在中间，忽然明白自己不是一个人，而是一份正在被转抄的卷宗。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 27 -> 39; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.duplicates 2 -> 3; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.sightings 2 -> 3; flag +another_file_opened; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013297_

  一个税务员把Klara的死亡记录和一张新房租收据订在一起，寄到了另一个区。三个星期后，Klara收到一封要求“已故纳税人”补缴暖气税的信；去问时，窗口说档案正在路上，让Klara先等通知。通知没有来，房租却涨了。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 48 -> 55; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 39 -> 49; cash -1 (跑窗口的车钱); flag +heating_tax_for_the_dead; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Klara把Friedrich Winter的礼拜日外套叠好，放在门房。两人去市政办事处问过，办事员说没有哪张表格能写清这种事；神父也不肯在文书上签字。Friedrich Winter把铺盖搬到工友的合租房，Klara退掉Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang的大房间，在同一栋楼里租了一间后屋，把结婚照留在原来的墙上。

  Klara用三个月把账本上的零碎支出并成四类，又停掉两份没有人看的简报。年底结账时，Hedwig发现不仅没有赤字，还剩下够换一批档案柜的钱；没有剪彩，只有仓库里新柜子的木头味。

- **1949.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/another-window-opens` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  一个税务员把Klara的死亡记录和一张新房租收据订在一起，寄到了另一个区。三个星期后，Klara收到一封要求“已故纳税人”补缴暖气税的信；去问时，窗口说档案正在路上，让Klara先等通知。通知没有来，房租却涨了。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 55 -> 62; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 49 -> 59; cash -1 (跑窗口的车钱); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1949.01** `r13-fam.care-sick-child::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/fever-breaks` tags: task, care, health, child, fever, night, vienna, urban
  Klara把湿布拧到不再滴水，敷在Rosa Hofer的额头上。孩子烧得说胡话，Klara就一遍遍念窗外的街名，直到天快亮时，孩子的手心终于出了一层汗。Klara把被子掖好，自己靠在床沿睡了过去，醒来时锅里还剩半碗冷粥。
  _effects: cash -2 (第三遍换湿布); health -0.030000000000000027 (第三遍换湿布); condition; flag +nursed_child_fever; health 0.010000000000000009 (the fever broke after a night of cold compresses); relation guardian with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:009425:child_

- **1949.06** `r1-cul.photography-studio::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/eyes-closed` tags: culture, photography, portrait, poverty, vanity, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi Hofer收了Klara两克朗，让Klara坐在白布前别眨眼，数到十二。镁粉一闪，Anton Kral在快门声里轻轻咳了一下；Mitzi Hofer没说话，把底片夹进暗袋。三天后取片，Klara的眼睛是闭的，Anton Kral的手却清楚得能看见指节上的墨。Mitzi Hofer说这张不能挂橱窗，Klara还是把它夹进了账本。
  _effects: cash -2 (曝光十二秒); flag +portrait_with_closed_eyes; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; knowledge “the sitter coughed during the exposure”_

- **1949.09** `r3-mig.residency-registration::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::reform-new-register/landlady-refused-signature` tags: migration, registration, housing, papers, bureaucracy, vienna, urban, postwar
  Gustav Kral把登记表推过来，要房东签字证明Klara住在那里。Katharina Winter在窗口外听见，立刻说自己只是合租，不能替人担责。Gustav Kral把表收回，在户籍卡上盖了“地址不明”。Klara当晚回去，门锁已经换了，房东隔着门说登记的事找别处。
  _effects: mood -0.09000000000000008; condition; flag +registration_refused_by_landlady; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000764:child; knowledge “a landlord’s signature is required for registration”_

  Katharina Berg把一把小钥匙和一张没有地址的卡片留在后台，说“你会知道是哪扇门”。Klara把钥匙带回家，Rosa Huber在缝纫机抽屉里找到它，问这是哪里的；Klara说不知道，却把钥匙攥得很紧。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 55 -> 65; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 45 -> 50; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +kept_the_key_

  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027_

  这一年，Klara学会了同时带三张纸出门：一张证明活着，一张证明死了，一张证明自己从没搬过家。窗口后面的人各自只看自己那一张，偶尔还会夸Klara的材料齐全。年底，Klara从补发的配给里省下一小袋面粉，觉得自己像个熟练的走私犯。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 62 -> 66; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 59 -> 54; cash 1 (省下的配给和车钱); mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Klara把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Rosa Berg借给Klara一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Klara睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。

  _effects: cash 2 (卖出半锅扁豆汤); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

  一个陌生女人在电车上叫住Klara，说自己认识她的“那位”，还劝她趁年轻找个正经人家。Klara没有回答，提前一站下了车；走在回家的路上，她发现自己的步子越来越快，像在躲一个并不存在的人。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 66 -> 74; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 19 -> 23; reputation -0.030000000000000027; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.01** `agency:publish` tags: agency, agency:publish
  Klara把一篇短文交到印刷所，谈当前的争论；几天后，认识字的人在咖啡馆里传阅它。
  _effects: knowledge “我读到Klara关于当前争论的文字。”; knowledge “我读到Klara关于当前争论的文字。”; knowledge “我读到Klara关于当前争论的文字。”; reputation 0.030000000000000027_

- **1953.02** `vienna-historical-social-movement.public-meeting/back-immediate-action` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  有人喊罢工，有人喊谈判，Klara站到前排说先动才有得谈。保守派转身去拿大衣，Friedrich Winter追出去又回来，说外头雪深，别把人推到对面去。会议没散，火气没降。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002251:child; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1953.02** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.river-walk/river-walk.bridge` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, walk, river
  Klara在桥边站了一会儿，看一艘平底船靠岸卸煤，几个工人用铁锹往岸上甩。风把煤灰吹到她脸上，她抹了一把，往回走时脚步比来时轻，回到巷口还给自己买了块三赫勒的糖。
  _effects: mood 0.06000000000000005; cash -0.03 (买了三赫勒的糖)_

- **1953.05** `r15-wish.power-offer::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/real-appointment` tags: politics, power, wish, office, status, vienna, urban, postwar

  _effects: position 部门主管 @org-sdap_

  会议室里有人念了一份动议，说Klara把预算当成人情。Emil Hofer把签名页传了一圈，最后停在自己手里：支持票比反对票少三张。Klara没有发言，只把下一季度的支出表放在桌上，表上每一笔都有收据。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; knowledge “动议只拿到少数支持，三名原本签名的委员在投票前撤回了。”; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105_

- **1954.01** `task-vienna-family-care.reconcile-siblings/meet-at-church-door` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  两人谁也没提柜子，饭吃得客气而生分。临走时Klara提议：和不和好另说，每年忌日就在教堂门口见一面，站十分钟也好。Elisabeth Bauer想了一下，说行，但别指望他进屋。Klara把这话记在心上，回去路上把没送出去的半包烟丝塞回口袋。
  _effects: relation kin with person-elisabeth-bauer; flag +truce_with_sibling_

- **1954.08** `office16-corruption.inspection::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/cover-up` tags: office, corruption, audit, legal, power, dark, vienna, urban
  Klara请Gustav Kral去咖啡馆，把一只没有标记的信封留在桌上。Gustav Kral没有碰信封，只把报告里“重复报销”改成“记账口径不一”；作为交换，Klara签了Gustav Kral侄子的实习证明。两个人都没有提那只信封后来去了哪里。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; flag +bribed_auditor; knowledge “审计报告里的“重复报销”被改成了“记账口径不一”，实习证明由我签字。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Klara把夜里咳醒的次数写在一张包装纸上，Elisabeth Kral 2扫了一眼，说是心事太重，给了几包安神粉。走廊尽头的护士压低声音，说住院部还有空床，但要医生签字。Klara把包装纸折回去，没再问。
  _effects: cash -1 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +clinic_dismissed_as_nerves; knowledge “the clinic doctor refused a hospital referral”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131_

  _effects: cash 2.3 (向邻居借来的现钱); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000750:child; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  化验单被Klara塞进炉子，第二天照常去上工。午休时咳出一口带血丝的痰，Helene Weiss 2用鞋底把它蹭进煤渣里，谁也没有提。那天下午，车间里照旧响着皮带轮，只是Klara每搬一箱纸都要停一停。

- **1957.12** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Klara看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Josef Kral追出来问怎么不领了，Klara说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Klara把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

### 292 subject events

- **1914.10** `vienna-infant-household.laundry-day/wet-floor` tags: infancy, health, family, home, injury
  Rosa Lechner端盆出去倒水，灶边淌了一地。Mitzi在摇床里翻身，摇床被湿地板滑得挪了位，撞在桌脚上。Rosa Lechner回来时孩子哭得满脸通红，摇床腿松了一条。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (摇床撞上桌脚，受了惊); cash -0.3 (请楼下木匠紧摇床腿); mood -0.030000000000000006_

- **1917.12** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/landlord-complaint` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  摔伤之后两天，房东女人在门厅拦住Rosa Lechner，楼里的Grete Stein正提着菜篮站在门边。房东女人说楼梯间不许孩子嬉闹，铁扶手磕坏要赔。Rosa Lechner把裹着布条的Mitzi往身后一拉，回了句“灯都没有，谁看得见台阶”。房东女人把话记在房租本边上，说下回修灯的钱从房租里扣。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; mood -0.07; flag +stairs_accident_noted_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Hermann Kral把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Mitzi伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Hermann Kral从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Mitzi手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Mitzi一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1919.01** `vienna-life-school-enrolment/enrolled` tags: childhood, school, fees
  Rosa Lechner带Mitzi到Leopoldstädter Volksschule报名。Grete Stein在名册上写下孩子的姓名和出生月份，收了两克朗书本费，让孩子第二天带一块石板来。
  _effects: education primary; cash -2 (school books); role; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-carried-afternoon` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  Helene Bauer哭得打嗝，手指一直朝井台方向指。Mitzi蹲下把Helene Bauer驮上背，沿着墙根走，数砖缝给Helene Bauer听：一、二、三……数到第四圈，背上的呼吸才匀了。天黑时Mitzi的胳膊麻得抬不过肩，Rosa Lechner回来接过孩子，Mitzi扶着门框甩了好一会儿手。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (背弟妹走了一下午，胳膊酸痛); skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000458:child_

- **1919.02** `iw18-friend.chair-by-the-stove::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/a-guest-named-by-the-child` tags: childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, family, wonder, rural, agriculture
  Mitzi从碗橱里多拿了一只缺口的盘子，摆在灶边那把没人坐的椅子上，还认真地告诉屋里的人：今天有客人。盘子里放着一小块土豆和一片面包边。Rosa Lechner问客人叫什么，Mitzi说了一个从没听过的名字。
  _effects: state_add iw18-imaginary-friend state:world-vienna-1912:26; food; mood 0.04; flag +invisible_guest_named; flag +imaginary_friend_active_

- **1919.02** `kernel-social.first-meeting::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/first-meeting.coal-basket` tags: social, friendship, first_meeting, childhood, neighbor, everyday, rural, agriculture
  在合住的出租公寓的楼梯上，Mitzi抱着一筐煤，Rosa从楼上下来，伸手托住筐底。两人把煤抬到四楼，在窗台上分了一块黑面包；此后上学、提水都结伴，谁家晚归就替谁留门。煤灰落在鞋面上，他们用同一块破布擦手。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; role; flag +childhood_friend; knowledge “那个总在楼梯上帮忙的孩子住在同一栋楼的顶层。”_

- **1919.10** `iw18-play.toy-theatre::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-parent-sells-the-theatre` tags: childhood, play, theatre, family, wonder, comic, rural, agriculture
  Rosa Lechner把鞋盒戏台和旧纸板一起卖给了收废纸的人，换回几枚硬币和一小筐煤。Mitzi放学回家，看见床底空了，没有哭，只把剩下的硬纸狗藏进课本里。那天晚上，Rosa Lechner多给孩子盛了半勺汤。
  _effects: cash 1 (the toy theatre was sold as waste paper); mood -0.05; flag +toy_theatre_sold_

- **1920.01** `vienna-life-school-day/punished` tags: childhood, school, hunger, injury
  Mitzi在课堂上和同桌说话，Elisabeth Huber用戒尺打了手心。孩子把红肿的手藏在桌下，放学后不敢让家里人看见；此后听见戒尺敲桌沿，Mitzi的肩膀就先缩一下，对Elisabeth Huber也多了几分怕。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (punishment at school); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1920.01** `iw18-friend.takes-the-blame::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-sibling-knows` tags: childhood, imaginary_friend, blame, family, secret, small_cruelty, rural, agriculture
  Rosa从门后探出头，说看见是Mitzi自己碰倒的。Mitzi赶紧把Rosa拉到楼梯间，答应把攒了半年的彩色玻璃珠分一半。Rosa想了一会儿，说还要替自己写一页大字；两个人在楼梯上坐了很久，牛奶在厨房里慢慢渗进砖缝。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.secrecy 25 -> 30; cash -1 (milk lost on the kitchen floor); flag +sibling_keeps_friend_secret; mood -0.019999999999999997_

- **1920.01** `state_tick:iw18-imaginary-friend/friend-year/still-there` tags: iw18-imaginary-friend, childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, imagination, secret, state
  又过了一年，Mitzi还给那把空椅子留着位置。椅面上有一道被裤子磨亮的痕迹，大人说那是旧漆；Mitzi知道那是朋友坐出来的。冬天煤少，Mitzi把最暖的位置让给椅子，自己坐在床沿。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.closeness 60 -> 63; state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.secrecy 30 -> 32; mood 0.020000000000000004_

  Mitzi空手站在窗口，听掌柜说“叫你Alois Lechner来”，耳朵一直红到脖子。后墙的渣堆冻得硬，Mitzi用鞋尖刨开表层，把煤核一颗颗捡进围裙。回家路上碰到同巷的孩子，Mitzi把纸袋往身后藏，进门才交给Alois Lechner。
  _effects: mood -0.04000000000000001; food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

- **1920.04** `iw18-school.ink-pot::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-child-takes-revenge` tags: childhood, school, cruelty, shame, teacher, ink, rural, agriculture
  放学后，Mitzi把剩下的半瓶墨水滴进Rosa挂在椅背上的外套口袋。第二天Rosa的母亲来学校闹了一场，Grete Stein没有再查是谁干的；Mitzi坐在最后一排，看见Rosa红着眼圈，忽然觉得那块墨迹比自己的抄写本还难看。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; reputation -0.02; mood 0.020000000000000004; flag +ink_revenge_

- **1920.05** `iw18-secret.cellar-door::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-parent-saw-the-child` tags: childhood, secret, family, cellar, knowledge, fear, rural, agriculture
  Mitzi刚走到地窖最后一级台阶，Rosa Lechner就从煤堆后面站起来，说“上去”。Mitzi转身时看见墙缝里露出一点油纸的角，回到厨房后，Rosa Lechner把一碗汤推到孩子面前，谁也没有先开口。
  _effects: state_add iw18-child-secret state:world-vienna-1912:30; knowledge “大人从地窖墙缝里站起来，让我立刻上楼。”; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner; mood -0.039999999999999994; flag +caught_at_cellar_door; flag +child_secret_active_

- **1921.01** `vienna-life-child-labour/runs-errands` tags: childhood, labour, piecework, injury
  Mitzi放学后替Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的排字工送校样，穿过两条街，拿到一克朗。回家时天已经黑了，Rosa Lechner把饭留在锅里；Mitzi的鞋底磨薄，脚后跟起了泡，却把那一克朗捏得平平整整，放进Rosa Lechner的围裙口袋；这跑腿的差事从此固定下来，Mitzi也算家里一个能挣钱的孩子。
  _effects: cash 1 (running errands); health -0.010000000000000009 (walking in the cold); role_

- **1921.01** `iw18-secret.confession::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-teacher-keeps-it` tags: childhood, secret, confession, school, relief, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Mitzi在空教室里把地窖的事说了一遍，说到一半就开始抠桌沿的木刺。Anna Huber没有打断，只把窗户关上，等孩子说完才问“你现在怕什么”。Mitzi说怕大人知道自己看见过；Anna Huber说知道了也不会少一块肉。
  _effects: state_remove iw18-child-secret state:world-vienna-1912:30; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; knowledge “孩子在地窖里看见了大人藏起来的东西。”; mood 0.04000000000000001; flag +teacher_kept_secret; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; flag -child_secret_active_

  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1921.02** `iw18-home.ration-tin::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/puts-back-the-coin` tags: childhood, family, money, hunger, secret, school, rural, agriculture
  Mitzi已经把硬币攥在手心，走到门口又折回来，把硬币放回铁盒，还照着原来的顺序摆好。Alois Lechner回来时，Mitzi正在数面粉袋上的补丁；那天下午孩子没有出门，只把面包铺的香味想了一遍又一遍。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 55 -> 57; mood -0.009999999999999995; flag +put_coin_back_

- **1921.02** `r14-soc.childhood-oath::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/shared-last-bread` tags: childhood_friendship, childhood_oath, poverty, loyalty, neighbor, rural, agriculture, small_town
  面包房后门的泔水桶边，Helene Bauer捡起半块没沾灰的黑面包，掰成两半。Mitzi把大的那半推回去，说自己早上吃过；其实Mitzi的胃从昨夜就在叫。两人蹲在台阶上吃完，约好谁也不许把这件事说出去。多年后Mitzi仍记得，那半块面包边上有股煤灰味。
  _effects: cash -1 (砖墙根下的铜纽扣); mood 0.05; flag +shared_last_bread; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000458:child_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  两个孩子在院子两头跑，一个递，一个夹，床单在风里鼓起来。最后一条挂好，Alois Winter从厨房拿出面包，先给了自家孩子，再掰一块给Mitzi。Mitzi把面包塞进围裙口袋，回家后放在家里人的碗边，说自己在外面吃过了。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

- **1922.06** `vienna-school-childhood.fountain-pen/fountain-pen.gift` tags: school, social, childhood
  旧钢笔的笔尖歪向左边，Mitzi写出来的字母也跟着歪。Rosa Lechner说工具包里翻出来的，凑合用。Grete Stein的红圈画在歪字母旁边。
  _effects: skill; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1922.08** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-ok` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Mitzi在土豆摊前蹲了很久，挑了一磅没有芽眼的。付完钱，手里只剩几个铜板，又去买了盐。围裙兜太浅，Mitzi用下巴压住布边，走两步就低头看一眼。到家Alois Lechner把土豆倒进盆里，盐包放在灶台边。
  _effects: food; cash -0.15 (市场花销); relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1922.09** `vienna-childhood-friends.hand-in-hand/glove-left-behind` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  课间操回来，同桌把一只毛线手套塞进Mitzi的桌肚，说家里多出来的。手套的拇指处补过，线头还没剪干净。放学时同桌被家里人叫走，手套留在抽屉里。Mitzi把它带回家，塞进枕头套，晚上伸手摸了好几次。
  _effects: mood 0.06; condition_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.stay-after-school/stay-twice-cleaning` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, teacher, learning
  错题纸被Mitzi攥出了汗印。Anna Huber看了一眼，说留两次可以，先把炉灰倒了。灰桶提到后院时，风从巷口灌进来，灰扑了Mitzi一脸。Anna Huber回来没催，用粉笔在黑板上把一道竖式拆成三步，让Mitzi跟着写在自己的石板边上。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1923.06** `r1-child.teacher-good-seat::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/made-a-seat-in-the-aisle` tags: school, favouritism, seating, class, parents, university, culture, schooling
  Mitzi自己搬着凳子坐到第三排旁边的过道上，说这里也能看见黑板。Helene Berg让她坐了一节课，下课时没有赶。第二天，过道上多了两张凳子。第三天Helene Berg索性把整排座位往前挪了半尺。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; flag +made_own_seat; skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1923.07** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-confiscated` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  书页在桌肚里翻动时，Anna Berg已经站在旁边。Mitzi的手还按在算术本上，书被抽走，书脊在讲台边磕了一声。Anna Berg看了一眼折角，说“放学留下”，又拉开抽屉把书放进去。Mitzi盯着抽屉缝，整节课没听进去。
  _effects: mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

- **1923.09** `r1-child.truancy-vintage::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/officer-on-the-field-path` tags: truancy, rural, harvest, school, labour, university, culture, schooling
  九月的葡萄园缺人手，Rosa Lechner让Mitzi留在家里捡落果，说学校少一个孩子没人会数。第三天，Maria Kral骑着自行车沿田埂找来，车铃在石墙间响。Rosa Lechner说孩子在学怎么活，Maria Kral说活也得先识数。
  _effects: cash 2 (葡萄熟的时候不上学); flag +harvest_absence; relation rival with person-maria-kral; education primary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1924.01** `state_tick:wish-knowledge/knowledge-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_tick
  Mitzi又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.02; flag +wish:knowledge:pursuing_

- **1924.01** `r13-edu.school-transfer::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/transfer-approved` tags: education, school, transfer, bureaucracy, family, university, culture, schooling
  Mitzi的转学材料缺一张居住证明，Gustav Berg让Mitzi去区公所补。Mitzi跑了三趟，第三趟正赶上下雨，Gustav Berg看见Mitzi把湿透的纸一张张摊在柜台上，没再挑毛病，盖了章。Mitzi赶在开学前一天把名字写进新学校。那间教室在二楼，Mitzi第一天就找到了靠窗的座位，把旧课本摆好。
  _effects: flag +school_transfer_approved_

- **1924.06** `r3-crime.juvenile-court::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/sent-to-reform-school` tags: crime, youth, court, family, school, university, culture, schooling
  Maria Kral说家里管不了，不如让学校管。Rosa Lechner想说什么，被Josef Berg 2递过来的一张表格挡住了。Mitzi被带走时，Rosa Lechner追到走廊尽头，把一包糖塞进Mitzi的口袋。Josef Berg 2说“到了那边会检查”，又把糖拿走了。
  _effects: condition; flag +sent_to_reform_school; role; relation rival with person-rosa-lechner; move place-wien-volksschule-leopoldstadt; education apprenticeship; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1925.01** `statistical_infection`
  Mitzi在Leopoldstädter Volksschule病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1925.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.ask-for-trial/too-young-refused` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:work
  Franz Stein把Mitzi的袖子往上捋了捋，看了看手腕，说骨头还没长开，搬不动整摞的纸。Mitzi没走，蹲在门口把被风刮散的纸边捡回筐里。Franz Stein给了两个赫勒，挥手赶人；Mitzi沿着街把另外两家作坊的门牌记下，才回家。
  _effects: cash 0.2 (替作坊捡纸边得到的两个赫勒); mood -0.04000000000000001; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

- **1925.03** `r13-turn.exam-failure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school::reform-open-secondary/retake-fee` tags: education, exam, failure, school, work, reform, medical, schooling
  成绩单上差两分。Grete Stein说可以补考，但要先交一克朗卷子费，还得让Alois Lechner来签字。Alois Lechner在办公室门口把帽子捏变了形，说家里拿不出这份钱。Mitzi把错题抄到烟盒背面，第二天照样去上课，只是不再举手。
  _effects: cash -1 (落榜名单上的第三个名字（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.15; condition; flag +failed_entrance_exam; skill; relation guardian with person-alois-lechner_

- **1925.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Mitzi的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1925.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Mitzi的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1925.07** `r2-faith.parish-school-fee::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/free-place-granted` tags: faith, school, charity, education, fees, reform, medical, schooling
  Alois Lechner在学费簿上找到Mitzi的名字，用红笔在旁边写了“免”。Luise Stein说课本还得自己买，Alois Lechner就从柜子里翻出一本旧算术，封面写着上一个学生的名字。Mitzi把书抱回家，Rosa Lechner用报纸包了书皮，说念完这本再说。
  _effects: flag +received_free_parish_school_place; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; knowledge “the parish keeps a red-ink list of fee exemptions”; education primary_

- **1925.07** `statistical_recovery`
  Mitzi熬过了这场肺炎，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1925.11** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/unpaid-trial` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Anna Berg先说试三个月，不给工钱，只管一顿午饭。家里同意了，因为多一张嘴比多一双手更难。Mitzi每天最早到，最晚走，三个月后师傅才把契约拿出来。
  _effects: food; mood -0.04000000000000001; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:66; education apprenticeship; role_

- **1925.12** `r15-education.entry-offer::ctx-vienna-medical-1930::milieu-university/full-scholarship` tags: education, scholarship, opportunity, medical, university, knowledge, student, research
  Mitzi的成绩打动了本地学校，拿到一份覆盖学费的奖学金；家里第一次不用为这笔钱吵到半夜。
  _effects: state_add scholarship-holder state:world-vienna-1912:67_

- **1926.01** `vienna-life-apprenticeship/printshop-apprentice` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, employment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne让Mitzi下周一到排字房报到，先搬铅字和扫地，月薪十八克朗。家里用第一笔预付工资买了双结实的鞋。
  _effects: education apprenticeship; employment Schriftsetzerlehrling @ org-winter-print; cash 6 (apprentice advance)_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/mistake` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Mitzi废掉了一块料，师傅扣了工钱。晚饭时谁也没提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 0 -> 1; cash -1 (wasted material); mood -0.04000000000000001_

- **1926.01** `r13-civ.first-aid-course::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/fainted-at-demo` tags: health, first_aid, course, skill, school, reform, medical, schooling
  演示到骨折固定时，Gustav Berg把夹板按在Mitzi的腿上，Mitzi忽然眼前发白，从凳子上滑下去。醒来时，Rosa正拿湿毛巾拍Mitzi的额头，教室里有人在笑。
  _effects: mood -0.06; flag +fainted_during_first_aid_demo; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg_

- **1926.06** `r14-edu.leaving-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/sent-to-factory` tags: education, school_dropout, child_labour, poverty, reform, medical, schooling, school
  Rosa Lechner把Mitzi的书包从钉子上取下来，说厂里招装订工，明天就去。Luise Stein傍晚来家里，站在门口劝了半个钟头，Rosa Lechner只说灶上还坐着锅。第二天清早，Mitzi穿着改短的围裙出门，书包留在门后，里面还有半块橡皮。头一个月工钱三克朗。
  _effects: cash 3 (书包挂在门后的钉子上); role; education none_

- **1926.07** `yf18-runaway.bundle::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/hide-the-bundle` tags: runaway, escape, family, plan, night_train, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi把两件衬衫、一双袜子和小时候的洗礼证明卷进一块旧桌布，塞到床底下。Alois Lechner在厨房里数硬币，数了三遍都没有抬头；Mitzi躺在黑暗里，听见夜车从远处经过，铁轨的声音一直传到枕头下面。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:90; flag +yf18_bundle_hidden; mood 0.01999999999999999; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1926.11** `r13-turn.school-leaving::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school::reform-leaving-age/straight-to-factory` tags: education, school, work, family, transition, reform, medical, schooling
  Anna Berg把Mitzi的名字从升学名单上划掉，说厂里正缺人手。家里没有人争，只把午饭里的半块面包塞给Mitzi。Mitzi进了纺织厂，工牌上写着“临时”，每天站十二个钟头，月底工钱比学徒多两克朗。
  _effects: cash 2 (毕业钟响过以后); condition; flag +left_school_for_factory; role_

- **1927.01** `r3-teen.scholarship-interview::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school::reform-scholarship-board/awarded` tags: education, scholarship, interview, class, institution, reform, medical, schooling
  面试官问Mitzi最近读的一本书。Mitzi说的是从旧书摊上买来的植物图鉴，还背出了拉丁名。Maria Kral在表格上写了“给予”，Helene Berg在门外听见，先把眼泪擦掉才进来。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; flag +won_scholarship_interview; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; education secondary_

  Maria Kral判Rosa Lechner监管不力，罚了六克朗，从下个月的救济金里扣。Rosa Lechner在法庭上数了三遍口袋，最后把戒指摘下来放在桌上。Maria Kral看了一眼，说“这个不收”，让书记员记下分期付款。Mitzi一直低着头，看见Rosa Lechner的手在抖。

- **1928.01** `task-vienna-youth-ask-old-worker/lesson-given` tags: task, task:education, work, apprenticeship, learning

- **1928.03** `vienna-youth-threshold.first-wage/half-for-shoes` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  鞋铺橱窗里一双结实的工作鞋标价四克朗二十赫勒，Mitzi的鞋底已经能吸进雪水。回家后Mitzi把信封拆开，一半放在桌上，一半揣进内袋，说下个月不向家里要鞋钱。Alois Lechner没说话，只把桌上的钱一张张抹平。
  _effects: cash 4 (工资的一半交给家里); cash -4.2 (买一双不漏水的工作鞋); health 0.05 (脚不再整日泡在湿冷里); relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1928.09** `vienna-adolescence.stolen-roll/caught-and-returned` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Mitzi的手刚碰到面包皮，身后就有人清了清嗓子。Alois Lechner站在晾衣绳下，手里还捏着夹子，什么也没问，只把手伸过来。Mitzi把面包放回筐里，手背挨了两下，面包筐的盖子被Alois Lechner合上，发出一声闷响。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; mood -0.09999999999999998_

  格拉茨的表姐来信，说旅馆缺一个叠床单的女孩，管吃住，工钱少但能攒下。Mitzi把信藏在算术本里，用铅笔在背面写回信；邮票是从Rosa Lechner的抽屉里偷的，信封上不敢写自己的名字，只写了“内详”。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:132; cash -1 (a stolen stamp and writing paper); flag +yf18_cousin_reply; mood 0.02999999999999997_

  _effects: state_remove yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:132; move place-wien; cash -2 (a few coins taken from the kitchen drawer); relation kin with person-rosa-lechner; flag +yf18_left_in_night; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Mitzi觉得一个人去不合适，绕到教堂请了修女一同上楼。修女看过病人，留下药包和一小瓶圣水，说会从教区账上出钱请医生来看一趟。Maria Winter在楼下等着，说这样妥当，病人家里没男人，多个见证人心里安稳。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +parish_sick_visitor; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

  这一年，Mitzi学会了同时带三张纸出门：一张证明活着，一张证明死了，一张证明自己从没搬过家。窗口后面的人各自只看自己那一张，偶尔还会夸Mitzi的材料齐全。年底，Mitzi从补发的配给里省下一小袋面粉，觉得自己像个熟练的走私犯。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 35 -> 39; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 52 -> 47; cash 1 (省下的配给和车钱); mood 0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水); health -0.020000000000000004 (牙病拖延未治); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_

  她在一家面包店门口摆开签名纸，Hedwig第一个签，写完又把丈夫的名字也添上。到傍晚，纸上有一百二十个名字；Gustav Kral收下时数了两遍，说“我们会研究”。回家的电车上，她发现签名纸的边角被汗浸软了。
  _effects: role; reputation 0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; cash -1 (签名纸、墨水和面包店门口的租金); knowledge “请愿书要按街道分开，窗口职员会当面数签名”; flag +petition_filed_

- **1934.05** `ds17-courtship.parlour::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-second-sunday` tags: love, courtship, intimacy, secret, class, secret_intimacy, vienna, urban
  Mitzi在约瑟夫城租下一间带钢琴的会客室，每小时一克朗，女房东把钥匙拴在一截蓝线上。Katharina Berg来时带着一束用报纸包好的紫罗兰，Emil Weiss坐在窗边打毛线，偶尔抬头看一眼钟。第三个星期日，Emil Weiss说自己要去楼下买咖啡，把门留了半掩。
  _effects: state_add forbidden-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:307; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000265:child; cash -1 (the parlour, the coffee and a bunch of violets); mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +rented_a_parlour; flag +secret_attachment_active_

- **1934.11** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.food-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family

  Mitzi考得不错，Gustav Kral把分数记在名册上，说位置可以给他，但保证金十五克朗要先交，还得有铺保。他手头只有十克朗出头，保人一时也找不到。椅子给了下一位，那人进门时袖口已经熨得笔直。Mitzi把答卷折好带走，纸边被汗浸软了。
  _effects: cash -1 (往返内城的车钱与考试杂费); mood -0.050000000000000044; reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1935.07** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty, injury
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Mitzi Weiss把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Mitzi把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Mitzi Weiss低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; flag +rent_increase_accepted; health -0.020000000000000018 (winter coal money spent on the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1935.08** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.friend-intercession/intercession-concert-ticket` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  Mitzi不再让Berta Winter传话，托其带去一张星期天音乐会的站票，花了一克朗二十赫勒，位置在柱子后头。票上不写多余的字，只说若不方便，票留着也无妨。Berta Winter把票揣进衬衫内袋，Mitzi则把找回来的铜板一枚枚放进铁盒，等一个回音。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (音乐会站票一张); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87_

- **1939.10** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.lost-dog/lost-dog.return-address` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, animal, neighbor
  她照着铜牌一路问到那栋楼，门房说裁缝天不亮就出门了，天黑才回。Mitzi把狗绳在栏杆上绕了两圈，蹲下来拍了拍狗脑袋，狗伸出舌头舔了舔她的手背，才跟着门房往门廊里走。

  Gustav Berg把Mitzi的工龄册翻到最后一页，又对照了厂里寄来的解雇单，说可以领十二周。钱不多，每周够买面包和煤。Mitzi在表格上按了手印，Gustav Berg把一张薄薄的领取券推过来，说下周同一时间来，别迟到。
  _effects: cash 6 (补助金窗口的第二张表); flag +work13_unemployment_benefit_approved; knowledge “the benefit runs for twelve weeks and must be collected in p”_

- **1940.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  Mitzi在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Katharina Hofer 2从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (左手背烫伤); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69_

  Mitzi在包厢里啃干面包，Anna Hofer问是不是从河东来的。三句话之后，两个人认出了同一个集市。Anna Hofer把地址写在烟盒纸上，说到了那边可以找他舅舅。Grete Stein进来查票，看见两个人挤在一个座位上，什么也没说。
  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +met_stranger_from_same_district; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; knowledge “the stranger’s uncle lives near the destination”_

- **1940.12** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.defer` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi把抽屉拉开又推上，铜币在木板上滚了半圈。Emil Weiss合上本子说，拖欠的人不少，可规矩就是规矩。她走下两级台阶又回头提醒，发工钱那天她会在教堂侧门等着。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

  Mitzi把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Katharina Hofer 2抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Mitzi把床单对折，腾出一半，Katharina Hofer 2用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008901_

  Mitzi的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Mitzi看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: health -0.12 (wound infection); cash -14 (drainage and medicine); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008901_

  _effects: reputation 0.08000000000000002; cash 2 (the prize money and the new customers); state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:484; flag +model_household_medal; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +public_desire_active_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; mood 0.040000000000000036_

  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 38 -> 34; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 53 -> 55; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107_

- **1943.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_lost_landlady` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Elisabeth Winter 2借给Mitzi一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Mitzi睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119; mood -0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119_

  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 34 -> 30; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 55 -> 57; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  公证人把一封厚信推到桌子中央：Mitzi的叔父在第二区留下一间小印刷所、一台坏掉的压机和十四克朗的欠账。Katharina Hofer 2说那台压机早该当废铁，但订阅名单上还有三百个名字。Mitzi把名单卖给对街的印厂，替叔父还了债，剩下的钱刚好够换一套冬衣。
  _effects: cash 12 (sale of the print shop subscription list); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:597; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; skill; flag +inherited_print_shop; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1944.07** `df18-blessing.laundry-water::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-candle-stall` tags: blessing, omen, water, faith, fraud, consolation, religious, eerie
  Gustav Berg在门口摆了一只木箱，卖蜡烛和印着水渍的小卡片，说摸一摸就能好。Mitzi看着邻居把零钱收进围裙，没有阻止。几天后，楼道里排起了队；Mitzi从后门进出，怕被人认成那个“显灵”的女人。
  _effects: cash 2 (邻居分给subject的蜡烛钱); reputation 0.020000000000000018; knowledge “邻居把我的水渍变成了卖蜡烛的摊子。”; flag +df18_water_stain_shrine; condition; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  苏联士兵进城时，Mitzi躲在门后听见坦克履带压过石板路。几天后街口贴出告示，Mitzi去领了一份汤和一块面包。
  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents)_

  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Mitzi的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Mitzi想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。

  _effects: flag +sponsor_refused_liability; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000458:child; knowledge “joint liability covers unemployment and illness”_

- **1946.04** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty, injury
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Hermann Stein把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Mitzi把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Hermann Stein低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; health -0.020000000000000018 (winter coal money spent on the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98_

  Mitzi把抽屉拉开又推上，铜币在木板上滚了半圈。Berta Hofer合上本子说，拖欠的人不少，可规矩就是规矩。她走下两级台阶又回头提醒，发工钱那天她会在教堂侧门等着。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

  _effects: cash 0.6 (争执后按降低的工价结算); reputation 0.030000000000000027; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000275:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

  又过了一个冬天，Mitzi能从楼梯的响声判断是谁在值夜，也能从煤气灯的颜色判断哪一段管道该修。师傅把巡查路线改了三次，最后仍然照Mitzi画的那张走；两个人都没有提这件事。

  Mitzi记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Alois Lechner说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Alois Lechner陪着Mitzi去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); mood -0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: cash 4 (lodger rent); health -0.040000000000000036 (overcrowded housing); condition_

  Mitzi觉得一个人去不合适，绕到教堂请了修女一同上楼。修女看过病人，留下药包和一小瓶圣水，说会从教区账上出钱请医生来看一趟。Anna Bauer在楼下等着，说这样妥当，病人家里没男人，多个见证人心里安稳。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1948.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_burns_curtain` tags: home, social, family, housing

  Mitzi把四只洗衣盆按到厨房的先后排成一列，用粉笔在门后写下各家的时辰：六点到七点半、七点半到九点。Emil Hofer起初嘟囔，看到自家名字排在第二格，也就把水壶提了回去。晾衣绳按同样次序分两段，谁家的床单先上谁先占。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1949.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Anton Winter抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Mitzi把床单对折，腾出一半，Anton Winter用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。

  会议室里有人念了一份动议，说Mitzi把预算当成人情。Gustav Kral把签名页传了一圈，最后停在自己手里：支持票比反对票少三张。Mitzi没有发言，只把下一季度的支出表放在桌上，表上每一笔都有收据。
  _effects: reputation 0.040000000000000036; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; flag +survived_removal_motion; knowledge “动议只拿到少数支持，三名原本签名的委员在投票前撤回了。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1949.01** `r1-civ.harbouring-fugitive::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/hid-fugitive-overnight` tags: politics, hiding, fugitive, neighbor, risk, vienna, urban, postwar
  敲门声三下，停一停，又两下。Rosa Moser站在门外，大衣上全是雪，说只住一晚。Mitzi把床让出来，自己睡在灶边；半夜，Gustav Kral来查户口，Mitzi说床上是生病的表妹，Gustav Kral隔着门问了一句，没有进来。
  _effects: food; health -0.030000000000000027 (半夜的第三下敲门); condition; flag +hid_fugitive_overnight; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-111; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; knowledge “the household register lists only the tenant family”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-111_

- **1949.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Mitzi去了Versammlungsraum der graphischen Arbeiter，坐在后排听完了关于Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter W的争论；散会时带走一张油印提纲。
  _effects: historical development:initiative-org-printers-union-1939 as participant; knowledge “我参加了Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter Wiens在。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1949.03** `ml18-investment.transylvanian-mine::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-mine-was-salted` tags: investment, mining, fraud, money, risk, business, vienna, urban
  抽水机运到山里才发现，矿洞的岩壁上早被人撒过金粉，岩芯也是挑过的。Rosa Kral在两个月前把股份转给了一家空壳公司，办公室只剩一张桌子和半瓶墨水。Mitzi Winter 2把调查报告放在Mitzi面前，最后一页写着“建议不予起诉”，因为找不到被告。
  _effects: cash -50 (the investment in a salted mine); state_add ml18-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:727; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000902:child; condition; flag +salted_mine; knowledge “矿洞岩壁上的金粉是撒上去的，岩芯也被挑过。”; mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +fortune_reversed; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1949.11** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.keep-quiet` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life, injury
  止咳药水是褐色的，瓶口塞着软木塞，摇一摇会起沫。Mitzi看着工友把表折成小块，折了三折才塞进帽檐。墙上的规章还贴在那里，纸角被风掀起，没人去按。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; health -0.03999999999999998 (没有正规病假，只能靠药水撑着); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-143; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-143_

- **1950.01** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/a-relative-remembers` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Mitzi的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Mitzi家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 0 -> 1; flag +windfall_claim_by_relative; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014335_

- **1950.01** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/weathered` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, vienna, urban
  报纸在头版登了Mitzi和承包商在一张桌子上的照片，配文只有一句“谁签的字”。质询会开了三个小时，Mitzi把合同、比价单和验收记录一页页摊开；记者第二天没有再写，但把照片留在了档案里。
  _effects: reputation -0.030000000000000027; mood -0.01000000000000012; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:754; knowledge “照片是真的，合同也是真的；报纸没有再追，但档案里留了底片。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1950.01** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/the-papers-hear-about-it` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  报纸的社会版把Mitzi家的门牌号和那笔钱的数目印在一起，标题是“一笔不该有的好运”。第二天门口多了两个陌生人，一个说是远亲，一个说代表一家孤儿院。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.public false -> true; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 3; reputation -0.020000000000000018; flag +windfall_went_public_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014554_

- **1950.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Mitzi被叫到学校，老师说Katharina 2这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Mitzi没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 24 -> 19; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 52 -> 57; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.01** `state_tick:ml18-reversal/reversal-half-year/the-swing-holds` tags: ml18-reversal, money, luck, reversal, risk, debt, reputation, loss
  半年里没有新的坏消息。Mitzi把零钱分成三份：房租、还账、留一点在抽屉里。旧债主没有上门，咖啡馆里的人也不再每见一次就提那件赔本的事；日子没有变好，但至少不再往下滑。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-reversal.swing -55 -> -49; state_parameter ml18-reversal.strain 55 -> 51; cash 1 (small economies and an odd job); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1950.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-keeps-working` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Mitzi把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Mitzi来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 3 -> 4; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1950.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.apology-at-bakery/semmel-no-words` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  在柜台前买了两只 Semmel，递过一只，自己咬另一只，旧账一个字没提。对方接过面包，两人并肩站在店门口，把那点热气吃完。钱的事还横在中间，但对方说星期六照旧来喝咖啡，别又躲着。
  _effects: cash -0.14 (两只 Semmel); food; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Mitzi对Gustav Lechner 2说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Gustav Lechner 2把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Mitzi的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89_

- **1950.07** `vienna-women-housework.care-old-parent/care.hire-neighbor` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family, injury
  上午的日头照进走廊，Helene Lechner扶着Rosa Lechner慢慢挪到椅子上。Mitzi听着隔壁的动静，低头缝完一整排扣眼，抬头时发现汤锅已经坐在炉上，火不旺不弱。
  _effects: cash -2.4 (付给邻居半个月的搭手钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; mood 0.020000000000000018; health -0.010000000000000009 (夜里仍需起身照料，疲劳未消)_

  Grete Moser 2在签字前把一张剧院包厢的票根留在桌上，说是“给孩子的”。Mitzi把票根推回去，却在许可证上签了名；排水图仍缺一页，但窗口当天就盖了章。扩建开工后，第一场雨把后院淹了半尺。
  _effects: organization_funds; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; flag +permit_for_favor; knowledge “许可证少了一页排水图，盖章时没有补。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Mitzi把Emil 2的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Emil 2今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Mitzi听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 49 -> 57; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 46 -> 43; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.01** `r13-fam.visit-grave::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/flowers-placed` tags: task, family, grave, mourning, neighbor, grief, vienna, urban
  Mitzi陪Mitzi Moser 2去公墓，把一把野菊放在新翻的土上。木牌上的名字被雨冲得发白，Mitzi Moser 2用手指一笔一画描了一遍，描到最后一笔，指腹上全是泥。Mitzi没有劝，只蹲下来把歪掉的木牌扶正。
  _effects: cash -1 (公墓新翻的土); condition; flag +visited_a_grave_with_neighbour; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140_

  市立太平间缺一个夜班杂役，Emil Berg只问Mitzi怕不怕冷。地下室的抽屉一排十二个，有的拉开了就不再推回去；Mitzi的工作是登记遗物、给无名尸挂上编号，再在天亮前把地拖干净。
  _effects: organization_found 市立太平间 (state); position 太平间杂役 @morgue:person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:015171; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:788; cash 2 (municipal night wages); condition; flag +mortuary_worker; knowledge “太平间地下室的抽屉一排十二个，无名尸也要有编号。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-154; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1951.07** `oj18-zoo.night::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/meat-ledger` tags: zoo, animals, night, keeper, work, odd_job, suspense, vienna
  肉商的账本上少了二十公斤马肉，Mitzi Winter 2要Mitzi签字认账。Mitzi知道是夜班门房把肉转卖给了普拉特的香肠摊，但门房是Mitzi Winter 2的小舅子。Mitzi不签，第二天被调去扫兽栏；臭味洗了三遍还在指甲缝里。
  _effects: reputation -0.030000000000000027; cash -1 (lost the night-shift supplement); mood -0.040000000000000036; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:789; flag +zoo_night_shift; flag +refused_meat_ledger; knowledge “少掉的二十公斤马肉被夜班门房卖给了香肠摊，门房是园长的亲戚。”_

  Mitzi在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Mitzi去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为第二区夜班出租合作社的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role_

- **1952.01** `r13-work.factory-gate::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/turned-away` tags: work, hiring, factory, queue, cash, vienna, urban, postwar
  Anna Huber 2把名单卷起来，说今天只要会看图纸的。Mitzi在铁门外站到换班，听见里面皮带轮转了一整天。Elisabeth Kral 2分给Mitzi半块黑面包，两人谁也没提明天还来不来；电车票钱花掉了，口袋里的铜板只够买一小袋煤。
  _effects: cash -1 (铁门开了一条缝); mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +work13_turned_away_at_factory_gate; knowledge “the factory takes only ten men from a gate queue of over a h”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131_

- **1952.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Katharina 2这周主动擦了黑板。Mitzi把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 17 -> 20; mood 0.020000000000000018_
