- **1952.03** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/a-relative-remembers` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Mitzi的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Mitzi家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 0 -> 1; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015567_

- **1952.04** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Emil 2这周主动擦了黑板。Mitzi把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 57 -> 60; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015871_

- **1952.10** `r3-mig.xenophobic-landlord::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/prepaid-year-of-rent` tags: migration, housing, hostility, landlord, poverty, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi把一年的房租一次付清，十二克朗拍在桌上。Franz Moser点了两遍，收下，却在租约背面加了一行：不得留宿亲友，不得在走廊做饭。Mitzi问访客算不算留宿，Franz Moser说“你说呢”。
  _effects: cash -12 (门上的粉笔叉); condition; flag +prepaid_year_rent_foreigner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121; knowledge “the lease forbids visitors and hallway cooking”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-153_

- **1952.12** `df18-luck.borrowed-at-the-card-table::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/marked-deck` tags: luck, debt, bargain, gambling, obligation, night, supernatural, vienna
  Mitzi赢了三克朗，起身时才发现牌背有指甲划出的记号。Friedrich Lechner 2笑着把牌收进袖子里，说这叫“借来的手气”。Mitzi没有把钱退回去，只把那张有记号的牌偷偷带走。第二天，Mitzi在当铺门口看见Friedrich Lechner 2正在和一个收旧货的人分钱。
  _effects: cash 3 (牌桌上赢来的三克朗); state_add df18-borrowed-luck state:world-vienna-1912:828; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126; flag +df18_marked_deck; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +df18_luck_borrowed; knowledge “我得到了一点不该白拿的好运，代价还没有说清。”_

- **1953.01** `ml18-tax.audit-of-the-windfall::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/hide-the-ledger` tags: tax, legal, money, windfall, bureaucracy, comic, vienna, urban
  Elisabeth Kral 2把账本塞进钢琴后面的夹层，说等风声过去再拿出来。Emil Berg第二次来时带了一个记录员和一张搜查令；钢琴被挪开时，琴键响了一串不成调的音。Mitzi看见黑皮本翻开的那一页上，已经用红笔圈出了三笔利息。
  _effects: cash -10 (the accountant's fee for hiding the ledger); state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:867; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131; flag +tax_ledger_hidden; knowledge “黑皮本上被红笔圈出的三笔利息都没有报税。”; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.01** `state_reaction:df18-borrowed-luck/luck-windfall` tags: df18-borrowed-luck, luck, debt, obligation, superstition, risk, bargain, transfer
  Mitzi在路边捡到一枚硬币，或是在旧外套里摸出一张没花完的票子。高兴只持续了很短；Mitzi随即想起，那本账还在别人手里。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.luck 15 -> 20; state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.debt 15 -> 20; mood 0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:016449_

  Mitzi被叫到学校，老师说Emil 2这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Mitzi没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 60 -> 55; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 43 -> 48; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.030000000000000027_

  第六个月，债主在楼梯口拦住Mitzi，说利息不能总按原来的算法。Mitzi看着对方手里的账本，忽然发现那上面的墨迹比上次新，数字也比上次多。
  _effects: cash -2 (利息被重新计算); state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.debt 20 -> 26; state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.luck 20 -> 15_

- **1953.05** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.overnight` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family, injury
  Mitzi点着煤油灯缝到后半夜，把一捆领子全做完了。针脚密是密，可天亮时她看东西重影，手指被针扎了三回。星期六交活，Emil Berg量了量尺寸，收下整捆，数出七十五赫勒推过桌面。
  _effects: cash 0.75 (交了一整捆衬衫领子的计件工钱); health -0.040000000000000036 (通宵缝纫，眼睛疲劳，手指扎伤); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.10** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.labour-exchange/exchange-trial-work` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, labour_exchange, bureaucracy
  介绍所里那个Alois Lechner 2说木牌上的地址多半过时了，不如自己去问。Mitzi走了四十分钟到洗衣房，院子里晾着床单。老板娘让她先熨一件试试，看完说留下吧，明天还这个时辰来。
  _effects: cash 3 (洗衣房试工半天按件计酬); skill_

- **1953.10** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hospital-deposit` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Mitzi跑了一趟医院，把Rosa Lechner送进病房：押金三克朗，搪瓷杯和旧毛巾一克朗二十赫勒，头三天的药费三克朗八十赫勒，一共八克朗。护士收走便盆，换上带刻度的玻璃壶。Mitzi回到空了的床边，把塌下去的椅子扶正，才发现自己一整天只喝了一杯咖啡。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院押金和住院用品); mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +parent_hospitalized_

  报纸的社会版把Mitzi家的门牌号和那笔钱的数目印在一起，标题是“一笔不该有的好运”。第二天门口多了两个陌生人，一个说是远亲，一个说代表一家孤儿院。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.public true -> true; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 3 -> 5; reputation -0.020000000000000018_

  名单在委员会卡住了。Katharina Hofer 2的名字被划掉，理由是“没有相关资历”；Mitzi没有争，只把名单收回公文包。晚上，Katharina Hofer 2来问结果，Mitzi说再等一年；Katharina Hofer 2没有坐，站了一会儿就走了。
  _effects: reputation -0.020000000000000018; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; flag +appointment_list_rejected; knowledge “委员会以“没有相关资历”为由划掉了联络员的名字。”_

- **1954.01** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Mitzi和Elisabeth Kral 2谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Mitzi家，钥匙只在白天给。Elisabeth Kral 2说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Rosa Winter 3听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Mitzi把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  半年过去，Mitzi还了一点钱，也替债主跑了两趟腿。债主没有夸Mitzi，只在账本上划掉一小行；Mitzi却发现，自己开始下意识地绕开那家店。
  _effects: cash -1 (还掉一小笔); state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.debt 26 -> 18; state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.luck 15 -> 18; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  钱在一年里薄了一层：屋顶要修，姐夫来借，彩票站的老主顾说有个稳赚的买卖。Mitzi把账本翻到最后一页，发现写下的支出比记得的多出四克朗；窗外有人在敲铁皮，声音像在催账。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 32 -> 27; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.spent 0 -> 5; cash -1 (the account does not balance); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1954.01** `agency:organize_meeting` tags: agency, agency:organize_meeting
  Mitzi在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang召集了一次小范围会议，和Rosa Winter 2把下一次行动、会费和分工写在纸上。
  _effects: project; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; reputation 0.020000000000000018_

- **1954.04** `df18-blessing.laundry-water::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sit-with-the-stain` tags: blessing, omen, water, faith, fraud, consolation, religious, eerie
  洗衣房的墙上有一块水渍，形状像一个披着斗篷的女人。Mitzi第一次看见时，正好腰疼得直不起来；她点了一根蜡烛，坐了十分钟，疼没有消失，但呼吸慢了下来。Hermann Stein说那是圣母，Mitzi没有纠正，也没有把水渍擦掉。
  _effects: cash -1 (洗衣房里的一根蜡烛); mood 0.030000000000000027; knowledge “洗衣房的水渍像一个披斗篷的女人，我在它面前坐了一会儿。”; flag +df18_water_stain_consolation_

- **1954.07** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.defer` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi把抽屉拉开又推上，铜币在木板上滚了半圈。Rosa Winter 3合上本子说，拖欠的人不少，可规矩就是规矩。她走下两级台阶又回头提醒，发工钱那天她会在教堂侧门等着。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-133; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-133_

  Josef Huber在走廊里凑够了票数，把Mitzi负责的那间办公室转给了一个更听话的人。会议记录写得很干净：因工作需要。Mitzi回到办公室时，门牌已经换了，钥匙还能插进去，只是转不动。

  Mitzi请Helene Kral 2白天来照看，自己照常上工，每天多付一克朗。Helene Kral 2把Rosa Lechner扶到窗边晒太阳，回来时顺便买药；Mitzi晚上接班，给Rosa Lechner读报纸上的战况和物价，读到一半就有人睡着。读报的声音越来越低，最后只剩炉子里的煤响。

  Mitzi说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Emil Berg把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Emil Berg在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。

- **1955.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_lost_landlady` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Katharina Berg借给Mitzi一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Mitzi睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。

- **1955.08** `office16-corruption.inspection::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/cover-up` tags: office, corruption, audit, legal, power, dark, vienna, urban
  Mitzi请Emil Hofer去咖啡馆，把一只没有标记的信封留在桌上。Emil Hofer没有碰信封，只把报告里“重复报销”改成“记账口径不一”；作为交换，Mitzi签了Emil Hofer侄子的实习证明。两个人都没有提那只信封后来去了哪里。
  _effects: reputation -0.020000000000000018; flag +bribed_auditor; knowledge “审计报告里的“重复报销”被改成了“记账口径不一”，实习证明由我签字。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1956.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill/p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill.rain-turn-back` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  走到半路雨点砸下来，山道立刻变得溜滑。Mitzi和Katharina Weiss躲进路边的酒棚檐下，商量了几句，决定改乘电车回家，车票六十赫勒。Mitzi把带去的面包掰开分给同车躲雨的人，自己站在车尾，湿透的裤脚贴着腿，一路打着哆嗦；远足没走完，倒记住了一车陌生人的谢意。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (改乘电车回家的车票); food; health -0.030000000000000027 (淋雨后穿着湿衣服乘车); mood -0.020000000000000018; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-145; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-145_

  Mitzi把圣马克斯殡葬合作社的名单藏进缝纫机抽屉的夹层。名单上有二十几个名字，其中两个已经搬走，一个被工厂开除。Mitzi没有把最后一行划掉。
  _effects: flag +movement_list_holder; reputation 0.030000000000000027_

  户籍官Grete Moser 2为了结掉一桩陈年失踪案，在档案上给Mitzi盖了“推定死亡”。三天后，Mitzi收到一张慰问卡和一本作废的配给簿；同一天，追了半年的债主忽然不再上门。旧货商听说后，花两克朗买走一份抄本，说要给一个躲债的朋友看看格式。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖给旧货商的死亡证明抄本); state_add bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:962; reputation -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; flag +declared_dead_while_alive; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-174; flag +bureaucratic_ghost; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: cash 2 (卖出半锅扁豆汤); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

  养老金办公室寄来一枚“忠诚遗属”铜章和三克朗，说这是对死者家属的最后抚恤。Mitzi不能亲自去领，只好让Katharina Bauer代收；邻居把铜章别在自家外套上，回来时只字不提三克朗。Mitzi没有追问，因为那枚铜章若挂在自己胸前，实在不知道该怎么解释。

### 194 subject events

- **1917.09** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  楼梯间的灯罩空着，第三级踏板比别处高。Josef一脚踩空，滚到平台，右小臂磕在铁扶手的弯头上。Josef Bauer听见哭声跑出来，把Josef抱回屋，用冷水毛巾敷那块青紫，再用旧布条缠了两圈。夜里Josef哭醒两次。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Elisabeth Huber把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Josef伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Elisabeth Huber从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Josef手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Josef一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

  河水那边传来同伴的笑声，Josef站在院门口听了听，还是回去把木盆扶正。Alois Bauer睡醒哭了一声，Josef把最后半块面包掰碎泡进温水，一勺一勺喂。Josef Bauer回来时，Josef正蹲在盆边打瞌睡，手里还攥着喂饭的木勺。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000004; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000275:child; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1919.10** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street, injury
  晒衣绳上的水一滴一滴落在石板上。Josef跑到第二圈，鞋底一滑，膝盖先着地。Berta Weiss从屋里端出半盆清水，蹲下冲掉伤口里的沙，又用手帕扎紧，说今天别跑了。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (膝盖在湿石板上磕破); relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss_

- **1919.11** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-ok` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Josef在土豆摊前蹲了很久，挑了一磅没有芽眼的。付完钱，手里只剩几个铜板，又去买了盐。围裙兜太浅，Josef用下巴压住布边，走两步就低头看一眼。到家Josef Bauer把土豆倒进盆里，盐包放在灶台边。
  _effects: food; cash -0.15 (市场花销); relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

  Josef在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

  Josef的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.music-lesson::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/broken-string` tags: education, music, lesson, craft, culture, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef换把时用力过猛，弦轴崩了，琴弦抽在手背上。Luise Stein没有责备，只让Josef先学三个月空弦。Josef每天对着镜子拉同一个音，拉到最后，连邻居都不再敲墙，只是经过门口时会放慢脚步。第二天Josef照常来，手上缠着布。弦上还有血。
  _effects: cash -2 (借来的小提琴); health -0.020000000000000018 (借来的小提琴); skill_

  介绍所的柜台前还有几个人在等，Gustav Berg把一张印好的通知推过来，用铅笔在“星期三”下面画了一道。他把纸条折成四折，塞进上衣内袋。
  _effects: flag +seeking_work; knowledge “下星期三早上七点，另一家机器厂在职业介绍所登记招上油工”; mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

- **1931.07** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.sick_child_doorstep/helps_fetch_doctor` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  隔壁Maria Winter的小儿子烧到说胡话，额头烫得能烘手。Maria Winter在楼道里抹眼泪，说请不起医生。Josef下楼叫了街角药房的伙计，花四十赫勒买了一包退烧药粉，又帮着用湿布敷了一整夜。天亮时孩子退了烧，Maria Winter端来一碗热汤面，面底下卧着一个鸡蛋。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (买退烧药粉); health -0.020000000000000018 (熬夜照料病人); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1931.11** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.share` tags: home, market, food, social
  Josef在家把一锅扁豆汤分出一半，让Rosa端回去给生病的老母亲。Rosa第二天还回一只空锅，锅底放着一小块猪油。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1932.01** `vienna-family-divorce/partner-moves-to-shared-housing` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation, household
  Josef Kral把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Josef留在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Josef在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods（其中4克朗记入债务）); role_

- **1932.01** `r13-civ.clinic-first-visit::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/dismissed-as-nerves` tags: health, clinic, queue, diagnosis, institution, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef把夜里咳醒的次数写在一张包装纸上，Alois Berg扫了一眼，说是心事太重，给了几包安神粉。走廊尽头的护士压低声音，说住院部还有空床，但要医生签字。Josef把包装纸折回去，没再问。
  _effects: cash -1 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +clinic_dismissed_as_nerves; knowledge “the clinic doctor refused a hospital referral”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

- **1932.03** `af17-luck.wallet-in-the-snow::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/kept-the-wallet` tags: luck, money, street, chance, irony, daily, reform, medical
  Josef把钱包塞进内袋，走了两条街才敢回头看。三克朗够买一周的煤，彩票也许什么都不是；但那天晚上，Josef把彩票压在盐罐底下，像压着一张还没有兑现的明天。
  _effects: cash 3 (the money in the lost wallet); state_add af17-lucky-idiot state:world-vienna-1912:249; flag +kept_the_snow_wallet; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +lucky_idiot; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1932.06** `vienna-family-courtship/wait-for-a-better-year` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Josef把话咽回去，只说等明年工钱稳一点再谈。Luise Stein没有追问，两人把桌上的面包分完，各自去上工；出门时Josef回头看了一眼，Luise Stein正把掉在桌上的面包屑拢进掌心，谁也没有把那个话头重新拾起来。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1932.06** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.food-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef只收下篮里的面包和豆子，把煤袋推回Emil Weiss手里，说房租自己再想办法，请她们给介绍点零工。Emil Weiss把煤放在走廊窗台，说礼拜四后院有人要拆旧木箱，一赫勒一捆，工钱不多但现结。Josef记下门牌，答应一早过去。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

- **1933.01** `bc18-luck.civic-mascot::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/mascot-union` tags: luck, charity, public, reputation, absurd, social, suspicion, reform
  城里几个自称运气好的人凑在一起，成立了一个“幸运者互助会”，每人每月交二十赫勒，谁遇到倒霉事就领一笔小钱。Josef被推举为会长，因为Josef的运气最没有道理；第一次开会，七个人带来七种护身符，最后决定把会费锁在一只旧饼干盒里。
  _effects: cash 2 (幸运者互助会的会费结余); state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 25 -> 20; role; relation colleague with person-rosa-lechner; skill; flag +founded_lucky_peoples_club; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1933.01** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.pawnshop/pawn-accepted-watch` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, pawn, cash
  怀表是黄铜壳，走起来慢半刻，伙计照样收了。七克朗换成纸钞加几枚镍币，Josef数了两遍。当票上印着三个月期限，字小得像蚂蚁，折起来正好塞进表袋原来的位置。
  _effects: cash 7 (当掉旧怀表所得); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:af17-lucky-idiot/luck-year/the-bill-comes-due` tags: af17-lucky-idiot, luck, fortune, windfall, suspicion, social, coincidence, absurd
  好运气没有出现。Josef错过电车，鞋跟断在环城大道的石缝里，回家才发现钥匙也丢了。邻居没有安慰，只说“总算轮到你了”，语气像在结一笔旧账。
  _effects: state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.streak 12 -> 11; state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 20 -> 30; cash -1 (修鞋和配钥匙); health -0.020000000000000018 (在冻雨里走回家); mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Josef把欠账按日期抄在纸上，先还利息最高的一笔。Ferdinand Bauer翻开米铺账本，把红字划掉一行，写“收到八克朗”。为了凑这笔钱，Josef把家里一只旧表送进当铺；Rosa知道后，把自家的赊账往后挪了一个月。
  _effects: cash -8 (偿还米铺欠账); role; flag +debt_reduced; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “米铺把赊账利息记在红字栏，先还红字最多的那笔最划算。”_

- **1933.04** `r15-ordinary.quiet-year::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/neighborhood-help` tags: ordinary, daily, family, leisure, peace, reform, medical, schooling
  隔壁家的男人病了一个月，Josef每天替他们提水。没有人提报酬。后来那家人搬走时，把一把钥匙留给Josef，说以后路过可以进来坐。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +neighborhood_help_

- **1933.04** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.nod` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Josef在窗边听了一刻钟，只听出是工钱和面包账的事，最后把窗虚掩上睡了。第二天清早在楼梯口碰见那个女人，眼睛有点肿，两人错身时她点了点头，谁也没说话。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +knows_downstairs_quarrel_

- **1934.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Josef在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Josef把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005523_

- **1934.01** `af17-luck.quiet-windfall::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-wallet-was-a-plant` tags: luck, windfall, daily, quiet, money, social, coincidence, vienna
  钱包刚放进内袋，一个戴圆顶礼帽的男人就抓住Josef的手腕，说Josef偷了他的钱。围观的人越多，男人说得越响；直到街角的巡警认出他上个月在同一位置演过同一出戏。Josef拿回自己的围巾，丢了一克朗的车钱，也学会了先看衣领再看钱包。
  _effects: cash -1 (被缠住后错过的车钱); state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 30 -> 35; reputation -0.01999999999999999; skill; condition; flag +wallet_was_bait; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1934.01** `state_tick:af17-lucky-idiot/luck-year/small-mercies` tags: af17-lucky-idiot, luck, fortune, windfall, suspicion, social, coincidence, absurd
  又一年过去，Josef在煤气灯下捡到一枚银币，又在当铺门口遇见一个认错人的老主顾。好事都不大，却一件接一件；街角咖啡馆里的人开始说，Josef一定知道什么别人不知道的门路。
  _effects: state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.streak 11 -> 12; state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 35 -> 41; cash 1 (捡到的银币); mood 0.020000000000000018_

  咖啡馆里有人问Josef，是不是替哪个议员跑过腿，或者认识一个会看牌的女人。Josef说自己只是运气好；这句话说出口，连Josef自己都觉得像在遮掩。
  _effects: state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 41 -> 53; reputation -0.01999999999999999; flag +luck_explained_as_a_deal; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  星期天下午窗边三把椅子，介绍只用了两句，随后就任两人自己聊。老友讲印厂的油墨味，缝纫女工讲机针怎么断。有人只管叫侍者续咖啡，三杯一共九十赫勒，坐在旁边听了一下午。
  _effects: cash -0.9 (三杯咖啡和给侍者的小费); relation friend with person-marie-huber; skill; mood 0.050000000000000044_

  两个人拧毯子的时候，Josef Berg 2在旁边递肥皂水，说你们俩力气倒合得上。毯子抬上绳子时还在滴水，Josef甩了甩手，看见自己掌纹里嵌着一道一道的布纹。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

- **1934.04** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.sell` tags: home, market, food, social
  鞋匠敲后门问有没有热汤，Josef把剩下的半锅倒进他的罐子里，收下两个十字币。Rosa Berg提着空碗上来时，只看见Josef把硬币塞进围裙口袋。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖出半锅扁豆汤); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000127:child_

- **1934.07** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  Josef在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Otto Winter从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: health -0.05 (左手背烫伤); relation friend with person-otto-winter_

  Josef把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Otto Winter抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Josef把床单对折，腾出一半，Otto Winter用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005816_

  _effects: cash 3 (义卖吉祥物的酬劳和分到的火腿); reputation 0.03; state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.streak 12 -> 12; state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 53 -> 61; relation acquaintance with person-ferdinand-winter; flag +civic_mascot; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  怀表是母亲陪嫁，表盖上有道划痕。Josef用指甲把表盖擦亮，说这个就够。兄弟把欠杂货店的账认下来，十一克朗。两人下楼时兄弟走在前面，到门口停了一下，说下个月一起去。
  _effects: relation kin with person-anna-bauer; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1935.02** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.help-in` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family

  _effects: cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; flag +parish_sick_visitor_

- **1935.10** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Alois Winter 2说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Alois Winter 2陪着Josef去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱（其中2.6克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; mood -0.09999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

- **1936.12** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-declined` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef正要把空床许给Anna Huber 2，Friedrich Stein却在楼梯上拦住了，说这条街上去年有人招了生人合住，失过钥匙，惹出些麻烦。Josef回家把铺盖重新叠好，回绝了Anna Huber 2，只让他白天来喝口热水。空床仍空着，房租还是从自己的工钱里补。

  四十七个弗朗茨·穆勒里，只有Josef拿得出干净的房租簿。公证人Gustav Kral把遗嘱念完，遗产是一辆灵车、两匹老马和十四克朗的欠账。Josef没有钱下葬，先把灵车租给搬家的人；夜里跑环城大道，白天替人运钢琴。Katharina Kral起初等着看笑话，后来开始给他介绍生意。
  _effects: cash 5 (用灵车替人搬家的收入); role; state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:702; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20; flag +hearse_became_a_moving_van; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.01999999999999999; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: cash 6 (三次模特酬劳); state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 25 -> 31; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.legitimacy 20 -> 32; reputation 0.03; role; flag +model_for_unknown_citizen; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  又一年过去，Josef仍在被介绍成那个人物。邀请函从市郊的园艺会、女子学校和退伍军人酒馆寄来；Josef学会在签名后加一句模糊的谦辞，听起来像有许多往事不便细说。每一次应付都带来一点小钱，也让真正的解释更不合时宜。

  办公室有人问Josef，为什么总有一个女人在楼下等她。Josef说那是表妹；对方笑了一下，说现在的表妹真体贴。下午，Josef把桌上的相框扣倒，又在没人时翻回来，用袖子擦了擦玻璃上的灰。

  Josef说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Marie Huber没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Josef听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱)_

  马车夫刹不住车，Josef被挤在车轮和路沿之间。Elisabeth Kral 2跑去叫警察，回来时人已经不动了；事故报告上写着“行人横穿”，赔偿金刚好够一副棺材。棺材铺的人来量尺寸时，街上的雪还没扫。车夫在一边搓着手，没说话。
  _effects: death (crushed chest in a street collision); cash 12 (small compensation assessed as pedestrian fault); flag -something_is_missing; flag -impossible_attachment_active; flag -protected_scandal_active_

  _effects: cash -2 (cloth, fuel and rice for a sick child); health -0.06000000000000005 (fever left the child weak); health -0.030000000000000027 (night nursing and lost sleep); condition; relation kin with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-neighbor-speaks` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  “记我名下。”Emil Weiss说完，掌柜才把秤砣挪开。账本上多出两赫勒的利息，煤筐却只装了半下。Rosa跟着Emil Weiss走出门，Emil Weiss低声嘱咐明天先把钱送来，别让掌柜再找到话头。
  _effects: cash -0.02 (赊账添的两赫勒利息); food; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

- **1920.05** `r2-rural.child-farm-labor::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/sent-to-pick-grapes` tags: rural, child_labour, harvest, school, injury, agriculture, small_town, peace
  Alois Lechner把一只小篮子挂在Rosa脖子上，说捡满一篮就回家。葡萄行里的刺划破手指，Rosa用嘴吸了吸，继续捡。Franz Berg过秤时多给了半个铜板，说这孩子手快。
  _effects: cash 1 (八岁的手和镰刀); health -0.050000000000000044 (八岁的手和镰刀); flag +child_harvest_labor; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

- **1921.01** `vienna-life-child-labour/runs-errands` tags: childhood, labour, piecework, injury
  Rosa放学后替Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的排字工送校样，穿过两条街，拿到一克朗。回家时天已经黑了，Rosa Lechner把饭留在锅里；Rosa的鞋底磨薄，脚后跟起了泡，却把那一克朗捏得平平整整，放进Rosa Lechner的围裙口袋；这跑腿的差事从此固定下来，Rosa也算家里一个能挣钱的孩子。
  _effects: cash 1 (running errands); health -0.010000000000000009 (walking in the cold); role_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Rosa后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Rosa没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Rosa用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.029999999999999992; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1921.06** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street, injury
  晒衣绳上的水一滴一滴落在石板上。Rosa跑到第二圈，鞋底一滑，膝盖先着地。Emil Weiss从屋里端出半盆清水，蹲下冲掉伤口里的沙，又用手帕扎紧，说今天别跑了。
  _effects: health -0.03999999999999998 (膝盖在湿石板上磕破); relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

- **1921.11** `iw18-secret.cellar-door::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-door-was-just-coal` tags: childhood, secret, family, cellar, knowledge, fear, rural, agriculture
  地窖里只有煤、旧箱子和一只缺腿的凳子，Rosa转了一圈，什么秘密也没找到。上楼时Rosa Lechner正在切洋葱，问孩子下去做什么；Rosa说找坛子，Rosa Lechner便让孩子把手洗了来帮忙，案板上的洋葱辣得两人都流泪。
  _effects: mood 0.010000000000000002; food; flag +cellar_was_only_coal_

- **1922.01** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-bullied` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  市场拱廊下晒不到太阳，Rosa却被堵出一头汗。带子越挣越紧，肩膀在柱子上磕了一下。Josef从人群里挤过来，先看了看Rosa的肩，又看了看柱子上的结，拿出小刀割断。两个大孩子骂了一句，Josef把Rosa拉到菜篮后面。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.05000000000000002; condition_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.school-slate/slate-homemade-wood-ash` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, supplies, education
  湿煤灰写出第一笔时，Rosa还挺高兴，写到第三行，手一蹭就全花了。Rosa Lechner看见了，把自己的旧手帕撕下一角，蘸水给木板擦了底。第二天在Leopoldstädter Volksschule门口，同学指着那块发黑的木板问是什么，Rosa说是石板，对方笑得弯下腰。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.04000000000000001; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.stay-after-school/stay-list-instead` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, teacher, learning
  锁门的人在走廊里咳嗽了一声。Elisabeth Huber一边道歉一边收拾，最后从粉笔盒底下抽出一张旧练习纸，圈了四道题。Rosa接过来，想问第二题怎么开头，Elisabeth Huber已经提着包下楼了。校门口还有太阳，Rosa把纸摊在台阶上看了一会儿。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1924.01** `task-vienna-youth-apprenticeship-search/trial-promised` tags: task, task:work, work, adolescence, apprenticeship
  Rosa在印刷厂门口等到工头出来，把姓名、住址和会写的字报了一遍。Anna Berg让他下周一早来试半天，先不给工钱，只管一顿午饭。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; flag +apprenticeship_trial_promised; mood 0.05000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

- **1924.08** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/coins-into-box` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Mitzi Lechner的箱子摊在床板上，里面是两件衬衫、一双旧靴和母亲塞的一小袋面粉。Rosa把攒了半年的零钱数到第三遍，一共一克朗二十赫勒，全塞进箱角的袜子里。Mitzi Lechner看见了，没说话，只把箱子扣上，铜扣响了一声。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (把攒下的零钱塞给离家的兄姐（其中1.2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.03_

- **1925.01** `statistical_infection`
  Rosa在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1925.01** `task-vienna-poor-apprentice.foreman-introduction/relative-introduces-trial-half-day` tags: adolescence, apprentice, work, education, poverty, printshop, task, task:work
  亲戚把他带到工头面前，工头让他下星期来试半天，先看裁纸和搬版。母亲熨平的衬衫领子还是硬得硌脖子，他站在厂门口把姓名说了两遍。
  _effects: flag +half_day_trial_at_big_printshop; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; mood 0.05000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

- **1925.02** `statistical_recovery`
  Rosa从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1925.08** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sell-the-coat` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  巷口的风把外套下摆吹起来。旧货贩子闻了闻领子，说给两克朗。Rosa把钱卷进袜筒，回家前在楼梯口站了一会儿。Alois Lechner发现衣钩空了，问了三遍，Rosa都说没看见，脚趾在鞋里把硬币越踩越紧。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉哥哥旧外套); relation kin with person-alois-lechner; mood -0.04000000000000001_

- **1926.01** `vienna-life-apprenticeship/kept-at-home` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship
  家里算了一笔账：学徒工钱扣掉车费和饭钱，剩不下多少。Rosa留在家里接缝纫店的零活，顺便照看弟弟妹妹。
  _effects: role; cash 2 (home piecework)_

- **1926.01** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/signed-contract` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa Stein把学徒契约摊在桌上，指给Rosa看期限、工钱和出师考试。Rosa签了名，第二天站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: relation employer with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:91; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; education apprenticeship; role_

  演示到骨折固定时，Maria Kral把夹板按在Rosa的腿上，Rosa忽然眼前发白，从凳子上滑下去。醒来时，Josef正拿湿毛巾拍Rosa的额头，教室里有人在笑。
  _effects: mood -0.06; flag +fainted_during_first_aid_demo; relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral_

- **1926.04** `yf18-runaway.bundle::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/hide-the-bundle` tags: runaway, escape, family, plan, night_train, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa把两件衬衫、一双袜子和小时候的洗礼证明卷进一块旧桌布，塞到床底下。Rosa Lechner在厨房里数硬币，数了三遍都没有抬头；Rosa躺在黑暗里，听见夜车从远处经过，铁轨的声音一直传到枕头下面。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:92; flag +yf18_bundle_hidden; mood 0.01999999999999999; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1926.04** `r13-turn.exam-failure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-open-secondary/name-missing` tags: education, exam, failure, school, work, reform, medical, schooling
  补考那天，Rosa在考场门口等到钟响，监考说名单上没有名字。Anna Berg跑去教务处，回来时手里只有一张转学建议。Rosa去了印刷厂当学徒，师傅说铅字比字母好认，至少不会骗人。
  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; education apprenticeship_

- **1927.01** `yf18-apprentice.press-guard::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/refuse-the-machine` tags: apprenticeship, work, danger, machine, injury, refusal, reform, medical
  教堂年历的订单催得紧，第二区夜班出租合作社把压印机的护板卸了，说这样进纸快一半。Rosa看着那块铁板，说手会进去；第二区夜班出租合作社把工钱袋往桌上一扔，问Rosa是不是不想出师了。Rosa没有碰机器，那天下午的活由Anton Lechner接手。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 10 -> 5; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 0 -> 1; flag +yf18_refused_unguarded_press; mood 0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/learned` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Rosa又学会了一道工序。师傅没有夸，只把下一块料推到面前。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 5 -> 25; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1927.01** `state_expire:yf18-runaway-plan` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  Rosa的「床底下的包袱」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:92; flag -yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1927.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.teacher-recommendation/night-school-first` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:education
  Grete Stein把练习本推回来，说排字排得再齐，簿记和德语不过关，印刷厂也不会收。Grete Stein在一张纸上写下夜校的街名，让Rosa先去问学费。Rosa当天傍晚就拐到六区，站在门口把课表抄了一遍，第二天把一克朗报名费交进窗口。
  _effects: education apprenticeship; cash -1 (夜校一学期报名费和课本钱); flag +enrolled_night_school; skill_

- **1927.06** `r14-edu.leaving-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sent-to-country` tags: education, school_dropout, child_labour, poverty, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Rosa没有进厂，也没有留在家里，而是搭了去乡下的火车。姨妈家在葡萄园边上，缺人摘果子。Rosa把课本塞进箱底，白天干活，晚上在油灯下认几个字。秋天寄回家的信里，Rosa说这里的星星比城里多。车票两克朗，是姨妈先垫的。
  _effects: cash -2 (书包挂在门后的钉子上（其中2克朗记入债务）); flag +left_school_for_country; move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house_

- **1927.08** `r2-leisure.servant-trial::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/rejected-at-the-back-door` tags: domestic_service, servant, hiring, household, reference, reform, medical, schooling
  Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien把介绍信折回信封，说上一家太太的名字她没听过。Grete Stein领Rosa从后门出去，塞给她一块冷面包，说下回别把指甲修得这样尖。Rosa在电车站把面包吃完，手指上还留着擦铜油的味道。
  _effects: mood -0.10000000000000003; flag +rejected_servant_trial; relation colleague with person-grete-stein; knowledge “an unfamiliar reference can end a trial before lunch”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1928.01** `yf18-apprentice.press-guard::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/tie-the-sleeve-and-work` tags: apprenticeship, work, danger, machine, injury, refusal, rural, agriculture
  Rosa把袖口卷到肘上，用麻绳扎紧，替Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien把一叠纸送进压印机。前两百张很顺，第二百零一张时，纸角卡住，Rosa伸手去拨，机器没有停；等Helene Stein拉开Rosa，食指的指甲已经翻了起来。
  _effects: condition; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 25 -> 17; cash -2 (a doctor and a bandage); flag +yf18_press_injury; mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 1 -> 2; cash -1 (wasted material); mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1928.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.ask-a-mentor/weekly-lesson` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Emil Lechner把老花镜往鼻梁上推了推，让Rosa用食指在字盘空位上比画。咖啡馆伙计来续水时，老人把字母表折起来，说下星期带旧字样本来看。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; flag +typesetting_mentor; mood 0.06_

- **1928.12** `vienna-adolescence.stolen-roll/caught-and-returned` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  面包房后门的筐里堆着隔夜面包。Rosa拿起半个黑面包，刚要往怀里塞，Alois Lechner从晾衣绳后面绕出来，手一伸，掌心朝上。Rosa把面包放回去，Alois Lechner用指节在Rosa手背上敲了两下，声音不大，巷口的人都听见了。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; mood -0.10000000000000003_

  _effects: state_transition apprenticeship-contract -> failed-apprentice; flag +failed_apprenticeship; mood -0.07999999999999996; role_

  Anna Berg让Rosa放学后送两趟货，按趟给钱，天黑前必须回铺子交账。Rosa把书包挂在柜台后面，提着篮子穿过三条街；第一趟送错了一户，第二趟跑得鞋底发热。收工时Anna Berg数了三个铜板，说明天还来。
  _effects: cash 2 (放学后的四个钟头); flag +work13_took_part_time_errands; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; employment Laufbursche @ clinic:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11-evt:world-vienna-1912:001925_

- **1929.04** `yf18-runaway.cousin-letter::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/show-the-letter-to-parent` tags: runaway, letter, cousin, work, plan, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa把信放在晚饭桌上。Rosa Lechner读完，问格拉茨远不远，又说家里的房租下个月要涨；那晚谁也没有说行或不行，信被压在糖罐下面，第二天边角沾了咖啡。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:158; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner; flag +yf18_family_knows_letter; mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1929.04** `r2-faith.parish-school-fee::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/expelled-for-arrears` tags: faith, school, charity, education, fees, reform, medical, schooling
  欠了兩個月学费，Helene Berg把Rosa的座位调到最后一排，说书本先留下。Rosa Lechner在门口拦住Rosa，说学校不是救济院。Rosa回家没有说，第二天照常出门，在火车站坐到放学时间。Alois Lechner后来在书包里发现了一张空白的退学单。
  _effects: mood -0.14; condition; flag +expelled_from_parish_school_for_arrears; relation rival with person-rosa-lechner; knowledge “two months of unpaid fees can cost a child his seat”_

  Rosa说Anton把答案抄在墨水瓶底，还答应分给自己一半。Rosa Kral 2把Anton的座位调到第一排；第二天，Rosa的课桌里多了一张纸条，上面只有一行字：“你以后别走夜路。”Rosa把纸条交给Rosa Kral 2，事情从此不再是两个人的事。
  _effects: reputation 0.020000000000000004; flag +yf18_named_friend; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

  Rosa在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1929.09** `r3-teen.first-love::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/rejected` tags: love, courtship, school, tram, secrecy, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa把叠成方块的纸条塞给Anton，第二天在课桌里找回同一张纸，边角压平了，字没有被打开过。放学时Anton从另一边走了，Rosa坐在电车上，一直坐到终点站。
  _effects: mood -0.16000000000000003; flag +first_love_rejected; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912_

- **1929.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`

- **1929.11** `statistical_recovery`
  Rosa从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1930.01** `yf18-runaway.north-station::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/turn-back-at-the-barrier` tags: runaway, station, train, papers, escape, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Rosa已经走到检票口，听见身后有人喊自己的小名。回头看见Alois Lechner提着一只保温壶，站在卖报纸的摊子旁边。Rosa把票递过去又收回来，最后陪她坐在长椅上喝完那壶汤；火车开走时，两个人都没有看站台。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 35 -> 15; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 0 -> 10; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1930.01** `state_reaction:yf18-runaway-plan/a-family-question` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  饭桌上又为房租吵起来。Rosa把面包掰成很小的块，听见父亲说“这个家养不起闲人”；Rosa没有回嘴，只在心里把格拉茨的地址又背了一遍。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 10 -> 18; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 15 -> 13_

  Rosa把包袱重新塞回床底，又在上面压了一双冬鞋。母亲进来换床单时，Rosa站在门口，假装在看墙上的圣像；床单抖开的声音像一列很慢的火车，Rosa数着它经过的每一节车厢。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.preparation 20 -> 24; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 18 -> 24; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  Rosa站在书架前抽出一本厚小说，就着门口的光读了半章，Elisabeth Huber在柜台后抬起眼皮看了一眼，没说话。读到要紧处，Rosa摸出铅笔头把页码记在车票背面，把书插回原处，说下次带够押金再来；白读半章，管理员的眼神却凉了一点。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1930.05** `vienna-market-household.market-credit/market-credit.default` tags: home, market, food, social
  玛丽亚在市场拱门下拦住Rosa，声音不大但周围人都听得见：三个克朗。Rosa翻遍口袋只摸出几个十字币。玛丽亚把布袋从Rosa胳膊上解下来，转身走了。
  _effects: food; reputation -0.15000000000000002; mood -0.09999999999999998_

- **1930.11** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.evicted-neighbours/evicted-neighbours.district-office-queue` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  区公所的长凳坐满了人。Maria Kral的笔尖在名册上划了一道，把纸推过来时没有抬眼。Rosa把纸折了三折，塞进Rosa Lechner手里。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-rosa-lechner; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1930.11** `oj18-lift.stuck::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/hand-crank` tags: lift, building, work, night, passenger, odd_job, suspense, rural
  电梯停在四楼半，门缝里只看见一截电线。Rosa让Rosa Huber抓紧扶手，自己摇动手柄，一格一格把轿厢放下来。门开时，Rosa Huber的白大褂后背全湿了；他塞给Rosa两张钞票，说手术室里还有人等着。
  _effects: organization_found 环城大道写字楼管理处 (other); position 电梯员 @building:person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:004053; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:193; cash 2 (the doctor's reward); reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; flag +lift_operator; knowledge “电梯停在四楼半时，手动柄要一格一格放，不能一次松到底。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1931.01** `state_expire:yf18-runaway-plan` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  Rosa的「床底下的包袱」到期；相关安排结束。

- **1931.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/trusted-with-the-keys` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  两年过去，Rosa已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Rosa也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 40; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 10 -> 20; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 24; cash 2 (night-shift wages); skill_

- **1931.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Rosa走到Arbeiter-Zeitung im Vorwärts-Haus门口，看见便衣和拥挤的人群，最终没有进去。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1931.05** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.borrow-fire` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Rosa端着一只旧锅去敲Emil Weiss的门，想借几块烧红的炭。Emil Weiss让她从自家炉膛里夹了一块，又抓了一小把引火的木片。Rosa连声道谢，回来把炭放进炉栅，火是有了，屋里却还是冷得能呵出白气。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; food; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1931.12** `r1-hc.home-fever::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/deaf-ear` tags: health, fever, home, care, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  烧退下去以后，Rosa发现左耳像塞了一团棉花。Josef Lechner在右边说话，Rosa要把头转过去才听得见。医生说是炎症走了耳道，没药可治，只开了一瓶橄榄油，让每天滴两滴。
  _effects: cash -4 (水银停在四十度); condition; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58; knowledge “a fever left the left ear deaf and the doctor offered only o”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58_

- **1931.12** `vienna-history-schnitzler-theatre/speak-in-the-foyer` tags: history, culture, arthur_schnitzler, burgtheater, theatre
  Rosa 在门廊里拦住Arthur Schnitzler，说第二幕的医生讲得太像真的。Arthur Schnitzler 停了一下，回答说他当过医生，知道那种语气；然后礼貌地告辞，没有留下地址。Rosa 看着他走进雨里，才想起自己忘了问下一部戏什么时候上演。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-arthur-schnitzler; knowledge “Schnitzler 当过医生，他的剧作里医生和官员的说话方式来自医院与部委的经验，而不是单纯的舞台效果。”; cash -3 (theatre ticket); reputation 0.010000000000000009_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-depression/short-time` tags: adulthood, depression, work
  Rosa改成每周上三天班，工钱按日结算。家里的面包从每天一条变成隔天一条，Rosa开始把午饭省下来带回家。
  _effects: cash -3 (short-time work); condition_

- **1932.01** `r13-civ.enter-hospital::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/admitted-third-class` tags: health, hospital, admission, deposit, institution, reform, medical, schooling
  Gustav Kral把押金收据和住院单钉在一起，让Rosa去三楼东侧等床。走廊里全是折叠床，Elisabeth Huber给Rosa一条洗得发硬的毯子，说今晚先别喝水。Rosa把鞋塞在枕头下面，听着隔壁的呻吟睡了过去。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院处窗口的押金); health 0.05999999999999999 (住院处窗口的押金); condition; flag +hospitalized_third_class; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

- **1932.03** `major-education-night-technical-school/night-school-certificate` tags: education, night_school, work, exhaustion, major
  夜校在第七区的旧校舍里，十二个工人挤在一张长桌前。Elisabeth Huber用粉笔画出齿轮的剖面，又让大家传看一只烧坏的保险盒。Rosa把图纸带回家，在厨房桌上描到半夜。结业考试通过后，Rosa拿到一张机械制图的证书，学费一共九克朗。
  _effects: education secondary; skill; cash -9 (evening-school fees); role; flag +night_school_certificate; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1932.10** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.enroll-first` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  来问入会的是个瘦高的年轻人，帽檐捏在手里，说自己在第十六区的织布厂做了半年。Rosa把名册翻到空白页，蘸水笔尖在“住址”那栏停了一下，等他报出街名。老成员在角落里把账本合上，声音很轻。
  _effects: cash 1 (新会员的入会费先记在桌上); relation acquaintance with person-helene-winter; skill_

- **1932.11** `vienna-historical-social-movement.public-meeting/speak-with-rent-numbers` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Rosa没有喊口号，只问在场谁家的煤还够烧三天。十几只手举起来，连Hermann Bauer也举了。书记说这些数字比演说有用，散会时几个人围上来问自己那条街怎么算。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

- **1933.01** `oj18-lift.night::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/refused-ninth-floor` tags: lift, building, night, secrecy, office, work, suspense, reform
  Rosa说九层的按钮坏了，只能停在八层。Gustav Berg看了Rosa一眼，自己爬楼梯上去；第二天，管理处把Rosa的班次调到了地下室的货梯。Alois Winter 2在楼梯间悄悄说，上一个不肯去九层的电梯员，三个月后就辞职了。
  _effects: relation rival with person-gustav-berg; reputation 0.020000000000000004; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 60 -> 65; cash -1 (was moved to the freight lift); knowledge “九层的按钮没有坏，但上一个肯去九层的电梯员三个月后辞职了。”; flag +refused_ninth_floor; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

- **1933.01** `ml18-inheritance.uncles-press::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/refuse-and-let-the-cousin` tags: inheritance, family, business, debt, legal, money, reform, medical
  Rosa在公证处坐了很久，最后说自己没有力气再经营一间铺子。Elisabeth Bauer接过钥匙，脸上先是惊讶，随后是一种被托付的沉重。半年后，那间印刷所改了招牌，Elisabeth Bauer在门口看见Rosa，只点了点头，没有提请客吃饭的事。
  _effects: cash -1 (the notary's fee for refusing the inheritance); relation rival with person-elisabeth-bauer; flag +refused_print_shop; knowledge “叔父的印刷所欠着十四克朗，订阅名单是唯一还值钱的东西。”; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Rosa的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Rosa想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 37 -> 21; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 65 -> 55; mood -0.04999999999999993; flag +odd_job_scare_

  夜班结束前，Rosa听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 20 -> 28; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 40 -> 37; mood -0.030000000000000027; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_

- **1933.08** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Rosa把通知念给Maria Weiss听，Maria Weiss摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Rosa去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

- **1933.09** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Lena在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Lena敲开Rosa的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1934.01** `r14-edu.night-class::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/learned-to-read` tags: education, adult_learning, literacy, night_school, vienna, urban, crisis, war
  Rosa下班后不回家，直接去夜校。教室由一间酒馆后屋改成，桌上还留着酒渍。Luise Stein从字母表教起，Rosa把每个字母念三遍，喉咙发干。三个月后，Rosa第一次独立读完一张报纸上的短讯，把那张报纸叠好，放进工具箱最底层。学费一克朗，Rosa用加班钱付的。
  _effects: cash -1 (夜校的第二排长凳); flag +adult_literacy; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002593_

- **1934.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-female/private-household` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, housing, lifecycle, partnership, household
  Rosa和Elisabeth Bauer合租一间房，对外说是表姐妹。两人共用一只炉子、一本账和一张床，白天各自上工，晚上把门闩好；房租从两人的工钱里各出一半，日子紧，却比各自的旧住处暖，窗台上还并排晾着两只杯子。
  _effects: relation partner with person-elisabeth-bauer; household; mood 0.06000000000000005_

- **1934.01** `r3-mig.train-compartment::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::war-troop-trains/same-district-stranger` tags: migration, train, travel, stranger, borders, vienna, urban, crisis
  Rosa在包厢里啃干面包，Hedwig问是不是从河东来的。三句话之后，两个人认出了同一个集市。Hedwig把地址写在烟盒纸上，说到了那边可以找他舅舅。Gustav Kral进来查票，看见两个人挤在一个座位上，什么也没说。
  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +met_stranger_from_same_district; relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the stranger’s uncle lives near the destination”_

- **1934.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Rosa去了Arbeiter-Zeitung im Vorwärts-Haus，坐在后排听完了关于维也纳社会民主党在工人街区、工会和报刊网络中持续活动，争取普选、劳动保护与社会救济的争论；散会时带走一张油印提纲。
  _effects: historical development:seed-vienna-social-democracy-1912 as participant; knowledge “我参加了维也纳社会民主党在工人街区、工会和报刊网络中持续活动，争取普选、劳动保护与社会救济。。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1934.07** `vienna-family-divorce/reconcile-for-now` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, partnership
  Rosa和Elisabeth Bauer在楼道里吵到半夜，最后因为下个月的房租又坐回同一张桌。谁都没有提和好，只把账本重新摊开；Elisabeth Bauer把煤钱推过来，Rosa接过去数了两遍，手指碰到一起又分开，屋里的灯亮到很晚。
  _effects: relation partner with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1934.12** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.borrow-fire` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Rosa端着一只旧锅去敲Emil Winter的门，想借几块烧红的炭。Emil Winter让她从自家炉膛里夹了一块，又抓了一小把引火的木片。Rosa连声道谢，回来把炭放进炉栅，火是有了，屋里却还是冷得能呵出白气。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; food; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

- **1935.01** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/keys-and-photo-returned` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, separation
  院子里晾着三条床单，风一过就拍到人脸上。Rosa把备用钥匙和一张照相馆拍的合影交还，Elisabeth Bauer把那件借来的外套叠好递回。两人说好各找各的住处，谁都没有提高声音；分手分得干净，可两个人心里都空了一块。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood -0.08999999999999997; flag +courtship_ended_cleanly_

  Rosa原定在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg开会，但来的人不够，或者有人临时退缩；最后只留下几张没发完的纸条。
  _effects: mood -0.02000000000000013_

- **1935.08** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Rosa在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Ferdinand Winter却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Rosa把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-ferdinand-winter; flag +still_looking_for_room_

- **1935.10** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Rosa在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Hermann Bauer 2的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

- **1935.11** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.refuse` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  学徒在旁边小声说，拒绝按手印的人名字会被单独抄在一张纸上。Rosa没接话，把八克朗换成一克朗的硬币，数给家里时硬币在桌上滚了两圈才停。屋里的煤油灯芯剪得太短，光只够照亮手边的桌面。

  医院派来一名实习医生，身后跟着两个学生。他们把Rosa的胸口听了个遍，最后请来上级；药费减半，但Rosa被当成了讲课的病例。学生们走后，实习医生把听诊器在手里捂了很久，才敢再贴上来。

  止咳药水是褐色的，瓶口塞着软木塞，摇一摇会起沫。Rosa看着工友把表折成小块，折了三折才塞进帽檐。墙上的规章还贴在那里，纸角被风掀起，没人去按。

  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (高烧硬熬伤身); condition; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68_

  _effects: organization_funds; reputation -0.010000000000000002; flag +deferred_maintenance; knowledge “屋顶的修理费被挪去补煤款，煤款又被挪去付印刷费。”_

  _effects: flag +graduation_project_revised; skill_

  Rosa在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Berta Bauer。接生的人收六克朗，Josef在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Rosa起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; health -0.08000000000000002 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:522_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; reputation 0.04000000000000001; skill_

### 146 subject events

- **1913.03** `vienna-infant-household.laundry-day/scalded-hand` tags: infancy, health, family, home, injury
  Josef Bauer提热水壶往木盆里兑水时壶底脱了焊，滚水溅在左手背上。她咬着牙把壶搁下，Emil被响声惊得大哭。手背起了水泡，接下来三天她只能用一只手洗尿布。
  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (左手被开水烫伤); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1915.02** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  黑楼梯间只有门缝透进来的一点光。Emil踩空时伸手去抓扶手，手臂先撞在铁端上，整个人滚下三级。Josef Bauer把Emil抱到灶边，拿湿冷毛巾按住肿起的地方，又撕了一条旧布缠紧。第二天Emil举着胳膊下楼，照样去院子里玩。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.05000000000000002_

- **1915.02** `statistical_infection`
  Emil在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，痢疾让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1915.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Emil的痢疾没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1915.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Emil的痢疾没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1915.08** `statistical_recovery`
  Emil熬过了这场痢疾，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1917.11** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-win` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  铁环在石板缝上颠了一下，Emil用钩子一拨，抢先绕过煤车。Katharina Kral跟在后面，铁环倒在地上转了两圈。三颗玻璃弹珠从Katharina Kral的口袋里摸出来，有一颗还带着体温。Katharina Kral把弹珠按进Emil手心，转身踢了一脚墙根。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20; mood 0.05; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-shelter-missed-river` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  河水那边传来同伴的笑声，Emil站在院门口听了听，还是回去把木盆扶正。Rosa Bauer睡醒哭了一声，Emil把最后半块面包掰碎泡进温水，一勺一勺喂。Josef Bauer回来时，Emil正蹲在盆边打瞌睡，手里还攥着喂饭的木勺。
  _effects: mood -0.02; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000285:child; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

- **1918.04** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  楼梯间的灯罩空着，第三级踏板比别处高。Emil一脚踩空，滚到平台，右小臂磕在铁扶手的弯头上。Josef Bauer听见哭声跑出来，把Emil抱回屋，用冷水毛巾敷那块青紫，再用旧布条缠了两圈。夜里Emil哭醒两次。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1918.06** `p9-childhood-household.water-and-coal/helped-neighbor-for-bread` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  Gustav Berg的煤筐歪在门槛上，黑煤球滚了一地。Emil蹲下把煤球捡回筐里，指头立刻染黑，又帮Gustav Berg把筐抬进灶间。Gustav Berg从抽屉里摸出半块面包，Emil揣进书包就往Leopoldstädter Volksschule跑，进门时半节课已经过去。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg; reputation -0.02_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Emil Berg家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Emil递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Emil Berg端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Emil把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1919.12** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/won-the-race-lost-a-button` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Emil跑到第二圈时鞋带松了，索性踩着鞋后跟跑。最后一圈，Emil从床单底下钻过去，先拍到墙。Maria Kral喘着气，把两颗弹珠拍进Emil手心。Emil低头才发现外套少了一颗扣子；窗口的Maria Kral扔下一根别针，说先别着。
  _effects: mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-falls-at-well` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  土豆滚进墙角的煤灰里，Emil弯腰去追，再抬头时Rosa Bauer已经坐在井台边，裤腿湿了一片，哭声一抽一抽。Hedwig Stein把孩子抱进灶间，Emil跟在后面，手指抠着门框。Josef Bauer回来没问经过，只把Emil的碗收走一半，说“看个人都看不住”。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (在井台边摔了一跤); mood -0.06; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

  _effects: mood 0.06000000000000001; reputation 0.02; relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912_

  Emil在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Josef。这一次对方先点了头，Emil准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Emil才发现，自己把书拿倒了。

- **1928.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.join-a-club/went-once-and-left` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Emil交了第一个月的会费，进去坐了一刻钟。屋里太吵，下棋的争论盖过了念书的声音，他借的书也被人翻得卷了边。下个月他路过门口，没再进去交十赫勒。

  检查员来查佣工合同，Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)让Emil把手藏在围裙下面。Emil没有藏，摊开掌心，那块新疤还没有褪成白色。检查员看了一眼，又看了一眼Franz Berg，把名字写进本子。Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)说这是烫衣服不小心，谁家没有这种事。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; flag +reported_iron_burn_to_inspector; role; knowledge “the inspector recorded the burn and the household address”; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

  Helene Berg把Emil和Anton一起叫到讲台前，问墨水瓶底下的公式是谁写的。Emil说不知道；Anton低着头，袖口还沾着同样的蓝墨水。Helene Berg让两人各写一份检讨，Emil写到一半，听见Anton在旁边小声说“对不起”，笔尖把纸戳破了。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; flag +yf18_exam_suspicion; mood -0.03999999999999998_

  客户把床单贴在脸上，又摸了摸边角，说“还是潮的”，把门合上一半。Emil把床单卷回筐里，背回洗衣房。Anna Hofer没骂，只让在后院重晾，件钱等重送完再结。Emil蹲在地上翻布角，晚风吹得膝盖发酸，天黑前才夹完最后一只夹子。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (蹲久了膝盖发酸，又吹了晚风); mood -0.03999999999999998; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27_

  Emil把那页地图撕下来，折成四折藏进袜筒。回家路上，Emil在煤气灯下把字读了三遍，又把纸塞进炉子；火苗舔到“多瑙河”三个字时，Emil伸手抢出来，指尖烫出一个泡。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:155; health -0.010000000000000009 (a burned fingertip); flag +yf18_burned_fingers; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:156; cash -1 (a stolen stamp and writing paper); flag +yf18_cousin_reply; mood 0.02999999999999997; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

  地窖里堆着煤和酸菜桶，两人站在楼梯下面，Alois Winter把一枚纽扣塞进Emil手里，说是军装上的，掉了还能再缝。Hermann Bauer的钥匙在楼上响，Emil把纽扣含在嘴里，出去时脸很红。
  _effects: mood 0.08000000000000002; flag +let_follower_into_cellar; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; knowledge “the housekeeper checks the cellar door after dark”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

  Emil在学校的煤炉边把油印纸烧了，灰烬和煤渣混在一起。Grete Stein第二天问第三十四页读得怎么样，Emil说译文看不出来；Grete Stein点点头，把课本收回去，从此没有再借给Emil任何书。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; flag +yf18_burned_pamphlet_

- **1930.01** `major-family.proposal-refused/proposal-refused.bad-blood` tags: family, courtship, refusal, major, lifecycle
  Katharina Hofer 3的母亲把Emil带来的点心原封退回，说家里不娶有肺病亲戚的人。点心是早上排队买的，油纸还热着，Emil把它抱在胸前。Katharina Hofer 3站在门后没有出来，只让妹妹传话。Emil把戒指留在门房，走回Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang时天还没黑，楼下的狗一直叫。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70; role; flag +marriage_refused; cash -2 (pastries and the returned ring); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70_

  Emil在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Katharina Hofer 3的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。

  Lena端来一碗兑了水的牛奶，顺手把Emil家的炉子捅旺。Emil靠在床头，听Lena讲楼下谁也被传染了，谁家把病人送去了医院。人走后，屋里还留着一点煤烟和奶味。
  _effects: cash -1 (退烧后的第一碗粥); health 0.020000000000000018 (退烧后的第一碗粥); flag +recovered_with_neighbor_help; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912_

- **1930.03** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hospital-deposit` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Emil跑了一趟医院，把Josef Bauer送进病房：押金三克朗，搪瓷杯和旧毛巾一克朗二十赫勒，头三天的药费三克朗八十赫勒，一共八克朗。护士收走便盆，换上带刻度的玻璃壶。Emil回到空了的床边，把塌下去的椅子扶正，才发现自己一整天只喝了一杯咖啡。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院押金和住院用品); health 0.06000000000000005 (父亲住进医院得到治疗); mood -0.06; flag +parent_hospitalized_

- **1930.06** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty, injury
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Alois Lechner 2把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Emil把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Alois Lechner 2低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; flag +rent_increase_accepted; health -0.020000000000000018 (winter coal money spent on the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1931.01** `major-work-first-employment/turned-away` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头翻完工票，问Emil上一个东家是谁。Emil说厂子关了，工头又问有没有辞工证明；没有证明，名册就不能写。后面的人挤上来，Emil被推到台阶下面。中午的钟响了，Emil站在街对面，看新来的人把名字一个个写上去。
  _effects: cash -2 (carfare and waiting); reputation -0.03; flag +no_work_references; role; mood -0.06999999999999995; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

  Emil在厨房里对着镜子练了七遍开场白，第二天却在离Rosa Bauer三步远的地方拐进面包店。Emil买了一袋并不需要的盐，回家路上把开场白又想了一遍；这一次，连标点都更熟练了。

  夜里十一点，母狮不肯碰桶里的马肉。Emil举着煤油灯绕到兽栏后面，看见它右槽牙断了一截，牙龈肿得发亮。Emil把肉切碎，又去敲Marie Huber的门；第二天兽医来了，Josef Berg 2在晨会上第一次记住Emil的名字。

- **1931.12** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.food-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Emil只收下篮里的面包和豆子，把煤袋推回Rosa Lechner手里，说房租自己再想办法，请她们给介绍点零工。Rosa Lechner把煤放在走廊窗台，说礼拜四后院有人要拆旧木箱，一赫勒一捆，工钱不多但现结。Emil记下门牌，答应一早过去。

- **1932.01** `oj18-zoo.escape::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/blamed-for-the-gate` tags: zoo, animals, escape, night, blame, work, odd_job, rural
  园长在晨会上说，第二道门的锁是夜班没扣好。Emil知道那锁早就坏了，报修单还压在办公室抽屉里，但没有人替Emil说话。当天下午，Emil的工牌被收走，兽栏钥匙交给了新来的临时工。
  _effects: position_end 夜班饲养员 @zoo:person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:004459; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 10; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 55 -> 45; cash -2 (lost the zoo job); reputation -0.04000000000000001; mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +dismissed_after_escape; knowledge “第二道门的报修单还在办公室抽屉里，出事时没有人提它。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state

- **1932.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/learned-the-building` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  又过了一个冬天，Emil能从楼梯的响声判断是谁在值夜，也能从煤气灯的颜色判断哪一段管道该修。师傅把巡查路线改了三次，最后仍然照Emil画的那张走；两个人都没有提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 10 -> 20; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 20 -> 30; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 24; cash 2 (night-shift wages); skill_

  Emil又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。

- **1932.01** `task-vienna-bourgeois-youth.family-business-or-study/chose-university` tags: bourgeois, education, culture, salon, university, family, task, task:education
  Emil把钥匙推回去，说要去听课。Josef Bauer当场停掉每月二十克朗的零用钱，母亲没敢再劝。他第二周就找了两家补习学生，每周四晚给人补拉丁文。
  _effects: cash -15 (失去零用后自付开销); relation kin with person-josef-bauer; flag +self_supporting_student; skill_

- **1932.03** `state_reaction:wish-love/love-betrayal` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_reaction
  Emil反复回想最后一次见面。对方说过的话被拆成很多小块，每一块都能扎人。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004838_

- **1932.08** `major-education-university-registration/matriculated` tags: education, university, migration, major
  注册处窗口前排了三十多人。Emil把中学毕业证、洗礼证明、居住证明和一份家庭收入证明一张张递进去，Maria Kral用放大镜看了一遍，收下四十五克朗，在名册上写下编号。开学前，Emil在利奥波德城租下一间朝院子的房，床板、煤炉和一张书桌，月租十二克朗。
  _effects: education higher; cash -45 (matriculation fee); cash -12 (first month rent for the student room); move place-wien-leopoldstadt-tenement; role; flag +matriculated; relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral_

- **1932.09** `bc18-crime.failed-burglary::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/health-inspectors` tags: crime, burglary, mistake, absurd, police, legal, comic, rural
  两人摸黑撬开一扇门，里面没有保险柜，只有背景布、三脚架和一池显影水。店主以为他们是卫生局派来查暗房通风的，殷勤地领着看排水口；Mitzi Berg只好装模作样地记了几笔，Emil顺手把柜台上一顶帽子戴走。临走时，店主还问要不要写整改通知。
  _effects: cash 1 (卖掉顺走的帽子); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79; condition; flag +burglary_mistaken_for_inspection; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79_

- **1933.01** `vienna-family-divorce/reconcile-for-now` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle
  Emil和Anton Lechner在楼道里吵到半夜，最后因为下个月的房租又坐回同一张桌。谁都没有提和好，只把账本重新摊开；Anton Lechner把煤钱推过来，Emil接过去数了两遍，手指碰到一起又分开，屋里的灯亮到很晚。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1933.01** `oj18-zoo.escape::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/hid-the-chimp` tags: zoo, animals, escape, night, blame, work, odd_job, reform
  马戏班老板Josef Bauer在普拉特门口拦住Emil，说愿意出三十克朗买那只黑猩猩，还缺一个懂它脾气的驯养人。Emil没有答应，也没有报告；夜里，Emil把卡斯帕藏进废弃的鸟舍，每天多带一份面包。
  _effects: cash -1 (bread for the hidden chimpanzee); relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 30 -> 38; state_add oj18-workplace-legend state:world-vienna-1912:279; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +hid_chimp; knowledge “卡斯帕藏在废弃鸟舍里，听见面包纸响就会从梁上下来。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; role; flag +workplace_legend_

- **1933.01** `r13-civ.clinic-first-visit::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/chronic-cough` tags: health, clinic, queue, diagnosis, institution, reform, medical, schooling
  Emil在诊所走廊的第三张长凳上坐到中午，膝上压着挂号纸和一块湿手帕。Helene Stein 2把听诊器贴上来不到半分钟，说是潮气和烟，开了褐色止咳糖浆，又让下周再来。药费从当天的菜钱里扣走，回家时篮子里只剩半棵卷心菜。
  _effects: cash -3 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); health -0.030000000000000027 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); condition; flag +clinic_first_visit; knowledge “the clinic doctor called the cough chronic bronchitis”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72_

- **1933.03** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Emil说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Helene Lechner没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Emil听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; flag +funeral_society_left; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

  来问入会的是个瘦高的年轻人，帽檐捏在手里，说自己在第十六区的织布厂做了半年。Emil把名册翻到空白页，蘸水笔尖在“住址”那栏停了一下，等他报出街名。老成员在角落里把账本合上，声音很轻。
  _effects: cash 1 (新会员的入会费先记在桌上); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

  两年过去，Emil已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Emil也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 20 -> 30; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 46 -> 56; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 24 -> 48; cash 2 (night-shift wages); skill_

- **1934.01** `p10-family-tasks.find-missing-kin/find-missing-kin.postcard` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, missing_kin
  旅店老板在柜台后想了想，说那批工人去了鲁尔，哥哥还欠两晚房钱。Emil在红十字会表格上一笔一划填地址，又贴了邮票。两个月后明信片到了，背面只有一行铅笔字：还活着，在矿上，别寄钱。
  _effects: mood 0.12; cash -1 (寄往鲁尔的信和明信片邮费); knowledge “哥哥在鲁尔矿区做工，还活着”_

- **1934.07** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.sick_child_doorstep/refuses_grudge` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Klara敲门想借半块煤给发烧的孩子烧热水，Emil那天刚和Klara为走廊堆东西吵过嘴，没开门，隔着门板说自家也不够用。后半夜听见Klara抱着孩子下楼去敲药房的门。第二天在走廊碰见，Klara低头绕过去，手里的铜壶碰在栏杆上，响了一声。
  _effects: relation rival with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1934.12** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.fill-form` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life, injury
  衙门走廊的地砖是黑白相间的，Emil站着站着就把重心从一只脚换到另一只脚。窗口里的办事员姓什么没人知道，只叫“三号窗”。表递进去时，里面说“等复核”，声音很平。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (在衙门走廊站了大半天); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:oj18-workplace-legend/story-year/story-grows-teeth` tags: oj18-workplace-legend, work, legend, odd_job, rumor, identity, night, state
  故事传到第三个街区时，已经多出一只会说话的猫和两具没有头的尸体。Emil想纠正，发现自己越解释，听的人越兴奋；酒馆老板干脆把最离谱的版本写在黑板上，下面画了一个箭头：本店常客。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-workplace-legend.legend 20 -> 28; state_parameter oj18-workplace-legend.truth 55 -> 50; state_parameter oj18-workplace-legend.toldCount 0 -> 2; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +legend_exaggerated_

- **1935.01** `r13-edu.study-group::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/both-pass-exam` tags: education, study_group, mutual_aid, politics, belonging, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil和Klara在阁楼里支起一块旧门板当桌子，每周三晚上互相考问。Klara把容易错的公式写在烟盒背面，Emil负责讲解历史背景。考试结果出来，两个人都过了，Klara把烟盒送给Emil作纪念，说以后别再弄丢。烟盒被Emil放在书架上，一直没拆。
  _effects: flag +study_group_passed; skill; relation colleague with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912_

- **1935.06** `ml18-lottery.same-numbers::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-partner-bought-it-secretly` tags: lottery, luck, money, family, regret, windfall, vienna, urban
  Anton Lechner在开奖前偷偷去了烟草铺，用买菜剩下的钱买了那组号码，把票藏在糖罐后面。号码中了，Anton Lechner把票放在餐桌上，说本来想等Emil生日再讲。Emil先是不信，随后笑得把椅子往后推了一截；两个人坐在厨房里数奖金，数到一半又停下来烧水。
  _effects: cash 70 (the lottery prize bought with the grocery money); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:335; reputation 0.03; flag +partner_bought_the_ticket; mood 0.09999999999999998; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.01999999999999999; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1935.06** `df18-luck.borrowed-at-the-card-table::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/marked-deck` tags: luck, debt, bargain, gambling, obligation, night, supernatural, vienna
  Emil赢了三克朗，起身时才发现牌背有指甲划出的记号。Ferdinand Bauer笑着把牌收进袖子里，说这叫“借来的手气”。Emil没有把钱退回去，只把那张有记号的牌偷偷带走。第二天，Emil在当铺门口看见Ferdinand Bauer正在和一个收旧货的人分钱。
  _effects: cash 3 (牌桌上赢来的三克朗); state_add df18-borrowed-luck state:world-vienna-1912:336; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; flag +df18_marked_deck; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +df18_luck_borrowed; knowledge “我得到了一点不该白拿的好运，代价还没有说清。”_

- **1935.07** `hs18-cinema.newsreel::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/clap-with-the-crowd` tags: cinema, newsreel, neighbor, recognition, media, history, vienna, urban
  新闻片放到新修的桥，观众跟着鼓掌。Emil也拍了两下手，掌心却停在半空——画面角落里那家面包店，昨天还排着另一条队。Klara说下回还来，Emil说票价太贵。
  _effects: cash -1 (two cinema tickets); mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +clapped_at_the_newsreel_

- **1935.10** `kernel-social.neighbor-conflict::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/neighbor-conflict.chalk-line` tags: social, neighbor, conflict, everyday, housing, scarcity, vienna, urban
  Berta Hofer把两家叫到楼梯口，用粉笔在地上画了一条线，说煤堆和童车各占一半。邻居先把自己的煤往里挪了半步，Emil也把童车折起来；第二天，线被谁的鞋底蹭花了一半，但煤堆没有再越界。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; role; flag +painted_floor_line; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

  _effects: cash -2 (利息被重新计算); state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.debt 5 -> 11; state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.luck 15 -> 10; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1936.01** `p10-family-tasks.arrange-childcare/arrange-childcare.sister` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, childcare
  姐姐把孩子裹在披肩里抱上电车，说城外有院子，一个月就送回来。Emil站在站台上看车走远。一个月后孩子会叫人了，可看见Emil就往姐姐身后躲。Emil把孩子抱进屋，他哭了半宿。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; relation kin with person-anna-bauer_

- **1936.03** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.buy` tags: home, market, food, social
  鱼摊快收了，Emil挑了一条最小的鳟鱼，摊主顺手抓了把欧芹丢进纸里。黄油在锅里化开，鱼皮卷起来，楼道里都是味。Alois Berg敲门时，Emil从鱼尾切下一小块递过去。
  _effects: cash -6 (买鳟鱼和黄油); food; mood 0.08000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

- **1936.03** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.cousin-bed/give-camp-bed-and-board` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Emil让Josef Bauer睡厨房的行军床，讲好每月三克朗饭钱，煤钱另算。白天把铺盖卷起来靠在碗柜边，晚上再铺开；厨房多一个人，面包和煤都下去得快，伙食上短了两克朗。看门人再来问时，Emil把藤箱指过去，说人住下了，灯油钱也记在账上。
  _effects: cash 3 (表亲交来的饭钱); food; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; flag +kin_staying_over_

- **1936.12** `r3-crime.victim-family::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::military-court-jurisdiction/case-dropped` tags: crime, family, grief, court, settlement, vienna, urban, crisis
  Gustav Kral第二次来，没有进门，只站在台阶上说证据不够。Rosa Bauer问“那天的血衣呢”，Gustav Kral说“在证物室，但编号对不上”。灵堂里的蜡烛烧到了底，Mitzi把状纸收起来，折成四折，塞进供桌下面。
  _effects: mood -0.3; condition; flag +case_dropped_for_lack_of_evidence; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; knowledge “the bloodstained coat lost its evidence number”_

  Emil在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Helene Kral 2的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction_

  _effects: cash -1 (还掉一小笔); state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.debt 11 -> 3; state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.luck 10 -> 13; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps); condition_

- **1938.01** `af17-wish.family-inheritance::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/kept-the-shop-and-the-sisters` tags: wish, love, family, inheritance, shop, debt, irony, vienna

  _effects: role; cash -2 (先还一部分烟铺赊账); relation kin with person-ferdinand-winter; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; reputation 0.020000000000000018; condition; flag +kept_the_tobacco_shop; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Emil把床上的旧褥子重新铺平，Mitzi Weiss放下自己的铺盖卷，从怀里掏出一把钥匙看了看。厨房里多了一口人的碗，灶边的煤省着烧也够。Anna Bauer在门口看了一眼，没多话。
  _effects: cash 4 (出租空床收进的月租); household; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer_

  洗衣房里的人压低声音说，Hermann Bauer 2已经把Emil的名字写进了门房的本子，说Emil替罢工的人送过饭。Emil没有吵，只问本子现在在谁手里。Rosa Berg把最后一件衣服拧干，说昨晚看见本子被送进了分局。
  _effects: reputation -0.12; condition; flag +named_in_porter_book; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; knowledge “the porter’s book was taken to the district police office”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

  梦里，Emil看见地窖的水漫过脚踝，木箱一只只浮起来。第二天一早，Emil把箱子和煤筐搬到床上。中午，楼下的水管裂了，水沿着墙根流进地窖，只浸湿了一只空桶。Ferdinand Berg 2说Emil运气好，Emil没有解释。
  _effects: cash -1 (搬箱子时打碎了一只罐子); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; knowledge “我梦见地窖进水，第二天水管真的裂了。”; flag +df18_moved_crates_before_flood; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

  _effects: cash -4 (付给勒索者的封口钱); reputation -0.020000000000000018; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; condition; flag +paid_then_reported_blackmail; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82_

  _effects: reputation 0.03999999999999998; flag +confronted_village_gossip; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

  Emil在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Otto Lechner从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (左手背烫伤); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93_

  Emil和Helene Kral 2因为一只水桶吵了一架，谁也不肯先开口。过了半个月，Helene Kral 2家的孩子病了，Emil把自家的退烧药分了一半放在她门口，没有留名字。第二天，水桶被放回了原处，桶底多了一块洗得发白的布。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +reconciled_over_a_water_bucket_

- **1942.11** `major-crisis.rent-increase/tenant-meeting` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Emil把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Mitzi站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Emil手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。
  _effects: cash -2 (paper, ink and tram fare for the tenants meeting); role; flag +rent_strike; reputation 0.030000000000000027_

- **1943.01** `curse15-betrayal.signature::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-files-went-first` tags: betrayal, office, power, politics, curse, institution, vienna, urban
  Rosa Stein 2先把Emil抽屉里的往来信件搬空，再在会议上说Emil从来没有保存过记录。没有人能证明那些信存在过，除了Emil自己。散会后，有人让Emil回家休息几天，工资照发。Emil知道这不是照顾，是让Emil别再回来。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65; knowledge “我经手的信件在会议前被人搬空了。”; condition; flag +records_removed_

  _effects: cash -0.6 (三杯 Melange 的咖啡钱); mood 0.050000000000000044; skill_

  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1943.12** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  Emil在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Mitzi Weiss从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (左手背烫伤); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1944.01** `statistical_death/violence` tags: death

  _effects: death (暴力)_

- **1915.08** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  黑楼梯间只有门缝透进来的一点光。Lena踩空时伸手去抓扶手，手臂先撞在铁端上，整个人滚下三级。Josefine Hofer把Lena抱到灶边，拿湿冷毛巾按住肿起的地方，又撕了一条旧布缠紧。第二天Lena举着胳膊下楼，照样去院子里玩。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food

  Rosa Kral 2让全班按座位排队，Gustav Berg在讲台边摆开酒精瓶和针盒。轮到Lena时，Rosa Kral 2替她把袖子卷好，说别怕，去年他自己也种过。下课后，黑板上还留着一行没擦掉的日期。

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58_

- **1933.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Lena想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。

- **1933.03** `r2-leisure.mourning-dress::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::mourning-dress-reform/altered-ready-made-mourning-dress` tags: leisure, dressmaker, mourning, grief, craft, reform, medical, schooling
  成衣店的黑裙第二天就送到，Otto Winter穿着来改腰。Lena拆开下摆，发现里面缝着一块旧衬，针脚是殡仪馆的手法。Otto Winter问要不要拆掉，Lena说拆了就不挺了，留着吧，反正没人看见。
  _effects: cash 3 (黑绸裙的最后一针); mood -0.030000000000000027; condition; flag +altered_ready_made_mourning_dress; relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter; knowledge “the ready-made mourning dress is lined with funeral-contract”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1933.06** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.send-out` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  洗衣妇把工服从报纸里抖出来，翻开领口看了看油垢，点头收下。Lena转身回屋，铜板在围裙口袋里磕出轻响。Grete Stein还在楼梯上嘟囔，说这钱不如买半斤猪油。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (请洗衣妇代洗工服); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1933.08** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.accept-all` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  门铃是根铁丝拉的，响得发闷。Ferdinand Winter提篮进门先看水槽里泡着的碗，再看墙角的煤箱，说价钱她心里有数。Lena把印泥盒推到桌边，按下去时指尖凉了一下。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; relation acquaintance with person-ferdinand-winter; flag +parish_registered_

- **1933.12** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  有人抱怨守夜人太多，踩坏了楼梯木板。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; reputation -0.03; mood -0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1934.01** `ld18-intimacy.hairwash::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-water-goes-cold` tags: love, intimacy, gentle, everyday, queer, body, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Emil坐在床边，Lena用搪瓷盆替她洗头。水凉了又添，肥皂泡顺着耳后流下来；Emil闭着眼说，小时候她母亲也是这样替她洗的。窗外有人收煤，铁锹刮过石板，两个人谁也没有动。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 53 -> 58; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 32 -> 29; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +hairwash_sunday_

- **1934.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/the-date-that-is-not-on-paper` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  Lena和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Lena在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 58 -> 62; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 29 -> 26; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1934.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/ignore-diagnosis` tags: health, medical, family, money
  下午的活是搬纱锭，Lena蹲下去再站起来眼前发黑，扶墙站了一会儿。工友老约瑟夫把自己的水杯推过来，说去诊所开个假条吧，她摇头，说假条要扣钱。
  _effects: condition; mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

- **1934.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Lena看Berta Hofer盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Berta Hofer愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Lena门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Lena打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; food_

- **1934.12** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Lena在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Rosa Berg 2却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Lena把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77; flag +still_looking_for_room; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Lena最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Lena把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Lena说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 26 -> 32; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 46 -> 43; mood -0.020000000000000018_
