  她把签名纸送到市政厅，Gustav Kral说格式不对，要按街道分开。她回去重抄，发现有几个名字已经认不出；再去时，窗口换了一个人，说前一班没有交接。她坐在市政厅台阶上重抄，风把纸吹到花坛里。
  _effects: flag +petition_refused; reputation 0.02; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; cash -0.5 (重抄签名纸的纸笔钱); knowledge “请愿书要按街道分页，窗口换班时前一班可能没有交接”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

- **1935.07** `vienna-social-movement.aftermath/testify` tags: development, social_movement, organization, consequence
  Lena在警察局走廊里等了一下午，最后把知道的地址写在纸上。回家时，楼道里有人把门关得很轻；Lena知道他们听见了脚步声。
  _effects: organization_membership; reputation -0.05_

- **1935.10** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/second-bridge-promise` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  在第二个桥洞下，Rosa Huber 2先停下来，用鞋尖碾一颗烤栗子壳，说下星期天还是这个时候、还是这座桥。Lena点头，掏出四赫勒买了两小袋栗子，两人分着剥，手上都是糖渍；桥洞上的水还在滴，谁也没有提万一明天下雨怎么办。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; cash -0.04 (两小袋烤栗子); mood 0.06000000000000005_

- **1935.12** `vienna-history-kraus-lecture/leave-before-the-end` tags: history, culture, karl_kraus, cafe_central, public_lecture, press
  朗读开始不久，Lena 发现后排有人在记名字，便提前离开。走到门口时，Karl Kraus 的声音还在背后，句子像钉子一样短。第二天，Lena 在报摊上看到同一篇文章被删去了两段。
  _effects: knowledge “Café Central 的公开朗读吸引便衣和报纸记者，现场听众并不全是同情者。”; relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; cash -1 (coffee before leaving the reading); mood -0.010000000000000009_

  两人在坟场里绕了半个钟头，才找到那块刻着名字的石头。Helene Lechner忽然说不是这一块，自己记得旁边有一棵小槐树。Lena陪她一排排找，天快黑时，守墓人过来说小槐树去年就砍了；Helene Lechner站在空地上，把花放在了脚边。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; condition; flag +could_not_find_the_grave; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; knowledge “the old tree by the grave was cut down last year”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1936.07** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/move-to-shared-tenement` tags: housing, home, health, family, household
  Lena把床架拆开，用绳子捆在借来的手推车上，搬进利奥波德城的合租房。押金和车钱花掉八克朗；新住处人多、墙薄，但至少每个人有一块能躺下的地方，Lena的腰在冷风里疼了一路，夜里却第一次不用侧着身睡。
  _effects: residence; cash -8 (deposit and cart)_

- **1936.07** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Lena没有立刻答话，先去敲Helene Kral 2的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Helene Kral 2提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Lena把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1936.08** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/report-to-official` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Lena把传单交给楼里的门房。门房没有接，只让Lena自己去找警察局。那天傍晚，两个穿便衣的人在街口站了很久。
  _effects: role; reputation 0.02_

- **1936.11** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.dock-morning/dock-watched-tools` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, dock
  河面的雾散尽时，码头上的人走了一半。Lena蹲在石阶上替Emil Weiss守着麻绳和铁钩，看装卸工在跳板上喊号子。汤送来时还烫嘴，豆子沉在碗底。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91_

- **1937.01** `vienna-family-divorce/partner-moves-to-shared-housing` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation, household
  Rosa Huber 2把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Lena留在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Lena在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods（其中4克朗记入债务）); mood -0.05999999999999994; role_

- **1937.01** `ld18-absurd.moral-prize::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/a-medal-for-model-household` tags: absurd, irony, scandal, public, marriage, queer, secret_intimacy, vienna
  道德协会把一枚铜牌送到Lena和Helene Berg的店里，说她们的铺子窗明几净、账目清楚，是模范家庭。Gustav Kral握着她们的手，问什么时候请吃喜酒；Lena说等生意再好一点，Helene Berg在旁边把抹布拧成了麻花。
  _effects: reputation 0.08; cash 2 (the prize money and the new customers); state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:389; flag +model_household_medal; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +public_desire_active_

  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 29 -> 39; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.grief 0 -> 4; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  办公室有人问Lena，为什么总有一个女人在楼下等她。Lena说那是表妹；对方笑了一下，说现在的表妹真体贴。下午，Lena把桌上的相框扣倒，又在没人时翻回来，用袖子擦了擦玻璃上的灰。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 39 -> 47; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 43 -> 39; reputation -0.020000000000000004_

  _effects: relation partner with person-elisabeth-bauer; household; mood 0.06000000000000005_

  一个陌生女人在电车上叫住Lena，说自己认识她的“那位”，还劝她趁年轻找个正经人家。Lena没有回答，提前一站下了车；走在回家的路上，她发现自己的步子越来越快，像在躲一个并不存在的人。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 30 -> 38; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 20 -> 24; reputation -0.03; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1938.01** `r13-fam.look-for-lost-relative::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/name-on-list` tags: task, family, missing, official, war, search, vienna, urban
  Lena替Rosa Lechner把名字、出生年和最后住址写在纸上，递进窗口。Grete Stein翻了三本名册，手指停在一行被水洇开的字上，说这个人登记在临时收容所。Rosa Lechner听见时，手里的伞掉在地上；Lena替她把伞捡起来，才发现自己的手也在抖。
  _effects: cash -2 (念错的名字（其中2克朗记入债务）); flag +found_missing_relative_on_list; role; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner; knowledge “the missing relative was registered at a transit shelter”_

- **1938.11** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.factory-gate/gate-queue-clash` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, factory, hiring
  招工纸上的工钱写得明白，一天三克朗八十赫勒。Lena怕再等下去名额没了，从人缝里挤到前面，后面的Alois Berg一把拽住袖子。门房抬头看了看乱糟糟的队伍，把玻璃窗拉上了。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73_

  Lena在编制表最下面添了一行“顾问”，月薪两克朗，职责是“必要时提供咨询”。Alois Bauer第二天就搬来一张靠窗的桌子，带来一盆天竺葵；一个月后，Grete Stein发现顾问唯一经手的文件是自己的午餐菜单。
  _effects: position 顾问 @zoo:person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:004043; organization_funds; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000275:child; flag +private_sinecure; knowledge “顾问岗位没有固定职责，考勤由我签字。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.01** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.sheet-quarrel` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Lena发现自己搭在栏杆上的床单被人挪到了绳尾，角上沾了煤灰，一时火起，在楼梯间拦住Gustav Lechner 2问是谁动的。Gustav Lechner 2说是Otto Lechner嫌滴水，两人越说越响，最后各自回屋摔上门，炉子空着，谁也没再去烧水。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93_

  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Lena最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Lena把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Lena说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 56 -> 62; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 39 -> 36_

  故事又换了一个版本：有人说Lena是为了钱，有人说是为了爱，还有人说她只是喜欢看别人议论。Lena在咖啡馆听见第三种说法，没有更正，只把杯子往窗边挪了挪；阳光照进来，她第一次觉得这些版本都离自己很远。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 38 -> 34; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 61 -> 63; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Helene Stein把煤筐往身后挪，说不用，自己搬得动。Lena站在楼梯转角，听见对方上一层歇一层，筐底在墙上刮出长长的白印。晚饭时，Lena把自家的汤盛了一碗，放在Helene Stein门口，没有敲门。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +left_soup_at_neighbour_door_

  Lena刚把钱包交给街角岗亭，Grete Stein就翻了翻里面的纸，问“你住哪条街”。钱包确实是失物，但失主是警局的人；Lena拿回一张收据，回家路上总觉得有人在后面跟着。
  _effects: reputation 0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; flag +returned_wallet_to_police; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Lena在后屋的会议上报出自己街区的名字：谁家缺煤，谁被房东催租，谁知道哪间印刷厂还在招人。Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung把这些事记进一本旧账本，散会时每个人带走一叠更小的纸条。
  _effects: skill; reputation 0.020000000000000004_

  _effects: cash -5 (报名费不退); skill; flag +shorthand_retake_

  来问入会的是个瘦高的年轻人，帽檐捏在手里，说自己在第十六区的织布厂做了半年。Lena把名册翻到空白页，蘸水笔尖在“住址”那栏停了一下，等他报出街名。老成员在角落里把账本合上，声音很轻。
  _effects: cash 1 (新会员的入会费先记在桌上); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

- **1940.08** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.with-priest` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Lena觉得一个人去不合适，绕到教堂请了修女一同上楼。修女看过病人，留下药包和一小瓶圣水，说会从教区账上出钱请医生来看一趟。Katharina Kral 3在楼下等着，说这样妥当，病人家里没男人，多个见证人心里安稳。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +parish_sick_visitor; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82_

- **1941.01** `curse15-betrayal.signature::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-files-went-first` tags: betrayal, office, power, politics, curse, institution, vienna, urban
  Anna Huber 2先把Lena抽屉里的往来信件搬空，再在会议上说Lena从来没有保存过记录。没有人能证明那些信存在过，除了Lena自己。散会后，有人让Lena回家休息几天，工资照发。Lena知道这不是照顾，是让Lena别再回来。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90; knowledge “我经手的信件在会议前被人搬空了。”; condition; flag +records_removed; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90_

  Lena和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Lena在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 74 -> 78; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 68 -> 65; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Lena站在书架前抽出一本厚小说，就着门口的光读了半章，Berta Winter在柜台后抬起眼皮看了一眼，没说话。读到要紧处，Lena摸出铅笔头把页码记在车票背面，把书插回原处，说下次带够押金再来；白读半章，管理员的眼神却凉了一点。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1941.06** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.dispute-rate` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Mitzi Berg用拇指甲刮着一件领子的边线，说这儿跳了针。Lena把煤油灯往前推了推，让他凑近看，说线是细了点，活没少做。Ferdinand Berg倚在门框上没走，屋里一时只有尺子碰桌面的响。
  _effects: cash 0.6 (争执后按降低的工价结算); reputation 0.03; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

  教堂门口的汤锅从早上烧到中午，Lena负责分面包，手被蒸汽烫红。Gustav Berg说每人一勺，不能多；轮到Katharina Winter时，怀里还抱着孩子，Lena把勺子在锅底多刮了一下。
  _effects: health -0.010000000000000009 (教堂门口的汤锅); flag +extra_ladle_for_child; role; relation colleague with person-gustav-berg; knowledge “the child had not eaten since the previous morning”_

- **1942.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Lena在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Lena把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.050000000000000044 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_

  任期在十二月三十一日结束。Lena把印章擦干净，钥匙串上只剩自己家的那把；Mitzi Winter 2来接手续，两个人把最后一份移交清单对了两遍。走出大门时，门房照旧叫了一声“先生”，没有加头衔。
  _effects: position_end 夜班饲养员 @zoo:person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:004043; reputation 0.02; mood -0.020000000000000018; condition; flag +left_office_in_order; knowledge “移交清单上最后一项是印章，钥匙串上只剩自己家的那把。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

- **1942.01** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Lena没有立刻答话，先去敲Josef的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Josef提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Lena把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  鹦鹉死在第二个冬天。窗缝里灌进来的风把笼布吹开，它缩在食盆旁边，像一团被揉皱的灰纸。Mitzi Winter 2说条件没有完成，十二克朗归了姑妈的教子；Lena把鸟埋在院子里，留下那只笼子。春天，笼子里长出一丛野草，Lena没有拔。

  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 62 -> 74; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 33 -> 25; reputation -0.03; mood -0.040000000000000036_

  亲戚的婚礼上，Lena被安排和伴侣分开坐。司仪问有没有人反对，Lena低头喝汤；伴侣在另一桌替别人倒酒，手很稳，酒没有洒出来。散席时，两个人在衣帽间碰见，只说了一句“外面下雨”。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 74 -> 84; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.grief 4 -> 8; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1943.03** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.organ-grinder/organ-grinder.listen-coins` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, music, street
  琴箱的螺丝松了，每转一圈都发出吱呀声，可圆舞曲还是圆舞曲。她靠着墙听完，看见三楼窗台有人用绳子吊下一只小篮子收钱，绳子在风里晃。她摸出铜板时，猴子已经蹲在她脚边了。
  _effects: cash -0.02 (给手摇风琴师的赏钱); mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1943.07** `r2-rural.typhus::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/nursed-the-village` tags: rural, epidemic, medicine, neighbor, fear, vienna, urban, crisis
  Gustav Kral在村口撒石灰，说斑疹伤寒已经进了第三户。Lena把家里的旧床单撕成绷带，挨家送热水。Emil烧得说胡话，Lena用湿布擦他的额头，擦到天亮。
  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (村口的石灰线); reputation 0.1; flag +nursed_typhus_patients; role_

- **1943.12** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.cousin-bed/turn-away-with-fare` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Lena直说家里腾不出地方，从抽屉里摸出半克朗车费和一块黑面包，塞给Rosa Winter。Rosa Winter的藤箱提手已经磨毛，在门槛上磕了一下。Lena关上门，听见脚步声沿楼梯往下，看门人在楼下问了一句，Rosa Winter答得含含糊糊；厨房里少了一块面包，也少了一个本该住下的人。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (给表亲车费); food; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001463:child; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1944.01** `state_expire:ld18-impossible-attachment` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  Lena的「无法登记的伴侣」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:131; flag -impossible_attachment_active_

- **1944.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.skip-both` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Lena在影戏棚和音乐厅之间走了两趟，最后哪处都没进。Lena拐进常去的酒馆，花三十赫勒要了半升啤酒，听角落里有人拉琴，拉得不准但很响。Otto Winter找到Lena时，桌上的杯子已经空了一半，省下的票钱还在口袋里叮当响；这一晚没看成戏，倒把半升啤酒喝出了几分自在。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (酒馆半升啤酒); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation friend with person-otto-winter_

- **1944.06** `r13-turn.remarriage::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/engagement-collapsed` tags: family, remarriage, marriage, widowhood, household, vienna, urban, crisis
  婚礼前一周，Josef的女儿从外地赶来，说父亲的养老金不能落在外人手里。Josef沉默了很久，把戒指收回盒子。Lena没有哭，只把已经写好的请客名单一张张撕掉，撕到孩子的名字时停了一下，还是撕了。
  _effects: cash -2 (旧戒指第二次戴上); condition; flag +remarriage_called_off; relation rival with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912_

- **1944.09** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Lena在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Mitzi Winter却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Lena把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000700:child_

- **1944.09** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.sheet-quarrel` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Lena发现自己搭在栏杆上的床单被人挪到了绳尾，角上沾了煤灰，一时火起，在楼梯间拦住Friedrich Winter问是谁动的。Friedrich Winter说是Ferdinand Berg 2嫌滴水，两人越说越响，最后各自回屋摔上门，炉子空着，谁也没再去烧水。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002251:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1944.10** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.river-walk/river-walk.bridge` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, walk, river
  Lena在桥边站了一会儿，看一艘平底船靠岸卸煤，几个工人用铁锹往岸上甩。风把煤灰吹到她脸上，她抹了一把，往回走时脚步比来时轻，回到巷口还给自己买了块三赫勒的糖。
  _effects: mood 0.06000000000000005; cash -0.03 (买了三赫勒的糖)_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/postwar-illness` tags: war, liberation, 1945, injury
  Lena在战后第二个月病倒，医院没有足够的药，医生只给了退烧粉。邻居每天送来一碗汤，放在门口就走。
  _effects: health -0.15000000000000002 (postwar illness); cash -4 (medicine); condition_

- **1945.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Lena在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Alois Winter 4的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-132_

- **1945.01** `task-vienna-family-care.reconcile-siblings/soup-and-grave` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  忌日那天，Lena在教堂旁的饭桌上先给Hedwig盛了一碗汤，把分家时那只柜子的事提了出来，说完就不再辩解。Hedwig用勺子搅了两圈，讲了自己当时欠的账。饭吃到一半，两人一起去墓地，路上买了半克朗的蜡烛。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (墓前的蜡烛); mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1945.05** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.keep` tags: home, market, food, social
  扁豆汤的香味从门缝飘出去，Friedrich Winter敲了门。Lena掀开锅盖又盖上，说只够自家吃。Friedrich Winter什么也没说就走了。夜里Lena听见隔壁有人用勺子刮碗底。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002251:child; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1946.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction, partnership, household
  Alois Winter 4在几天后找到Lena，说可以试试。两人开始合付房租，也把各自欠的账摊在桌上。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-132; household; role; role; interaction_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011553_

- **1946.01** `sm18-object.tram-lost::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/accuses-the-conductor` tags: object, loss, child, tram, family, everyday, vienna, urban
  Lena认定是电车售票员把纸包收进了自己的口袋，在站台上拦住他，声音大得整条街都回头。售票员把票夹和口袋都翻出来，里面只有几张旧票和一只坏表。Franz Hofer站在后面，把脸埋进围巾里。
  _effects: state_add sm18-missing-object state:world-vienna-1912:650; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; reputation -0.03; mood -0.01000000000000012; flag +accused_the_tram_conductor; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; flag +something_is_missing_

- **1946.01** `r13-fam.write-missing-relative::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/letter-sent` tags: task, family, letter, missing, war, literacy, vienna, urban
  Lena替Rosa Winter 2写信，把最后知道的部队番号、收容站和一处旧住址都写上去。Rosa Winter 2不识字，却要Lena把“家里都好”念三遍，怕写错。信投进邮筒时，她伸手在筒口摸了一下；Lena把回信地址又描粗了一笔。
  _effects: cash -1 (没有地址的信); flag +wrote_to_missing_relative; skill; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; knowledge “A letter with the last known unit and shelter addresses was ”_

- **1946.08** `bc18-inherit.forty-seven-franz::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/bury-the-stranger` tags: inheritance, mistaken_identity, debt, funeral, work, absurd, family, vienna
  遗嘱里还附了一笔丧葬费，条件是要有“家属”到场。死者没有亲人，Lena只好穿黑衣站在墓旁，听牧师念一个从未听过的名字。雨把花圈上的纸花打湿，泥水漫过鞋面；葬礼结束后，Grete Moser 2把九克朗交给他，说账已结清。Lena回家脱鞋，发现袜子上沾着别人的土。
  _effects: cash 7 (以家属身份到场后的遗产余款); reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; condition; flag +buried_a_stranger_for_the_estate; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85_

- **1946.09** `hs18-queue.refugee::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/give-a-false-address` tags: refugee, queue, station, papers, official, history, vienna, urban
  Lena把隔壁空屋的门牌号写在表格上。Mitzi Winter 2看了一眼，居然没有追问。Rosa Lechner千恩万谢，提着箱子往那条街走；Lena没有提醒那屋已经塌了半边，只把围巾拉高，转身去赶电车。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-rosa-lechner; knowledge “空屋的门牌号也能通过登记。”; flag +gave_false_refugee_address_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Lena把Anna Stein的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Anna Stein今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Lena听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 28; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Lena把Emil Stein的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Emil Stein今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Lena听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 28; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:sm18-missing-object/another-drawer/a-receipt-in-the-lining` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  半年过去，Lena又翻了一遍那只装旧纽扣的铁盒，在盒盖夹层里摸到一小片发黄的收据。上面的日期和那件东西最后一次出现的那天对得上，但字迹已经淡得只剩半个印章。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 5 -> 11; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 45 -> 43; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1947.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club/p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club.ask-fare` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:social
  Lena向Josef Berg 2问清下个月郊外演唱会的车费由谁出，Josef Berg 2说团里只贴一半，另一半要团员自己凑。Lena把四十赫勒硬币在指间转了两圈，最终没有放进收款盒，说等发了工钱再来；这一来一去，也算清了半票、酒棚和车钱各是多少。出门时Grete Moser 2正扛着啤酒桶进后厅，两人侧身让过。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; skill_

- **1947.02** `ml18-luck.strongbox-in-the-wall::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-notes-are-still-good` tags: money, luck, housing, windfall, inflation, ownership, vienna, urban
  泥瓦匠敲开卧室的旧墙，掉出一只锈死的铁盒；里面是一叠旧纸币、一枚银扣和一张当票。Alois Winter 4闻声上楼，说墙是他家的，盒子也该归他。Lena把当票翻过来，看见上面写着自己外祖母的名字，两个人都安静了一会儿。
  _effects: cash 45 (old banknotes found in the wall); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:677; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-132; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89; flag +found_strongbox; knowledge “铁盒里的当票写着外祖母的名字，纸币是很多年前的旧版。”; mood 0.06000000000000005; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.02; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1947.05** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Lena傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Alois Winter 4在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Alois Winter 4敲开Lena的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-132; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1947.08** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.market-closing/market-closing.share-spinach` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, market, food, bargain
  两人同时伸手，又同时缩回去。Katharina Hofer 2说自己本来想买回去做馅，Lena说那正好一人一半。摊主用草绳把菜捆好，嘴里嘟囔着收摊了收摊了，把剩下的零钱扒拉进木匣。
  _effects: cash -0.04 (分买最后一把菠菜); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69_

  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Lena的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Lena家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 2 -> 3; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: cash -5 (板车与押金收据); flag +move_arranged_with_cart; knowledge “the new landlord writes receipts without an address”_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 28 -> 31; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  七月最后一天，区公所墙上贴出黄色告示，Gustav Kral用尺子把纸压平，念到“全体预备役”时，街上的人已经围了三层。Lena挤到前面，看见自己的出生年份被划在一行小字里。身后的乐队开始奏进行曲，有人跟着唱，有人往回走。
  _effects: condition; flag +read_mobilization_notice; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; knowledge “the poster lists reservists by birth year, not by name”_

- **1948.07** `df18-mark.first-appearance::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/fever-hand` tags: body, mark, omen, illness, rumor, supernatural, eerie, vienna
  一连三天的高烧退下去以后，Lena的手腕上多了一块暗褐色的印子，边缘像五根很短的手指。诊所的医生说只是皮下色素，Mitzi Hofer 2却在院子里看了很久，说这形状不是随便长的。Lena把袖子拉下来，回家路上一直用另一只手按着那块皮。
  _effects: cash -1 (去诊所问这块印子); health -0.030000000000000027 (高烧后的虚弱); state_add df18-uncanny-mark state:world-vienna-1912:703; flag +df18_mark_appeared_after_fever; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99; flag +df18_mark_noticed; knowledge “我身上有一处说不清的痕迹，别人对它各有各的说法。”_

- **1948.07** `state_reaction:sm18-missing-object/a-rumor-hardens` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Lena在院子里听见两个人在说“那家的东西”，话头在看见Lena时停住。晚上，Lena把首饰盒从五斗柜挪到床边，又觉得这样更像在防谁。

  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Mitzi Stein这周主动擦了黑板。Lena把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。

  Lena在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Lena把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.04999999999999999 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）)_

- **1949.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/waiting-list` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family
  Lena去住房局问结果，办事员翻过卡片说分数不够：家里人口少，现住房虽挤但还没到拆除标准。回程的电车上，Rosa Winter 2劝他明年再递一次申请。
  _effects: role; cash -2 (application and tram fare); mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85_

- **1949.01** `df18-mark.reading-at-market::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/read-as-curse` tags: body, mark, omen, rumor, superstition, market, vienna, urban
  肉摊的Berta Stein看见Lena挽袖子，刀停在半空，说这种印子像吊死的人留下的手印。旁边买菜的人安静了一瞬，又各自低头挑菜。Lena想笑，却发现自己的手在抖；那天回家，Lena绕开了平时走的巷子。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 25 -> 40; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.claimed illness -> curse; reputation -0.039999999999999994; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-127; flag +df18_mark_read_as_curse; mood -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-127_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:sm18-missing-object/another-drawer/the-same-question` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Lena把同一只抽屉拉开又推上，里面的东西已经按原来的位置摆了三遍。楼下有人搬煤，铁锹刮过石阶；Lena忽然想不起那件东西最后一次被谁拿在手里，只觉得每个人的脸都有一点可疑。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 51 -> 59; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 17 -> 15_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-year/kept-small` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  又过了一年，Lena学会了在洗澡时不去盯那块印子。它还在，边缘有时发红，有时像普通的旧疤；街坊有了新的闲话，Lena的袖子也换了更长的款式。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 40 -> 34; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 5 -> 1; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1949.01** `r13-civ.recover-at-home::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/neighbor-soup` tags: health, recovery, care, household, food, vienna, urban, postwar
  Otto Winter端来一碗兑了水的牛奶，顺手把Lena家的炉子捅旺。Lena靠在床头，听Otto Winter讲楼下谁也被传染了，谁家把病人送去了医院。人走后，屋里还留着一点煤烟和奶味。
  _effects: cash -1 (退烧后的第一碗粥); health 0.040000000000000036 (退烧后的第一碗粥); flag +recovered_with_neighbor_help; relation friend with person-otto-winter_

- **1949.02** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/part-at-arch` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  走到桥洞前，Friedrich Winter说该往回走了，家里炉子还没生。Lena站在原地看着Friedrich Winter拐上台阶，围巾的一角被风掀起来；下周的事谁也没有提，两人就此分开，口袋里只剩一颗凉透的栗子。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002251:child; flag +uncertain_courtship_

- **1949.12** `vienna-market-household.market-credit/market-credit.delay` tags: home, market, food, social
  三个克朗拖了九天。玛丽亚收下时数都没数，只把Lena的名字从木牌上抹去，粉笔灰落在卷心菜上。Katharina Hofer 2拎着篮子从旁边过去，没停脚。
  _effects: cash -3 (延迟偿还市场赊账); food; reputation -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69_

- **1950.01** `df18-dream.water-in-the-cellar::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/ignore-the-dream` tags: dream, omen, home, water, coincidence, eerie, everyday, vienna
  Lena醒后只当是晚饭太咸，翻个身又睡了。下午，地窖的水管爆开，煤和旧箱子泡在齐膝的水里。Josef Bauer帮着舀水，Lena一边舀一边想起梦里的水位，正好到第二个台阶。
  _effects: cash -4 (泡坏的煤和旧箱子); health -0.030000000000000027 (在冷水里舀了一下午); condition; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; knowledge “我没有理会梦，地窖真的淹了。”; flag +df18_ignored_flood_dream_

- **1950.01** `r1-hc.pickpocket::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/pickpocket-caught` tags: street, pickpocket, tram, theft, wages, vienna, urban, postwar
  Lena一把抓住那只手，是个穿短外套的少年。Katharina Weiss不挣扎，只把钱包举起来，说里面只有三克朗；车厢里的人全看着Lena，没有人说话，车铃在下一站响了一声。
  _effects: cash -1 (电车上的第三只手); condition; role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-145; knowledge “the boy returned the wallet but kept the coins”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-145_

  入冬后，Lena在集市上听见一个陌生女人向别人描述“那个有手印的人”。故事已经比原来多了一个雨夜和一声尖叫。Lena低头挑菜，发现自己的手正按着袖口。

  邻居老太太的屋里有一股樟脑味，她把孩子放在膝上颠了颠，说先试一星期。Lena每周付一克朗，早晨把孩子裹好送过去，晚上接回来时孩子手里攥着老太太给的干面包皮。
  _effects: cash -1 (每周付邻居老太太的照看费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; health -0.030000000000000027 (孩子夜里哭醒，{subject}睡眠不足); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119_

- **1950.10** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Lena在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Luise Weiss 2却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Lena把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104_

- **1950.11** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/keys-and-photo-returned` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, separation
  院子里晾着三条床单，风一过就拍到人脸上。Lena把备用钥匙和一张照相馆拍的合影交还，Maria Lechner把那件借来的外套叠好递回。两人说好各找各的住处，谁都没有提高声音；分手分得干净，可两个人心里都空了一块。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003489:child; flag +courtship_ended_cleanly_

- **1951.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-female/private-household` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, housing, lifecycle, partnership, household
  Lena和Helene Berg 3合租一间房，对外说是表姐妹。两人共用一只炉子、一本账和一张床，白天各自上工，晚上把门闩好；房租从两人的工钱里各出一半，日子紧，却比各自的旧住处暖，窗台上还并排晾着两只杯子。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-150; household; mood 0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-150_

  Friedrich Kral 2把账本塞进钢琴后面的夹层，说等风声过去再拿出来。Gustav Kral第二次来时带了一个记录员和一张搜查令；钢琴被挪开时，琴键响了一串不成调的音。Lena看见黑皮本翻开的那一页上，已经用红笔圈出了三笔利息。

  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 44 -> 54; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 7 -> 13; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.claimed curse -> curse; reputation -0.01999999999999999; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1951.01** `p9-work-career.clerk-post/p9-work-career.clerk-post.archive` tags: work, career, craft, clerk, education, task, office, application
  Lena的信写得好，笔画干净，算盘也快，可速记一栏空了大半。Mitzi Winter 2把卷子批了“文书可用，速记欠熟”，人事科把他安排到档案室抄旧保单，日薪两克朗四十赫勒，比正式办事员少一截。桌旁没有窗，只有一盏悬在头顶的灯，他抄到下班时右肩发僵。
  _effects: cash 2.4 (档案室抄写日薪); skill; health -0.020000000000000018 (阴面档案室坐久了肩颈发硬); flag +awaiting_clerk_slot_

- **1951.03** `bc18-crime.failed-burglary::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/right-office-wrong-drawer` tags: crime, burglary, mistake, absurd, police, legal, comic, vienna
  他们终于摸到公证处的后窗，撬开的抽屉里却没有遗嘱，只有一叠慈善捐款收据，数目和报纸上公布的对不上。Lena把收据塞进外套，找到一位律师，换回七克朗；律师说，这不是盗窃，是取证。Friedrich Lechner 2问那遗嘱怎么办，Lena说，先让死人再等一等。
  _effects: cash 7 (把慈善收据交给律师的酬劳); knowledge “公证处抽屉里的慈善捐款收据与报纸公布的数目对不上。”; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126; condition; flag +stole_charity_receipts; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126_

  Franz Moser趁Lena不在，把租约上的名字改成了自己。Lena回家时，门口多了一只不属于自己的箱子；Franz Moser站在门里，说房东已经同意。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121; flag +home_taken_by_betrayal; state_add grief state:world-vienna-1912:787; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121_

  Hedwig想把钱拿去盘下街角的小酒馆，Lena却把十五克朗直接交到学校，替孩子续了下一年的学费。Hedwig把餐巾摔在桌上，说这家人永远只会把钱埋进土里。Helene Berg 3没有劝，只把汤碗往Lena那边推了推。

- **1952.01** `state_tick:grief/anniversary/overwhelmed` tags: grief, death, family, mind, state, state_tick
  Lena在整理旧衣服时突然停下来。那天剩下的活没有做完。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; state_parameter grief.years 0 -> 1_

- **1952.01** `r13-fam.visit-grave::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/flowers-placed` tags: task, family, grave, mourning, neighbor, grief, vienna, urban
  Lena陪Elisabeth Bauer去公墓，把一把野菊放在新翻的土上。木牌上的名字被雨冲得发白，Elisabeth Bauer用手指一笔一画描了一遍，描到最后一笔，指腹上全是泥。Lena没有劝，只蹲下来把歪掉的木牌扶正。
  _effects: cash -1 (公墓新翻的土); condition; flag +visited_a_grave_with_neighbour; role_

- **1952.06** `bc18-ghost.death-certificate::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/collect-the-benefit` tags: bureaucracy, legal, death, identity, mistake, paperwork, absurd, vienna
  邻居出主意说，死者家属可以领一笔丧葬补助。Lena让Anna Bauer冒充远房表姐，自己在旁边按了手印；钱到手后，两人在厨房里分成三份，第三份留着买煤。第二个星期，Emil Berg寄来一封信，说抚恤金已经核准，请家属来拍一张遗像；Lena把信压在盐罐下面，盐受潮结了块。
  _effects: cash 4 (冒领的丧葬补助分成); state_add bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:827; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; condition; flag +collected_own_funeral_benefit; mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +bureaucratic_ghost; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1952.07** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.defer` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Lena把抽屉拉开又推上，铜币在木板上滚了半圈。Emil Weiss合上本子说，拖欠的人不少，可规矩就是规矩。她走下两级台阶又回头提醒，发工钱那天她会在教堂侧门等着。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

- **1952.12** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.labour-exchange/exchange-swap-tips` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, labour_exchange, bureaucracy
  台阶上蹲着几个等消息的人，Gustav Bauer 2把烟盒纸摊在膝盖上画路线，说北站货栈的管事认字就行，不认登记所那一套。Lena把那半截铅笔头借来，在纸角写上时辰，折好收进内袋。
  _effects: flag +has_work_lead; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-148; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-148_

- **1953.01** `bc18-ghost.pension-window::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/prove-alive` tags: bureaucracy, pension, identity, legal, paperwork, absurd, poverty, vienna
  Lena花了三天跑教堂、区公所和旧雇主，最后拿到一张六个月内有效的“在世证明”。Hedwig把证明压在玻璃板下，说下次再死，最好提前通知。养老金恢复了，每月两克朗；Lena走出门，阳光照在证明的钢印上，像一枚刚刚盖好的出生章。
  _effects: cash 2 (恢复发放的养老金); state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 35 -> 45; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 40 -> 37; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; condition; flag +six_month_living_certificate; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.01** `state_reaction:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/another-file-opened` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  Lena刚在一个窗口证明自己活着，另一个窗口又从抽屉里抽出一张死亡通知。两个办事员隔着走廊互相看了一眼，谁也没有先开口；Lena站在中间，忽然明白自己不是一个人，而是一份正在被转抄的卷宗。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 37 -> 49; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.duplicates 2 -> 3; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.sightings 2 -> 3; flag +another_file_opened; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/another-window-opens` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  一个税务员把Lena的死亡记录和一张新房租收据订在一起，寄到了另一个区。三个星期后，Lena收到一封要求“已故纳税人”补缴暖气税的信；去问时，窗口说档案正在路上，让Lena先等通知。通知没有来，房租却涨了。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 45 -> 52; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 49 -> 59; cash -1 (跑窗口的车钱); flag +heating_tax_for_the_dead; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Anton Winter在楼道里拦住Lena，问家里那张盖红章的纸是不是出了事。Lena说只是旧房租收据，回家却把纸从柜底翻出来，和Hedwig一起塞进米缸夹层。两人把米重新铺平，Hedwig的手一直在抖；Lena说这事到此为止，谁也别再提。
  _effects: condition; flag +hid_family_paper_in_rice_bin; role; knowledge “the paper is hidden between the rice bin’s layers”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114_

  学务督察按着新户籍找上门，说有一名适龄儿童从未入学。Lena躲在阁楼里，Ferdinand Bauer 2在楼下说孩子出疹子，不能见风；督察留下了一张入学通知和一瓶鱼肝油。晚上，Lena从阁楼下来，看见通知上写着“七岁”，忽然觉得自己这一生确实该重新开始。

  _effects: cash -2 (洗衣房里那句话); health -0.040000000000000036 (洗衣房里那句话); condition; flag +laundry_brawl; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002251:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-123_

- **1954.03** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Lena把Hermann Bauer和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Lena把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1954.07** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/keys-and-photo-returned` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, separation
  院子里晾着三条床单，风一过就拍到人脸上。Lena把备用钥匙和一张照相馆拍的合影交还，Helene Berg 3把那件借来的外套叠好递回。两人说好各找各的住处，谁都没有提高声音；分手分得干净，可两个人心里都空了一块。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-150_

- **1954.09** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Lena看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Rosa Huber 2追出来问怎么不领了，Lena说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Lena把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; food_

  Anton Kral 2起身去添灯油时，外套从椅背上滑下来，掉出一叠按姓名排好的卡片。Lena看见自己的名字在最上面，下面写着“丧偶，铜纽扣，易感”。Anton Kral 2回来时，Lena已经把卡片放回原处，也把剩下的钱放回了口袋。
  _effects: cash -1 (已经付出去的车马钱); mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-137; flag +fm17_seance_fraud_seen; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-137_

  Lena陪Helene Berg 3去公墓，把一把野菊放在新翻的土上。木牌上的名字被雨冲得发白，Helene Berg 3用手指一笔一画描了一遍，描到最后一笔，指腹上全是泥。Lena没有劝，只蹲下来把歪掉的木牌扶正。
  _effects: cash -1 (公墓新翻的土); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-150_

  学徒在旁边小声说，拒绝按手印的人名字会被单独抄在一张纸上。Lena没接话，把八克朗换成一克朗的硬币，数给家里时硬币在桌上滚了两圈才停。屋里的煤油灯芯剪得太短，光只够照亮手边的桌面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.030000000000000027; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105_

  等到第九天，Lena把没寄出的草稿和那封写了一半的信一起塞进炉子。纸卷起来，火苗发蓝，屋里一股焦味；Lena拿火钩把灰捅散，决定不再去问信的下落。炉门上的铁片烫手，那个“亲”字先没了。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +gave_up_on_letter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-163; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-163_

- **1955.10** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.sick_child_doorstep/borrows_coal_risk` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  Lena把自家煤箱里仅剩的四块煤分了两块给Rosa Berg，自己家那一夜没生火，裹着大衣睡的。孩子烧退了，Rosa Berg第二天还煤时多塞了一把粗盐。Lena当天下午就开始流清鼻涕，第三天咳得直不起腰。
  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (受凉咳嗽); food_

  孤儿院门口排着领孩子的队伍，Alois Lechner 3把Luise Moser推到Lena面前，说这孩子夜里不哭，只是不肯吃饭。Lena蹲下来，把带来的半块糖放在孩子手心；孩子没有吃，先把糖纸抹平，夹进衣袋。那天下午，Lena在登记簿上按了手印。
  _effects: cash -4 (门口多出来的一双小鞋); flag +fostered_postwar_orphan; role; relation guardian with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:011350:child; knowledge “the child hoards smooth paper and sugar wrappers”; household; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-159_

- **1956.03** `ml18-investment.transylvanian-mine::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-mine-strikes-gold` tags: investment, mining, fraud, money, risk, business, vienna, urban
  Franz Hofer 2把一小截岩芯放在桌上，说特兰西瓦尼亚的矿脉比图纸上厚三倍，只是缺一台抽水机。Mitzi Winter 2验过矿样，Lena把积蓄的一半投进去。八个月后，第一批金砂运到维也纳，Lena在交易所门口看见自己的名字被写在行情板上，后面跟着一个从未见过的数字。
  _effects: cash 90 (the first gold shipment from the mine); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:960; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-162; reputation 0.04999999999999999; flag +mine_struck_gold; mood 0.09999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-162; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1956.04** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.enroll-first` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  来问入会的是个瘦高的年轻人，帽檐捏在手里，说自己在第十六区的织布厂做了半年。Lena把名册翻到空白页，蘸水笔尖在“住址”那栏停了一下，等他报出街名。老成员在角落里把账本合上，声音很轻。
  _effects: cash 1 (新会员的入会费先记在桌上); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140_

  婚礼在借来的房间里举行，证人是洗衣房的Helene Kral 2。戒指是从当铺买的，誓词写在洗衣单背面；Helene Kral 2念到“无论贫穷还是疾病”时，楼下有人敲门，三个人同时屏住呼吸。门开了，是来收煤气费的。
  _effects: state_add ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:961; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-145; cash -1 (the pawnshop rings and the gas bill); mood 0.05999999999999994; flag +private_wedding_rite; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; flag +impossible_attachment_active_

- **1956.10** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Lena把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Mitzi Moser 2抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Lena把床单对折，腾出一半，Mitzi Moser 2用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1957.01** `ml18-family.fortune-split::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/split-it-equally` tags: family, money, windfall, inheritance, conflict, dark, vienna, urban
  星期天的晚饭多摆了一副刀叉。Hedwig带着一本记账簿来，说钱是大家的运气，不该只落在一个人家里。Lena把二十四克朗分成三份，Hedwig数了两遍；Katharina Weiss把烤肉端上桌，谁也没有再提那本账簿。
  _effects: cash -24 (the windfall was divided among the family); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 50 -> 26; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 3 -> 5; flag +fortune_split_equally; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1957.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/the-date-that-is-not-on-paper` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  Lena和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Lena在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 70 -> 74; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 15 -> 12; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1957.01** `r14-soc.club-election::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/disputed-the-count` tags: task, club_election, club, status, community, vienna, urban, postwar
  Lena输了五票，要求把票重新数一遍。数到第二遍还是五票，Alois Winter 5把铁盒盖上，说“再数就难看了”。Lena说账本也得查，后厅一下子安静下来。那晚的合唱没有唱成。散会后，Alois Winter 5把铁盒抱在怀里，锁进酒馆老板的柜子。铁盒被放回柜子，账本却留在了桌上。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; reputation -0.04999999999999999; flag +disputed_club_election; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-138_

- **1957.06** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-help` tags: faith, social, family, home
  楼梯间只点一根蜡烛。Elisabeth Kral 2凑过来低声说，楼下老寡妇的喘病又重了，教区明早才能派修女来。Lena把汤碗接过去，碗底还温着。
  _effects: condition; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131_

- **1957.11** `r3-mig.refugee-aid-society::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/police-raid-on-society` tags: migration, relief, organization, charity, risk, vienna, urban, postwar
  两个便衣从后门进来，Franz Moser把名册摊在桌上，一页页翻。Rosa Moser说名册上只有领汤的人，Franz Moser说“领汤的人也要登记原籍”。地下室的锅被踢翻，豆汤流到门槛。Lena从煤槽爬出去时，听见Rosa Moser在里屋咳嗽。
  _effects: mood -0.12; condition; flag +police_raid_on_aid_society; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-111; knowledge “the police take the society’s membership ledger”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-111_

- **1914.03** `iw18-infancy.first-word::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-dead-grandmothers-name` tags: infancy, first_word, memory, family, wonder, eerie, vienna, urban
  Anton扶着桌沿站起来，指着门口的空处，清清楚楚叫出一个名字。屋里没人教过这个词；Josefine Hofer手里的铝勺停在半空。那是去年冬天埋掉的老人的名字，窗外正好有送煤的车经过，铁轮压过石缝。
  _effects: mood -0.03; knowledge “孩子说出的第一个词，是去年死去的祖母的名字。”; flag +first_word_dead_name_

- **1914.06** `statistical_infection`
  Anton在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1914.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Anton的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1914.12** `statistical_recovery`
  Anton熬过了这场肺炎，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-refused-nuggets` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  Anton空手站在窗口，听掌柜说“叫你Friedrich Hofer来”，耳朵一直红到脖子。后墙的渣堆冻得硬，Anton用鞋尖刨开表层，把煤核一颗颗捡进围裙。回家路上碰到同巷的孩子，Anton把纸袋往身后藏，进门才交给Friedrich Hofer。
  _effects: mood -0.04; food; skill_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单太长，Anton拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Helene Winter弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Anton接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.049999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-helene-winter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-half-credit` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  “月底一起结。”掌柜说完，把半筐煤推到柜台外。Anton用胸口顶住筐沿往家挪，煤灰落进领口，手心被麻绳磨得发亮。Josefine Hofer在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口等到人，先看了看筐底，再看了看Anton的手，把漏掉的碎渣扫进簸箕。
  _effects: food; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单浸了碱水，沉得拽不动。Anton站在木凳上，把湿布一点点递过绳，水顺着肘弯滴进袖口。Elisabeth Huber在绳头数夹子，最后一条挂好，从围裙口袋里摸出三枚赫勒，按进Anton掌心。晚上洗手时，虎口那道口子被水一蜇，Anton才看见。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍从面包房门口拐进巷子，Anton把布袋夹在腋下，学着前面人的样子，用胳膊肘顶住后面人的篮子。窗口的伙计舀面粉时，Anton踮脚看着秤，直到半袋面倒进袋口。挤出人群时鞋跟被踩掉，Anton单脚跳了两步，在泥里摸回鞋，索性赤脚走回家。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1922.04** `vienna-childhood-street.washhouse-fever/fever-recover` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  洗衣房里全是蒸汽和碱水味。Anton靠在墙边等Josefine Hofer，湿衣服堆在脚边。夜里Anton开始发烧，Josefine Hofer把湿布叠成长条，敷在额头，凉了再换。三天后热度退下去，Anton的嘴唇起了一层白皮，Josefine Hofer的手指也裂了口子。
  _effects: condition; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1922.11** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Anton想从床单底下钻过去，脚下一滑，膝盖磕在石板棱上。Josefine Hofer听见哭声出来，先看了看伤口，又回去端水。手帕扎得很紧，Anton试着站起来，被Josefine Hofer按回台阶上。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Ferdinand Bauer家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Anton递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Ferdinand Bauer端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Anton把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1923.06** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-trade` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  “三张换一张。”Emil把皇帝像在Anton眼前晃了晃。Anton数出三张攒了半年的烟画，一张张递过去。皇帝像到手还没捂热，Emil已经推开杂货铺的门，用同一张卡换了糖。Anton把卡举到光下看，觉得皇帝的脸有点歪。
  _effects: mood 0.04; relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912_

- **1923.08** `p9-childhood-household.strap-by-the-door/neighbor-interrupted` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  Emil Weiss探头进来时，Josefine Hofer手里的皮带还没放下。Josefine Hofer把它挂回门后，改口数落Anton，声音一阵高一阵低。Emil Weiss接过盐，站了一会儿才走。Anton缩在灶台后面，等脚步声下了楼，才慢慢挪出来。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; mood -0.029999999999999992; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1924.01** `task-vienna-poor-apprentice.foreman-introduction/foreman-records-name` tags: adolescence, apprentice, work, education, poverty, printshop, task, task:work
  工头说暂时没有空缺，但答应把他的名字记在招工簿上。亲戚在旁边补了两句好话，工头只点点头；他走出来时，招工簿还摊在门房的木台上。
  _effects: flag +name_in_hiring_book; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; mood -0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43_

- **1925.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.ask-for-trial/too-young-refused` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:work
  Alois Hofer把Anton的袖子往上捋了捋，看了看手腕，说骨头还没长开，搬不动整摞的纸。Anton没走，蹲在门口把被风刮散的纸边捡回筐里。Alois Hofer给了两个赫勒，挥手赶人；Anton沿着街把另外两家作坊的门牌记下，才回家。
  _effects: cash 0.2 (替作坊捡纸边得到的两个赫勒); mood -0.039999999999999994; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27_

- **1926.01** `r13-work.part-time::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/errands-after-school` tags: work, part_time, school, adolescence, cash, reform, medical, schooling
  Maria Kral让Anton放学后送两趟货，按趟给钱，天黑前必须回铺子交账。Anton把书包挂在柜台后面，提着篮子穿过三条街；第一趟送错了一户，第二趟跑得鞋底发热。收工时Maria Kral数了三个铜板，说明天还来。
  _effects: cash 2 (放学后的四个钟头); flag +work13_took_part_time_errands; relation colleague with person-maria-kral; employment Laufbursche @ clinic:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11-evt:world-vienna-1912:001925; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

- **1926.03** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/keep-it-in-the-satchel` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, reform, medical
  Anton把手套塞进书包最里层，回家后放在枕头下面。第二天上学，Anton每隔一会儿就摸一下书包带，像在确认一件不能给家里看见的东西；手套上还留着电车木座的气味。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:85; flag +yf18_kept_glove; mood 0.020000000000000004; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1926.12** `yf18-body.danube-cabin::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/stay-behind-the-curtain` tags: body, desire, river, swimming, shame, first_love, reform, medical
  Anton把浴巾裹到下巴，说自己怕水。Lena在门外笑，说去年还见Anton从木塔上跳下去；Anton没有解释，只隔着板缝看Friedrich Stein把水撩到肩上。回家时，Anton的泳衣还是干的，衣角被手攥得起了皱。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:86; flag +yf18_stayed_dry; mood -0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

- **1927.01** `state_expire:yf18-first-awakening` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Anton的「第一次心动」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:85; flag -yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Anton在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Friedrich Stein。这一次对方先点了头，Anton准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Anton才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 40 -> 45; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 0 -> 8; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; mood 0.03_

- **1927.01** `r13-civ.first-aid-course::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/course-cancelled` tags: health, first_aid, course, skill, school, reform, medical, schooling
  Elisabeth Huber来了两次，第三次教室门锁着，黑板上写着“课程暂停”。Anton和Elisabeth Bauer在走廊里等了一刻钟，最后把绷带卷带回教室，互相缠着玩。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; flag +first_aid_course_cancelled_

- **1928.01** `yf18-firstlook.essay-exam::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-teacher-keeps-it` tags: first_love, exam, essay, school, humiliation, reform, medical, schooling
  德语作文题是《我的家乡》。Anton写了半页多瑙河，剩下半页写一个总在电车上看书的人。Helene Berg没有打分，只在页边写“感情太多，论据太少”，把作文折好放进自己的公文包；放学后，Anton看见Josef在门口等，忽然不敢走过去。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 45 -> 51; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 3; skill; reputation -0.01; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1928.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/rehearsed-conversation` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Anton在厨房里对着镜子练了七遍开场白，第二天却在离Friedrich Stein三步远的地方拐进面包店。Anton买了一袋并不需要的盐，回家路上把开场白又想了一遍；这一次，连标点都更熟练了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 54 -> 60; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 3 -> 6; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1928.01** `state_reaction:yf18-first-awakening/exam-week-distraction` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  考试周里，Anton把课本摊在桌上，眼睛却一直看着窗外那条电车线。笔记的页边多了几个没有写完的名字，Anton用橡皮擦掉，纸面起了毛。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 60 -> 57; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 6 -> 8_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003398_

- **1928.01** `kernel-work.task-apprenticeship-place::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::work-apprentice-reform-office/work-apprentice-subsidized` tags: task, work, apprenticeship, training, placement, reform, medical, schooling
  Alois Lechner翻过学校成绩单，把Anton的名字写进市立学徒名册：介绍费由市政补贴，师傅每月多领两克朗带徒津贴。Elisabeth Huber原来嫌麻烦，看到津贴才点头。合同仍是三年，头一年月钱十四克朗；Anton把盖章的通知拿回家，母亲把它压在镜子下面。
  _effects: employment Lehrling @ org-winter-print; education apprenticeship; role; cash 8 (市政学徒安置补贴); relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; flag +municipal_apprenticeship_

- **1928.08** `yf18-dare.bridge-rail::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/climb-back-down` tags: dare, bridge, river, friendship, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton的手已经搭上栏杆，听见桥下有驳船拉笛，忽然把手收回来。Rosa Bauer骂了一句，Emil却把围巾解下来递给Anton；三个人在桥头站了一会儿，谁也没有再提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 51 -> 54; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 8 -> 3; relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1929.01** `vienna-life-young-worker/joins-union` tags: adolescence, labour, union
  工会的人在厂门口拦住Anton，说学徒工也该有工票和保险。Anton每月交一克朗会费，把会员证塞进上衣内袋。
  _effects: role; cash -1 (union dues（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

- **1929.01** `yf18-firstlook.parish-dance::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/friend-cuts-in` tags: first_love, dance, church, courtship, jealousy, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa Berg在第二支曲子前把Emil请走，说Anton答应过要替她看管外套。Anton抱着一件不属于自己的大衣站在圣像下面，看他们在蜡烛光里转圈；回家路上，Anton把舞卡撕成小片，扔进多瑙运河。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 57 -> 62; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 3 -> 0; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 8 -> 13; relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.04000000000000001_

  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 59 -> 64; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 0 -> 8; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated true -> true; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; mood 0.03_

- **1929.01** `r13-civ.learn-instrument::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/self-taught` tags: culture, music, instrument, learning, craft, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton没有钱请老师，就趴在窗边听隔壁的留声机，再把听到的调子按在琴弦上。手指磨出茧子，音还是不准，但他第一次拉完了一整支曲子。
  _effects: mood 0.03; flag +learned_instrument_by_ear; skill_

- **1929.03** `vienna-apprentice-printshop.union-leaflet/union-leaflet.caught` tags: work, apprentice, factory, social
  Anton把传单卷进裤腰，在门口被Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne叫住。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne抽出纸卷念了两行，说“印刷所不是煽动所”，把学徒补贴减半，并要Anton Hofer在排字房当众道歉。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (学徒补贴被减半（其中1.5克朗记入债务）); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61_

  Anton不敢去助产士家，也不敢问Hedwig，只在放学后绕着那条街走。第三天，Hedwig在教室门口拦住他，把一张折成小方块的化验单拍在他胸口，说“没有”，然后转身走了；Anton站在走廊里，第一次觉得一张纸也能把人压弯腰。
  _effects: cash -2 (a clinic fee paid too late（其中2克朗记入债务）); flag +yf18_avoided_scare; mood -0.05000000000000002_

  Anton在柱子后面站了两支曲子，才走过去问Katharina Kral下一支能不能一起跳。Katharina Kral把手里的手套交给Maria Kral，跟着Anton走进场子。Anton踩了两次对方的鞋尖，第三次终于跟上拍子；Maria Kral在墙边把钟点记在手袋上。
  _effects: cash -1 (乐队奏到第三支曲子（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +danced_together; role; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

- **1930.04** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/part-at-arch` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  走到桥洞前，Grete Stein说该往回走了，家里炉子还没生。Anton站在原地看着Grete Stein拐上台阶，围巾的一角被风掀起来；下周的事谁也没有提，两人就此分开，口袋里只剩一颗凉透的栗子。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; flag +uncertain_courtship_

- **1930.10** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/read-and-decline` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Anton把传单读了两遍，记下上面的地址，却没有去。第二天照常上工；午休时听见两个工友低声谈论同一场集会。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1931.01** `p9-work-career.journeyman-exam/p9-work-career.journeyman-exam.pass` tags: work, career, craft, clerk, education, task, apprentice, journeyman
  Anton在三月最后一个星期一清出那张空工作台，用铅笔在接缝处划了三条线，一块榆木抽屉板刨到发亮。Gustav Berg用拇指沿燕尾榫摸过去，没说话，只把评语写在纸上：榫严，面平，可出师。Anton向柜上付了两克朗考试费，把证明折好放进内袋，下周起工钱按伙计算。
  _effects: cash -2 (出师考试的考试费与材料费（其中2克朗记入债务）); skill; reputation 0.05; relation employer with person-gustav-berg_

- **1931.02** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.closed` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Anton把窗关上，争吵声立刻变成闷响，像隔着一层棉被。可那男人的一句话还是从缝里钻进来，她躺到床上翻来覆去，数着墙上的水渍，到很晚才睡着。
  _effects: mood -0.03_

- **1931.05** `major-education-apprenticeship-exam/exam-failed-repeat-year` tags: education, apprenticeship, work, exam, major
  装版时Anton把一行诗排倒了，Gustav Berg用指甲敲了敲纸面，说再等一年。考试费不退，收据上印着考试委员会的蓝章，Anton把它折成四折，塞进工帽的衬里。回排字房的路上，两个学徒在模仿Gustav Berg刚才那声叹气。
  _effects: cash -2 (examination fee（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.12; flag +failed_journeyman_exam; skill; relation colleague with person-gustav-berg_

- **1931.07** `p10-crisis-migration.eviction/eviction-deal-landlord` tags: adulthood, crisis, migration, war, police, eviction, refugee, housing
  房东站在楼梯转角数钱，数完把钥匙串往手心里磕了磕，说涨价这一成是行情。看门人拎着工具箱上来，锁芯换了回去，门一关，院子里的床架才搬回屋里。Anton把剩下的克朗一张张压在碟子底下。
  _effects: cash -20 (补齐部分欠租才换回门锁（其中20克朗记入债务）); mood 0.04999999999999999; flag +rent_arrears_partial; relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss_

  Anton把十四克朗的整张票子压在药费单上，说这钱不用还，只要求母亲那间朝院子的屋子以后归自己照看，钥匙也归自己管。Hedwig的咖啡杯在桌上顿了一下，说照看可以，房契上的名字另说；药费单总算从糖罐底下抽了出来，兄妹之间却多了一层账。
  _effects: cash -14 (先垫付母亲全部药费（其中14克朗记入债务）); relation kin with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; flag +claims_mother_room_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family
  Anton的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Anton看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: cash -14 (drainage and medicine（其中14克朗记入债务）); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004287_

- **1932.06** `vienna-life-work-injury/fatal-accident` tags: adulthood, labour, injury, death
  压板落下时Anton没能把手抽出来。工友把人抬到门口，等救护车到时已经没有了呼吸；Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne当天下午派人送来三十克朗抚恤金，车间里的机器停了一个钟头，又响起来，像什么都没有发生过。
  _effects: death (crushing injury at the printing press); cash 30 (employer death benefit)_

### 135 subject events

- **1917.07** `p9-childhood-household.fever-night/vinegar-compress` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  没有钱请医生，Josefine Hofer把醋瓶拎到床边，拿两只碗轮换着兑水。Hedwig烧得嘴唇起皮，Josefine Hofer用小勺喂了两口温水，又把冷布敷上去。鸡叫时热度退了一点，Hedwig睁开眼，看见Josefine Hofer趴在床沿，手指还攥着湿布。
  _effects: health 0.030000000000000027 (湿敷后退了烧); mood 0.02; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Emil Berg家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Hedwig递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Emil Berg端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Hedwig把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1918.12** `vienna-early-childhood.fever-night/doctor-bill` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  Hedwig烧得嘴唇发干，Josefine Hofer把湿布敷上去，凉了再换，撑到窗外发白才去请医生。医生把听诊器贴在Hedwig背上，留下三包药粉，收了一古尔登。Josefine Hofer从围裙内袋里摸出硬币，一枚一枚排在桌上，差一点不够。
  _effects: cash -1 (医生的诊金（其中1克朗记入债务）); health 0.06 (服药后退烧); mood -0.05000000000000002_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍拐弯时，Josefine Hofer朝Hedwig招手。Hedwig挤过去，替两家把面包领出来，纸包用不同的绳结系住。家里人晚上解开绳结，先掂了掂份量，再问价钱；Hedwig把每一包的面包数、找零和欠的铜板都说了一遍，家里人听完才把两包分别收进柜子。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer; skill_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单浸了碱水，沉得拽不动。Hedwig站在木凳上，把湿布一点点递过绳，水顺着肘弯滴进袖口。Alois Winter在绳头数夹子，最后一条挂好，从围裙口袋里摸出三枚赫勒，按进Hedwig掌心。晚上洗手时，虎口那道口子被水一蜇，Hedwig才看见。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

  _effects: food; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1922.01** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-bullied` tags: childhood, street, social, home

  Ferdinand Bauer在众人面前说Hedwig用诅咒害人。没有人接话，因为上个月Ferdinand Bauer也用同样的话说过另一个人。事情没有闹大，但Hedwig发现，从此每次走进那间屋子，都有人先看Hedwig的手。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:218; flag +accusation_echoed; mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1931.01** `state_reaction:suspected-witchcraft/another-name` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  Ferdinand Bauer在指控Hedwig的人面前添了一句话，说Hedwig曾经在夜里念过谁的名字。那句话没有证据，但登记册上又多了一行。Hedwig后来才知道，添这句话的人就是Ferdinand Bauer。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 15 -> 25; reputation -0.03; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

  一个陌生女人在电车上叫住Hedwig，说自己认识她的“那位”，还劝她趁年轻找个正经人家。Hedwig没有回答，提前一站下了车；走在回家的路上，她发现自己的步子越来越快，像在躲一个并不存在的人。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 41 -> 49; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 45 -> 49; reputation -0.03; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1931.01** `major-task.scholarship-application/half-fee-place` tags: task, task:education, education, scholarship, school
  委员会只肯减一半学费。Anna Berg替Hedwig争到六克朗书费，条件是走读，每天放学后替学校抄名册。名册要抄到天黑，手指常被蓝墨水染蓝。Hedwig算过车票和墨水钱，还是不够，只好在印刷厂接晚间折页活，把课本钱先挣出来。
  _effects: education higher; cash 6 (book allowance from the school fund); role; flag +works_evenings_for_school; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-depression/laid-off` tags: adulthood, depression, work, unemployment
  厂里把一张短工名单贴在厂门口。Hedwig的名字在第三行，旁边写着“下周等通知”。等到月底，通知没有来，工票也停了。
  _effects: unemployment (laid off in the depression); cash -5 (lost wages); condition_

- **1932.01** `state_expire:ld18-public-desire` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety

  又一个人站了出来，说自家的母牛在Hedwig经过之后不再产奶。Hedwig没有辩解，因为辩解本身也需要被登记。Hedwig开始绕远路去市场，每月多出几枚铜币的车钱。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.danger 20 -> 30; reputation -0.04000000000000001; cash -1 (绕远路增加的车钱); mood -0.03999999999999998_

  排在他前面的Berta Winter很快过了关。轮到他时，医生把灯挪近了些，他还是念错。出了南站，太阳才刚爬上车皮，他站在路边把揉皱的证明捋平。
  _effects: mood -0.06; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64_

  负责人说这是不信任，Hedwig回说数目对得上才叫信任。Mitzi把卷角的账页压平，指着一行重复的支出，屋里安静得能听见煤气灯嘶声。最后决定下次集会再报账。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; reputation 0.04000000000000001; skill_

- **1932.11** `vienna-market-debt.coal-credit/coal-part-cash-part-furniture` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你数出四十赫勒搁在秤盘上，说剩下的一克朗用那张旧折叠桌抵。老板撇了撇嘴，让伙计跟你上楼搬桌子。桌子腿有点晃，但木料还结实。邻居在楼道里搭了把手，账本上这一页只写了一行，没写利息。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (买煤付的部分现钱); food; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer_

  舅公家的咖啡是兑了菊苣的。Hedwig把从前的活一样样报出来，舅公听着，偶尔嗯一声。转天Rosa Huber看他搬箱子的架势，说下星期来试杂活。
  _effects: relation kin with person-berta-weiss; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; flag +trial_work_pending; mood 0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

  Elisabeth Bauer把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Hedwig留在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Hedwig在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-elisabeth-bauer; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods（其中4克朗记入债务）); mood -0.06; role_

  他把旧刨子放在工作台上，刃口的缺口在灯下看得清。Alois Lechner用拇指试了试，说先用一个月。那天起他收工后留下来磨刃，磨石上的水混着铁屑，指尖冻得发木。
  _effects: cash -2 (买磨刀石与补工具柄（其中2克朗记入债务）); flag +apprenticeship_trial; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

  _effects: cash -3 (延迟偿还市场赊账（其中3克朗记入债务）); food; reputation -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-rosa-lechner_

  _effects: cash -1 (星期六下午减少的计件工钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +accepted_hours_reform; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

  站在门口把借钱的话过了两遍，最后没有开口。转身去找了街角的搬运行，一辆板车加两个帮工，一趟一克朗五十赫勒。家里能当的都当了，现钱只够这一趟，搬完当天连买煤的钱都要现算。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (搬运行一趟的工钱（其中1.5克朗记入债务）); mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

  话在喉咙里滚了几遍，终究没有等到对方进门。门一响，有人就把紫罗兰放在门槛边，转身往街角走。花被早晨的扫帚拨到墙根，对方捡起来闻了闻，追到巷口只看见一个拐弯的背影，午后托人捎来一句话，说钱的事再算。
  _effects: cash -0.24 (一束紫罗兰（其中0.24克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77_

  后屋的桌上摊着旧报纸，Hedwig用尺子画格子，一格一户，旁边写清煤价和面包房欠条。Josef Berg 2指着第三格说那家孩子咳了整夜，先划过去。蜡烛烧到桌沿时，名单终于能看懂了。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  两人谁也没提柜子，饭吃得客气而生分。临走时Hedwig提议：和不和好另说，每年忌日就在教堂门口见一面，站十分钟也好。Lena想了一下，说行，但别指望他进屋。Hedwig把这话记在心上，回去路上把没送出去的半包烟丝塞回口袋。
  _effects: relation kin with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; flag +truce_with_sibling_

  汤是豆子熬的，表面浮着几粒油星，气味把整条巷子都占了。Elisabeth Bauer排在前面，回头替Hedwig看着位置，嘴里数着还剩几个人。铁罐递过去时烫手，Hedwig用围裙角垫着。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  巷子里卖煤的正往板车上装货，看见狗绕过来，说这狗在环城道那边就有人找。Hedwig把面包递过去时，狗用鼻子拱了拱她的掌心。男人跑来时手里还攥着半截绳子，连声道谢，说这狗是替人看院子的。
  _effects: food; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/resists` tags: politics, persecution, 1938

  _effects: condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work（其中5克朗记入债务）)_

  _effects: cash -5 (分摊母亲药费中自己的一份（其中5克朗记入债务）); relation kin with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912_

- **1939.04** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.door-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family

  _effects: health 0.01 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

  _effects: cash -0.6 (街角药房买退烧药（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73_

  _effects: cash 1 (周末帮工的工钱); relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93; flag +weekend_shop_work_

- **1941.02** `vienna-historical-social-movement.public-meeting/stay-and-tidy-chairs` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Hedwig整场没上台，散会后留下把折叠椅叠好，扫掉地上的瓜子壳。Elisabeth Winter帮着把借来的煤气灯还给店主，顺手记下谁走得最早。Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung的人问Hedwig为何不发言，Hedwig说先把椅子还齐。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001691:child; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.02** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.market-closing/market-closing.bruised-apples` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, market, food, bargain
  她蹲在菜市场湿漉漉的石板地上挑苹果，Hermann Lechner从旁边经过，说这种碰伤的煮出来最甜。她回家把苹果切成块，锅里水刚开就下了糖精片，满屋子都是煮水果的酸香。
  _effects: cash -0.1 (买收摊前的碰伤苹果（其中0.1克朗记入债务）); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

- **1941.05** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.write-off` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  窗外有人来问入会，接待的人一时不知道怎么开口，Hedwig替他说“进来坐”。老成员把账本拿回去，用一块旧布包好，放进抽屉最里面。抽屉关上的声音比平时响。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; flag +union_debt_written_off; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91_

- **1941.08** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.labour-exchange/exchange-trial-work` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, labour_exchange, bureaucracy
  介绍所里那个Alois Winter说木牌上的地址多半过时了，不如自己去问。Hedwig走了四十分钟到洗衣房，院子里晾着床单。老板娘让她先熨一件试试，看完说留下吧，明天还这个时辰来。
  _effects: cash 3 (洗衣房试工半天按件计酬); skill_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/kept-the-files-apart` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  这一年，Hedwig学会了同时带三张纸出门：一张证明活着，一张证明死了，一张证明自己从没搬过家。窗口后面的人各自只看自己那一张，偶尔还会夸Hedwig的材料齐全。年底，Hedwig从补发的配给里省下一小袋面粉，觉得自己像个熟练的走私犯。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 29 -> 33; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 15 -> 10; cash 1 (省下的配给和车钱); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1942.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.railway-depot/railway-depot.day-labor` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, railway, depot
  货场的煤烟落在肩上。Ferdinand Berg 2帮他扛第一袋时教他把腰沉下去。站务员在名册边上记了日期，说干得好才有正式名分，Hedwig点头，把袋子往肩上一顶。
  _effects: employment 货场临时装卸工 @ morgue:person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000265:child-evt:world-vienna-1912:005848; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100_

- **1942.03** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Hedwig把Alois Bauer和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Hedwig把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +tenant_collective_

- **1942.10** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/pawn-chain-only` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  链子从你指缝里滑下去，落在绒布上几乎没有声音。掌柜说两克朗四十赫勒，你点了点头。出门时你把表掏出来上了几圈弦，它还在走，只是再没法挂在马甲上了。
  _effects: cash 2.4 (典当银表链所得); mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93_

- **1943.01** `curse15-notice.the-second-illness::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/calls-it-a-fever` tags: curse, notice, illness, fear, knowledge, supernatural, vienna, urban
  医生说只是换季的烧，Hedwig便也这么说。第二次退烧后，Hedwig把那张写满日期的纸烧了，灰倒进花盆。花盆里的植物活了下来，Hedwig因此更愿意相信巧合，也不再去数下一个十一天。
  _effects: flag +curse_dismissed_as_fever; condition; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1943.01** `state_expire:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  Hedwig的「档案里的活鬼」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:460; flag -bureaucratic_ghost_

  Hedwig把数字键和字母键弄混，一份账单打错了三位数。老板把纸摔在桌上，说打字员连数都认不清。Hedwig当天被辞退，工钱扣了一半，走出门时才想起自己连那台打字机的型号都没记住。Hedwig在门口站了一会儿才走。
  _effects: cash -2 (打字机的字母盘（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.09999999999999998; unemployment (打字机的字母盘); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-124_

- **1943.07** `vienna-church-community.saint-vincent-box/box-request-granted` tags: faith, social, family, home
  Hedwig在圣文生会门口登记，领到一张面包券和一小袋土豆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1943.08** `vienna-market-debt.coal-credit/coal-scavenge-station` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你把已经掏出来的赫勒又放回口袋，说不赊了。老板哼了一声，把账本往柜台上一合。你拎着空筐跟邻居往火车站方向走，在货运站边的煤渣堆里捡了半袋碎煤和焦炭。手指冻得发僵，指甲缝里全是黑灰。
  _effects: food; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1943.08** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.caretaker-notice` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Hedwig去找看门人，说院子里四面墙，声音绕不出去。第二天楼梯间的木板上贴了一张纸：晚上十点后请勿放留声机，鞋匠铺也请收锤。纸条没有落款，只有房东事务所的戳。Josef Berg 2路过时看了很久，把留声机的盖子合上了。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1944.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.ask-trial/ask-trial.accepted` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, trial, factory
  午休的哨子刚响，Mitzi Winter 2夹着烟走到门口。Hedwig报上姓名、住址和干过的活，声音有点发紧。工头嗯了一声，说明早六点来，管一顿午饭，干得好再谈工钱。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110_

- **1944.09** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/part-at-arch` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  走到桥洞前，Grete Moser 3说该往回走了，家里炉子还没生。Hedwig站在原地看着Grete Moser 3拐上台阶，围巾的一角被风掀起来；下周的事谁也没有提，两人就此分开，口袋里只剩一颗凉透的栗子。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107; flag +uncertain_courtship; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/displaced` tags: war, liberation, 1945, migration
  炮火把Hedwig住的那条街烧掉一半。Hedwig跟着人群走到城外，在临时收容所登记，床位上铺的是发霉的稻草。
  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; role; cash -5 (temporary shelter（其中5克朗记入债务）)_

- **1945.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.relative-introduction/relative-introduction.never-mentions` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, relative, introduction
  点心纸包搁在膝上，Berta Weiss说熟人多，慢慢问，别急。Hedwig回家把这些话学给母亲听，母亲只叹了口气。那包糖后来自己家里分着吃了。
  _effects: relation kin with person-berta-weiss; cash -1 (再买一包糖上门（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1946.01** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Hedwig在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Hedwig去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter Wiens的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role_

- **1946.01** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money

  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费（其中4克朗记入债务）); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912_

  午休的哨子刚响，Alois Lechner 2夹着烟走到门口。Hedwig报上姓名、住址和干过的活，声音有点发紧。工头嗯了一声，说明早六点来，管一顿午饭，干得好再谈工钱。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

  Elisabeth Huber从围裙里掏出几张揉皱的收据，Hedwig把它们按日期排开。屋外有人上楼，脚步在门口停了一下又走了。Hedwig把新表折成四折塞进衣袋，说先管这条街，别的明天再说。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1946.05** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.closed` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Hedwig把窗关上，争吵声立刻变成闷响，像隔着一层棉被。可那男人的一句话还是从缝里钻进来，她躺到床上翻来覆去，数着墙上的水渍，到很晚才睡着。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1947.01** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.rush` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  木盆边的水渍映着天光，Hedwig跪在石阶上揉搓床单，肘弯发酸。晾绳上的湿布滴答作响，Berta Lechner探身把自家被单也挂上去，两人没多说话。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001733:child_

  房租催到门口，Hedwig把已写的章节卖给一家小报，换了八克朗，够付一个月房租和两顿热饭。长篇锁进抽屉，钥匙搁在盐罐后面，Alois Bauer问起时他只说“先不提了”，第二天照常去上工。
  _effects: cash 8 (小报买下章节的稿酬); mood -0.06000000000000005; reputation -0.020000000000000018; flag +novel_shelved_

- **1948.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.railway-depot/railway-depot.day-labor` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, railway, depot
  天色还灰着，Hedwig手里攥着几张折皱的证明。Luise Weiss 4让他念车皮上的号码，听见念对了，就朝装卸班一挥手，说先按日结钱，一天两克朗四十分。
  _effects: employment 货场临时装卸工 @ morgue:person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:009912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-135; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-135_

  Hedwig只收下篮里的面包和豆子，把煤袋推回Alois Bauer手里，说房租自己再想办法，请她们给介绍点零工。Alois Bauer把煤放在走廊窗台，说礼拜四后院有人要拆旧木箱，一赫勒一捆，工钱不多但现结。Hedwig记下门牌，答应一早过去。
  _effects: food_

- **1949.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/flat-allocated` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family, household
  等了三年，住房局终于给Hedwig一张分配单：利奥波德城一栋战后修复的公寓，两间房，走廊和水龙头仍与隔壁共用。Anna Bauer帮着把床架抬上三楼，墙上的弹孔用石灰草草抹过。
  _effects: residence; cash -10 (deposit and haulage（其中10克朗记入债务）); mood 0.050000000000000044; role_

- **1949.01** `r13-edu.typing-course::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/jammed-keys-repaired` tags: education, typing, office, work, training, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hedwig的打字机老是卡键，修一次要两克朗。Hedwig自己拆开，把纠缠的字锤一根根掰直，装回去时多出一颗螺丝。机器居然还能用，只是每次打到字母“e”都会顿一下，Hedwig就用力按下去。Hedwig把那颗多出的螺丝放进抽屉，没扔。
  _effects: cash -2 (打字机的字母盘（其中2克朗记入债务）); skill_

  Hedwig用鞋蜡把裂缝填上，又拿粗针和麻线把鞋底缝了一圈。针脚歪，鞋底一高一低，走路时右脚落地总比左脚重。第二天上工回来，脚后跟磨出一个水泡，可鞋没再渗水。
  _effects: skill_

  _effects: reputation 0.03999999999999998; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +signed_balanced_budget; knowledge “把冬季煤房的款项并成一笔后，省下的钱够修会议室屋顶。”_

  窗外有人来问入会，接待的人一时不知道怎么开口，Hedwig替他说“进来坐”。老成员把账本拿回去，用一块旧布包好，放进抽屉最里面。抽屉关上的声音比平时响。

- **1951.05** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.hide-defects` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  篮底那两件领子卷得很紧，Hedwig用围裙角把篮沿擦了擦。Klara翻了翻面上几件，拿尺子比了比，没再往下。Gustav Huber在走廊里问交了多少，Hedwig含混说了句够买面包。
  _effects: cash 0.7 (交活所得，含未返工的两件); mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation -0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.01** `office16-corruption.inspection::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/clean-audit` tags: office, corruption, audit, legal, power, dark, vienna, urban
  Grete Moser 2带了两只空箱子来，走的时候还是空的。Hedwig把三年的合同、收据和会议记录按月份排在地上，Grete Moser 2抽查了十二份，每一份都能对上签字人；最后一句是“下次可以少准备一点”。
  _effects: reputation 0.04999999999999999; organization_funds; skill; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +passed_audit; knowledge “审计抽查了十二份合同，每一份都能对上签字人。”_

- **1952.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Hedwig走到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，看见便衣和拥挤的人群，最终没有进去。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1952.04** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Hedwig把通知念给Berta Lechner听，Berta Lechner摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Hedwig去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1952.05** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/second-bridge-promise` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  在第二个桥洞下，Mitzi Winter 2先停下来，用鞋尖碾一颗烤栗子壳，说下星期天还是这个时候、还是这座桥。Hedwig点头，掏出四赫勒买了两小袋栗子，两人分着剥，手上都是糖渍；桥洞上的水还在滴，谁也没有提万一明天下雨怎么办。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; cash -0.04 (两小袋烤栗子（其中0.04克朗记入债务）); mood 0.06000000000000005_

- **1952.10** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.cold-day` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  孩子放学回来搓着手说冷，Hedwig把仅有的半捧煤末压进炉底，火苗细得像灯芯。她没接Anna Lechner递来的煤块，只说等明天煤店开门。屋里哈气成雾，晚饭的土豆是凉的。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002485:child_

- **1953.01** `office16-favor.private-sinecure::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/favor-refused` tags: office, favor, corruption, patronage, family, power, vienna, urban
  Elisabeth Lechner把一篮鸡蛋放在Hedwig家门口，说只要一个不用打卡的位子。Hedwig把鸡蛋拎回对方手里，说编制表不是自家的抽屉。两个人站在楼梯间，谁也没有再提这件事；第二天见面，还是点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002794:child; reputation 0.03; flag +refused_private_favor; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Hedwig把报修单交上去，三天没有回音。再去问，Maria Lechner说单子夹在工资册里弄丢了，让Hedwig重写一张。那台机器还在转，螺栓一天比一天松；Hedwig每次经过都绕开半步，耳朵却一直听着它的声音。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; condition; flag +work13_safety_form_lost; knowledge “the repair request never reached the maintenance book”_

- **1953.09** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Ferdinand Berg 2在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.10** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/keep-watch-borrow-elsewhere` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你们站在当铺招牌下面，风从巷口灌进来。邻居说他婆娘这个月也要买煤，但两克朗还能匀出来。你把表给他看了一眼，说这表不能当，是爹留下的。他点点头，没再问。
  _effects: cash 2.3 (向邻居借来的现钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Klara用Hedwig留在抽屉里的印章签了一份采购单，把库房里最后一批煤卖给了城外的砖厂。Hedwig发现时，账已经做平，签字栏里是Hedwig自己的名字。Klara说只是替Hedwig省了麻烦，还把那支用旧的印泥放回了原处。
  _effects: cash -3 (为平账先垫出去的钱（其中3克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.1; relation rival with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; condition; flag +seal_used_by_trusted_hand; mood -0.07000000000000006_

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:017088_

  _effects: organization_funds; reputation 0.04000000000000001; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; knowledge “把冬季煤房的款项并成一笔后，省下的钱够修会议室屋顶。”_

  _effects: reputation -0.1; condition; flag +pamphlet_seized; knowledge “the censor has the printer’s name and the shop address”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121_

  收破烂的把两克朗数在门槛上，顺手敲了敲桌板看木料。Hedwig把铁盒扣好，那里面只有照片和几封旧信。Anna Bauer 2替Hedwig扶着门，说旧床架值不了几个钱，能脱手就不亏。

  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +curse_mercy_

  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; knowledge “门框上出现了我的名字和当天的日期。”; flag +df18_name_carved_on_door_

  Hedwig和Emil Berg谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Hedwig家，钥匙只在白天给。Emil Berg说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Rosa Berg听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Hedwig把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: reputation 0.020000000000000018; cash -2 (lost wages and paid a fine（其中2克朗记入债务）); condition; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:931; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +showed_banned_reel; knowledge “那卷新闻片里有一张被按下去的脸，散场前警察就堵住了侧门。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

  又过了一个冬天，Hedwig能从楼梯的响声判断是谁在值夜，也能从煤气灯的颜色判断哪一段管道该修。师傅把巡查路线改了三次，最后仍然照Hedwig画的那张走；两个人都没有提这件事。

  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 35 -> 39; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 52 -> 47; cash 1 (省下的配给和车钱); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1957.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Franz Moser在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Franz Moser敲开Hedwig的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1957.11** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.remake-coat/remake-coat.left-on-chair` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, mending, clothing, household
  Hedwig只把外套的里衬拆了一半，就搁在椅背上。冬天天短，她每天回来都看见那件敞着口子的外套，想着明天接着弄，明天又总是有别的事。到开春外套还搭在那儿，呢面上落了一层灰。
  _effects: flag +unfinished_coat_
